    PRINT "No .jdb files found in the current directory."
    STOP
ENDIF

PRINT "Found " + STR$(LEN(jdb_files$)) + " files to combine."

' 2. Initialize an empty string to hold all the code
combined_code$ = ""
file_separator$ = CHR$(13) + CHR$(10) + "' --- End of File ---" + CHR$(13) + CHR$(10) + CHR$(13) + CHR$(10)

' 3. Loop through the list of files
FOR i = 0 TO LEN(jdb_files$) - 1
    current_file$ = jdb_files$[i]

    if len(dir$(current_file$))[0] > 0 THEN
        PRINT "Reading: "; current_file$
    else
        PRINT "NOT FOUND: " ; current_file$
        CONTINUEFOR
    ENDIF
    
    ' Read the entire content of the current file
    file_content$ = TXTREADER$(current_file$)
    file_content$ = "Filename: " + current_file$ + vbnewline + file_content$
    
    ' Append the content to our master string, followed by a separator
    combined_code$ = combined_code$ + file_content$ + file_separator$
NEXT i

' 4. Write the combined string to a new file
output_filename$ = "all_code.txt"
PRINT "Writing combined code to: "; output_filename$
TXTWRITER output_filename$, combined_code$

PRINT "Done. All files have been combined into 'all_code.txt'."

' --- End of File ---

Filename: mandelbrot.jdb
' Mandelbrot Set Generator for jdBasic
' -------------------------------------
' This program calculates and draws the Mandelbrot fractal.
' It iterates through each pixel, mapping it to the complex plane
' and coloring it based on how quickly the value escapes to infinity.

' --- 1. Configuration and Setup ---
SCREEN_W = 800
SCREEN_H = 600
CALC_W = 400
CALC_H = 300
DX = SCREEN_W / CALC_W
DY = SCREEN_H / CALC_H

MAX_ITERATIONS = 50 ' Increase for more detail, but slower rendering

' Define the view of the complex plane to render
' Default view
X_MIN = -2.0
X_MAX = 1.0
Y_MIN = -1.0
Y_MAX = 1.0

' A more zoomed-in view to see more detail
' X_MIN = -0.75
' X_MAX = -0.74
' Y_MIN = 0.1
' Y_MAX = 0.11

' --- 2. Initialize Graphics ---
PRINT "Initializing graphics screen..."
SCREEN SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H, "Mandelbrot Set in jdBasic"
CLS 0, 0, 0 ' Clear screen to black

' --- 3. Main Calculation and Drawing Loop ---
PRINT "Calculating Mandelbrot set... This may take a moment."

' Loop through every pixel on the screen
FOR PY = 0 TO CALC_H - 1
  FOR PX = 0 TO CALC_W - 1
    ' Map the pixel coordinates (PX, PY) to a complex number (C_REAL, C_IMAG)
    C_REAL = X_MIN + (PX / CALC_W) * (X_MAX - X_MIN)
    C_IMAG = Y_MIN + (PY / CALC_H) * (Y_MAX - Y_MIN)

    ' Initialize the iteration variables for this point
    Z_REAL = 0
    Z_IMAG = 0
    ITER = 0

    ' The main Mandelbrot iteration loop
    DO WHILE (Z_REAL * Z_REAL + Z_IMAG * Z_IMAG) <= 4 AND ITER < MAX_ITERATIONS
      ' Calculate z^2 + c
      Z_REAL_NEW = (Z_REAL * Z_REAL - Z_IMAG * Z_IMAG) + C_REAL
      Z_IMAG_NEW = (2 * Z_REAL * Z_IMAG) + C_IMAG

      ' Update z for the next iteration
      Z_REAL = Z_REAL_NEW
      Z_IMAG = Z_IMAG_NEW

      ITER = ITER + 1
    LOOP

    ' --- 4. Coloring the Pixel ---
    ' If the loop finished because it reached max iterations, the point is in the set.
    IF ITER = MAX_ITERATIONS THEN
      ' Draw a black pixel for points inside the set
      RECT PX*DX, PY*DY, DX, DY, 0, 0, 0, TRUE
    ELSE
      ' The point is outside the set. Color it based on the escape time (ITER).
      ' This creates the colorful bands around the set.
      R = (ITER * 8) MOD 255
      G = (ITER * 5) MOD 255
      B = (ITER * 12) MOD 255
      RECT PX*DX, PY*DY, DX, DY, R, G, B, TRUE
    ENDIF
  NEXT PX
  PRINT "ROW: "; PY
NEXT PY

' --- 5. Display the Final Image and Wait ---
PRINT "Calculation complete. Displaying result."
SCREENFLIP ' Push the completed drawing to the screen

PRINT "Press any key to exit."
' Wait for a key press before closing the window
do
 a$ = inkey$()
 sleep 10
loop until a$ > ""

' --- End of File ---

Filename: mandelbrot_eigen.jdb
' Mandelbrot Set Generator for jdBasic (Interactive Zoom)
' --------------------------------------------------------------------
' This version delegates the main calculation to a high-performance
' C++ function from an external DLL for maximum speed.
' --------------------------------------------------------------------

' Load the DLL with the fast Mandelbrot function
DLLIMPORT "numerics.dll"

' --- 1. Global Configuration and Setup ---
SCREEN_W = 1920
SCREEN_H = 1080
MAX_ITERATIONS = 100
CALC_W = 1920 / 2
CALC_H = 1080 / 2
DX = SCREEN_W / CALC_W
DY = SCREEN_H / CALC_H
X_MIN = -2.0
X_MAX = 1.0
Y_MIN = -1.5
Y_MAX = 1.5

' --- 2. Initialize Graphics ---
PRINT "Initializing graphics screen..."
SCREEN SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H, "Interactive Mandelbrot Set in jdBasic"
CLS 0, 0, 0

' ====================================================================
' ==  SUBROUTINE TO CALCULATE AND DRAW THE FRACTAL
' ====================================================================
SUB CalculateAndDrawMandelbrot()
    ' --- 3. Vectorized Coordinate Setup  ---
    PRINT "Creating coordinate matrices for the current view..."
    PX_VEC = (IOTA(CALC_W) - 1) / (CALC_W - 1)
    PY_VEC = (IOTA(CALC_H) - 1) / (CALC_H - 1)
    C_REAL = OUTER(PY_VEC*0, PX_VEC, "+") * (X_MAX - X_MIN) + X_MIN
    C_IMAG = OUTER(PY_VEC, PX_VEC*0, "+") * (Y_MAX - Y_MIN) + Y_MIN

    ' --- 4. High-Performance Calculation  ---
    PRINT "Calculating Mandelbrot set with C++ core function..."
    ' The entire slow FOR...NEXT loop is replaced by this single, fast call.
    ITERATIONS = MANDELBROT(C_REAL, C_IMAG, MAX_ITERATIONS)

    ' --- 5. Vectorized Coloring  ---
    PRINT "Calculating final color matrix..."
    INSIDE_SET_MASK = (ITERATIONS >= MAX_ITERATIONS)
    R = ((ITERATIONS * 8) MOD 255) * (1 - INSIDE_SET_MASK)
    G = ((ITERATIONS * 5) MOD 255) * (1 - INSIDE_SET_MASK)
    B = ((ITERATIONS * 12) MOD 255) * (1 - INSIDE_SET_MASK)
    COLOR_MATRIX = R * 65536 + G * 256 + B

    ' --- 6. Draw the Final Image  ---
    PRINT "Drawing final image with PLOTRAW..."
    PLOTRAW 0, 0, COLOR_MATRIX, DX, DY
    SCREENFLIP
    PRINT "Ready. Click to zoom or press ESC to exit."
ENDSUB

' ====================================================================
' ==  MAIN INTERACTIVE LOOP 
' ====================================================================
CalculateAndDrawMandelbrot
DO
    IF MOUSEB(1) THEN
        MX = MOUSEX() : MY = MOUSEY()
        CLICK_REAL = X_MIN + (MX / SCREEN_W) * (X_MAX - X_MIN)
        CLICK_IMAG = Y_MIN + (MY / SCREEN_H) * (Y_MAX - Y_MIN)
        ZOOM_FACTOR = 2.0
        NEW_WIDTH = (X_MAX - X_MIN) / ZOOM_FACTOR
        NEW_HEIGHT = (Y_MAX - Y_MIN) / ZOOM_FACTOR
        X_MIN = CLICK_REAL - NEW_WIDTH / 2
        X_MAX = CLICK_REAL + NEW_WIDTH / 2
        Y_MIN = CLICK_IMAG - NEW_HEIGHT / 2
        Y_MAX = CLICK_IMAG + NEW_HEIGHT / 2
        CalculateAndDrawMandelbrot
    ENDIF
    KEY$ = INKEY$()
    IF KEY$ <> "" AND ASC(KEY$) = 27 THEN EXITDO
    SLEEP 20
LOOP
PRINT "Program finished."
' --- End of File ---

Filename: mandelbrot_interactive.jdb
' Mandelbrot Set Generator for jdBasic (Interactive Zoom)
' --------------------------------------------------------------------
' This program interactively calculates and draws the Mandelbrot fractal.
'
' - It uses APL-style array operations for high performance.
' - Click on the image to zoom in on that point.
' - Press the ESC key to exit the program.
' --------------------------------------------------------------------

' --- 1. Global Configuration and Setup ---
SCREEN_W = 1920
SCREEN_H = 1080
MAX_ITERATIONS = 100 ' Increase for more detail, but slower rendering
CALC_W = 384         ' Increased calculation resolution for better zoom quality
CALC_H = 216
DX = SCREEN_W / CALC_W
DY = SCREEN_H / CALC_H

' Define the initial view of the complex plane
X_MIN = -2.0
X_MAX = 1.0
Y_MIN = -1.5
Y_MAX = 1.5

' --- 2. Initialize Graphics ---
PRINT "Initializing graphics screen..."
SCREEN SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H, "Interactive Mandelbrot Set in jdBasic"
CLS 0, 0, 0 ' Clear screen to black


' ====================================================================
' ==  SUBROUTINE TO CALCULATE AND DRAW THE FRACTAL
' ====================================================================
SUB CalculateAndDrawMandelbrot()
    ' This subroutine contains the core calculation and drawing logic.
    ' It is called once at the start and every time we zoom.

    ' --- 3. Vectorized Coordinate Setup ---
    PRINT "Creating coordinate matrices for the current view..."

    ' Create 1D vectors for all X and Y coordinates on the screen
    PX_VEC = (IOTA(CALC_W) - 1) / (CALC_W - 1)
    PY_VEC = (IOTA(CALC_H) - 1) / (CALC_H - 1)

    ' Use OUTER to expand the 1D vectors into 2D coordinate matrices
    C_REAL = OUTER(PY_VEC*0, PX_VEC, "+") * (X_MAX - X_MIN) + X_MIN
    C_IMAG = OUTER(PY_VEC, PX_VEC*0, "+") * (Y_MAX - Y_MIN) + Y_MIN


    ' --- 4. Vectorized Iteration ---
    PRINT "Calculating Mandelbrot set with matrix operations..."

    ' Initialize Z and iteration-count matrices
    Z_REAL = RESHAPE([0], [CALC_H, CALC_W])
    Z_IMAG = RESHAPE([0], [CALC_H, CALC_W])
    ITERATIONS = RESHAPE([0], [CALC_H, CALC_W])

    ' This loop replaces the inner DO/WHILE. It iterates for all points at once.
    FOR I = 1 TO MAX_ITERATIONS
      ' Create a boolean mask of points that are still within the escape radius
      STILL_CALCULATING_MASK = (Z_REAL^2 + Z_IMAG^2) <= 4

      ' If all points have escaped, we can stop early
      IF NOT ANY(STILL_CALCULATING_MASK) THEN EXITFOR

      ' Increment iteration count only for points still being calculated
      ITERATIONS = ITERATIONS + STILL_CALCULATING_MASK

      ' Calculate z^2 + c for all points
      Z_REAL_NEW = (Z_REAL^2 - Z_IMAG^2) + C_REAL
      Z_IMAG_NEW = (2 * Z_REAL * Z_IMAG) + C_IMAG

      ' Update Z values only for the points still inside the mask
      Z_REAL = Z_REAL_NEW * STILL_CALCULATING_MASK + Z_REAL * (1 - STILL_CALCULATING_MASK)
      Z_IMAG = Z_IMAG_NEW * STILL_CALCULATING_MASK + Z_IMAG * (1 - STILL_CALCULATING_MASK)
    NEXT I


    ' --- 5. Vectorized Coloring ---
    PRINT "Calculating final color matrix..."

    ' Create a mask for points inside the set (those that reached max iterations)
    INSIDE_SET_MASK = (ITERATIONS >= MAX_ITERATIONS)

    ' Calculate colors for points outside the set based on escape time
    R = (ITERATIONS * 8) MOD 255
    G = (ITERATIONS * 5) MOD 255
    B = (ITERATIONS * 12) MOD 255

    ' Apply the "inside set" mask to make those pixels black
    R = R * (1 - INSIDE_SET_MASK)
    G = G * (1 - INSIDE_SET_MASK)
    B = B * (1 - INSIDE_SET_MASK)

    ' Pack the R, G, and B matrices into a single integer color matrix
    ' The formula is equivalent to (R << 16) | (G << 8) | B
    COLOR_MATRIX = R * 65536 + G * 256 + B


    ' --- 6. Draw the Final Image ---
    PRINT "Drawing final image with PLOTRAW..."

    ' Call the high-performance procedure to draw the entire matrix at once
    PLOTRAW 0, 0, COLOR_MATRIX, DX, DY

    ' Push the completed drawing to the screen
    SCREENFLIP
    PRINT "Ready. Click to zoom or press ESC to exit."
ENDSUB


' ====================================================================
' ==  MAIN INTERACTIVE LOOP
' ====================================================================

' First, draw the initial view
CalculateAndDrawMandelbrot

DO
    ' --- Check for User Input ---
    ' Check for a left mouse button click
    IF MOUSEB(1) THEN
        MX = MOUSEX()
        MY = MOUSEY()
        PRINT "Mouse clicked at: "; MX; ", "; MY

        ' --- Zoom Logic ---
        ' Convert screen coordinates (MX, MY) to complex plane coordinates
        CLICK_REAL = X_MIN + (MX / SCREEN_W) * (X_MAX - X_MIN)
        CLICK_IMAG = Y_MIN + (MY / SCREEN_H) * (Y_MAX - Y_MIN)

        ' Define how much to zoom in. 2.0 means the view will be half the size.
        ZOOM_FACTOR = 2.0

        ' Calculate the new width and height of the view in the complex plane
        NEW_WIDTH = (X_MAX - X_MIN) / ZOOM_FACTOR
        NEW_HEIGHT = (Y_MAX - Y_MIN) / ZOOM_FACTOR

        ' Recenter the view on the point that was clicked
        X_MIN = CLICK_REAL - NEW_WIDTH / 2
        X_MAX = CLICK_REAL + NEW_WIDTH / 2
        Y_MIN = CLICK_IMAG - NEW_HEIGHT / 2
        Y_MAX = CLICK_IMAG + NEW_HEIGHT / 2

        ' Now that the view coordinates are updated, recalculate and redraw
        CalculateAndDrawMandelbrot
    ENDIF

    ' Check if the user pressed a key
    KEY$ = INKEY$()
    IF KEY$ <> "" THEN
        ' Check if the pressed key was ESC (ASCII code 27)
        IF ASC(KEY$) = 27 THEN
            EXITDO ' Exit the main loop
        ENDIF
    ENDIF

    ' Pause briefly to prevent the loop from using 100% CPU
    SLEEP 20
LOOP

PRINT "Program finished."
' --- End of File ---

Filename: mandelbrot_reactive.jdb
' Mandelbrot Set Generator for jdBasic (Optimized Reactive Zoom)
' --------------------------------------------------------------------
' This version uses a single REACT MAP as a trigger to prevent
' multiple recalculations during a single zoom operation, ensuring
' high performance and responsiveness.
' --------------------------------------------------------------------

' Load the DLL with the fast Mandelbrot function
DLLIMPORT "numerics.dll"

' --- 1. Global Configuration and Setup ---
SCREEN_W = 1920
SCREEN_H = 1080
CALC_W = 1920 / 2
CALC_H = 1080 / 2
DX = SCREEN_W / CALC_W
DY = SCREEN_H / CALC_H

DREH_ENDE = TRUE
DREH = "|/-\-"

ASYNC FUNC DREHDING(DREHI)
    DREH_ENDE = FALSE
    DO
        TRY
            DREHI = DREHI + 1
            DREHY = GETY()
            LOCATE DREHY, 1
            PRINT MID$(DREH, DREHI MOD 5, 1); 
        CATCH
            PRINT "Error: ", ERRMSG$
        ENDTRY
    LOOP UNTIL DREH_ENDE
    RETURN "Ausgedreht!"
ENDFUNC

' --- Define the reactive trigger map ---
' This is the ONLY variable the main loop will modify to trigger updates.
DIM VIEW_PARAMS AS REACT MAP

' Set the initial view parameters in the map
VIEW_PARAMS = {
    "x_min": -2.0,
    "x_max": 1.0,
    "y_min": -1.5,
    "y_max": 1.5,
    "max_iter": 100
}

' --- 2. Initialize Graphics ---
PRINT "Initializing graphics screen..."
SCREEN SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H, "Interactive Mandelbrot Set in jdBasic (Optimized)"
CLS 0, 0, 0

' ====================================================================
' ==  REACTIVE CALCULATION AND DRAWING CHAIN
' ====================================================================

' --- 3. Reactive Coordinate Setup ---
' These variables now depend on the values within the VIEW_PARAMS map.
DIM C_REAL[1] AS REACT DOUBLE
DIM C_IMAG[1] AS REACT DOUBLE

' --- These intermediate variables are not strictly necessary but improve clarity ---
DIM PX_VEC, PY_VEC
PX_VEC = (IOTA(CALC_W) - 1) / (CALC_W - 1)
PY_VEC = (IOTA(CALC_H) - 1) / (CALC_H - 1)

' The reactive chain now starts from the VIEW_PARAMS map.
C_REAL -> OUTER(PY_VEC*0, PX_VEC, "+") * (VIEW_PARAMS{"x_max"} - VIEW_PARAMS{"x_min"}) + VIEW_PARAMS{"x_min"}
C_IMAG -> OUTER(PY_VEC, PX_VEC*0, "+") * (VIEW_PARAMS{"y_max"} - VIEW_PARAMS{"y_min"}) + VIEW_PARAMS{"y_min"}

' --- 4. High-Performance Calculation ---
DIM ITERATIONS[1] AS REACT DOUBLE
ITERATIONS -> MANDELBROT(C_REAL, C_IMAG, VIEW_PARAMS{"max_iter"})

' --- 5. Vectorized Coloring ---
DIM COLOR_MATRIX[1] AS REACT DOUBLE
COLOR_MATRIX -> ( ( (ITERATIONS * 8) MOD 255) * (1 - (ITERATIONS >= VIEW_PARAMS{"max_iter"})) ) * 65536 + ( ( (ITERATIONS * 5) MOD 255) * (1 - (ITERATIONS >= VIEW_PARAMS{"max_iter"})) ) * 256 + ( ( (ITERATIONS * 12) MOD 255) * (1 - (ITERATIONS >= VIEW_PARAMS{"max_iter"})) )

' ====================================================================
' ==  MAIN INTERACTIVE LOOP
' ====================================================================

FIRSTTIME = TRUE: FOREVER = FALSE

PRINT "Ready. Click to zoom or press ESC to exit."
DO
    IF MOUSEB(1) OR FIRSTTIME THEN
    
        dreh_task = DREHDING(0)

        MX = MOUSEX() : MY = MOUSEY()

        IF FIRSTTIME THEN MX = SCREEN_W / 2 : MY = SCREEN_H / 2: FIRSTTIME = FALSE

        ' Get the current view parameters from the REACT map for calculation
        current_x_min = VIEW_PARAMS{"x_min"}
        current_x_max = VIEW_PARAMS{"x_max"}
        current_y_min = VIEW_PARAMS{"y_min"}
        current_y_max = VIEW_PARAMS{"y_max"}

        ' Calculate the new center based on the mouse click
        CLICK_REAL = current_x_min + (MX / SCREEN_W) * (current_x_max - current_x_min)
        CLICK_IMAG = current_y_min + (MY / SCREEN_H) * (current_y_max - current_y_min)

        ZOOM_FACTOR = 2.0
        NEW_WIDTH = (current_x_max - current_x_min) / ZOOM_FACTOR
        NEW_HEIGHT = (current_y_max - current_y_min) / ZOOM_FACTOR

        ' Calculate the new view boundaries
        new_x_min = CLICK_REAL - NEW_WIDTH / 2
        new_x_max = CLICK_REAL + NEW_WIDTH / 2
        new_y_min = CLICK_IMAG - NEW_HEIGHT / 2
        new_y_max = CLICK_IMAG + NEW_HEIGHT / 2

        ' Update the REACT map in a single assignment. This triggers the
        ' entire reactive recalculation and redraw chain exactly ONCE.
        VIEW_PARAMS = {
            "x_min": new_x_min,
            "x_max": new_x_max,
            "y_min": new_y_min,
            "y_max": new_y_max,
            "max_iter": VIEW_PARAMS{"max_iter"} ' Keep the old iteration count
        }

        PLOTRAW 0, 0, COLOR_MATRIX, DX, DY
        SCREENFLIP
        DREH_ENDE = TRUE
        drehresult = AWAIT dreh_task
        PRINT
        PRINT "New X range: "; new_x_min; " to "; new_x_max
        PRINT "Ready. Click to zoom or press ESC to exit."
        
    ENDIF

    KEY$ = INKEY$()
    IF KEY$ <> "" AND ASC(KEY$) = 27 THEN EXITDO
    SLEEP 20
LOOP UNTIL FOREVER

PRINT "Program finished."
' --- End of File ---

Filename: mandelbrot_vectorized.jdb
' Mandelbrot Set Generator for jdBasic (Vectorized & Optimized Drawing)
' --------------------------------------------------------------------
' This program calculates and draws the Mandelbrot fractal using
' APL-style array operations and a high-performance raw plotting procedure.

' --- 1. Configuration and Setup ---
SCREEN_W = 1920
 SCREEN_H = 1080
MAX_ITERATIONS = 50 ' Increase for more detail, but slower rendering
CALC_W = 192
CALC_H = 108
DX = SCREEN_W / CALC_W
DY = SCREEN_H / CALC_H
' Define the view of the complex plane to render
X_MIN = -2.0
X_MAX = 1.0
Y_MIN = -1.0
Y_MAX = 1.0

' A more zoomed-in view to see more detail
'X_MIN = -0.75
'X_MAX = -0.74
'Y_MIN = 0.1
'Y_MAX = 0.11

' --- 2. Initialize Graphics ---
PRINT "Initializing graphics screen..."
SCREEN SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H, "Mandelbrot Set in jdBasic (Vectorized)"
CLS 0, 0, 0 ' Clear screen to black

' --- 3. Vectorized Coordinate Setup ---
PRINT "Creating coordinate matrices..."

' Create 1D vectors for all X and Y coordinates on the screen
PX_VEC = (IOTA(CALC_W) - 1) / (CALC_W - 1)
PY_VEC = (IOTA(CALC_H) - 1) / (CALC_H - 1)

' Use OUTER to expand the 1D vectors into 2D coordinate matrices
C_REAL = OUTER(PY_VEC*0, PX_VEC, "+") * (X_MAX - X_MIN) + X_MIN
C_IMAG = OUTER(PY_VEC, PX_VEC*0, "+") * (Y_MAX - Y_MIN) + Y_MIN


' --- 4. Vectorized Iteration ---
PRINT "Calculating Mandelbrot set with matrix operations..."

' Initialize Z and iteration-count matrices
Z_REAL = RESHAPE([0], [CALC_H, CALC_W])
Z_IMAG = RESHAPE([0], [CALC_H, CALC_W])
ITERATIONS = RESHAPE([0], [CALC_H, CALC_W])

' This loop replaces the inner DO/WHILE. It iterates for all points at once.
FOR I = 1 TO MAX_ITERATIONS
  ' Create a boolean mask of points that are still within the escape radius
  STILL_CALCULATING_MASK = (Z_REAL^2 + Z_IMAG^2) <= 4

  ' If all points have escaped, we can stop early
  IF NOT ANY(STILL_CALCULATING_MASK) THEN EXITFOR

  ' Increment iteration count only for points still being calculated
  ITERATIONS = ITERATIONS + STILL_CALCULATING_MASK

  ' Calculate z^2 + c for all points
  Z_REAL_NEW = (Z_REAL^2 - Z_IMAG^2) + C_REAL
  Z_IMAG_NEW = (2 * Z_REAL * Z_IMAG) + C_IMAG

  ' Update Z values only for the points still inside the mask
  Z_REAL = Z_REAL_NEW * STILL_CALCULATING_MASK + Z_REAL * (1 - STILL_CALCULATING_MASK)
  Z_IMAG = Z_IMAG_NEW * STILL_CALCULATING_MASK + Z_IMAG * (1 - STILL_CALCULATING_MASK)
  PRINT "Calculating iteration: "; I
NEXT I


' --- 5. Vectorized Coloring ---
PRINT "Calculating final color matrix..."

' Create a mask for points inside the set (those that reached max iterations)
INSIDE_SET_MASK = (ITERATIONS = MAX_ITERATIONS)

' Calculate colors for points outside the set based on escape time
R = (ITERATIONS * 8) MOD 255
G = (ITERATIONS * 5) MOD 255
B = (ITERATIONS * 12) MOD 255

' Apply the "inside set" mask to make those pixels black
R = R * (1 - INSIDE_SET_MASK)
G = G * (1 - INSIDE_SET_MASK)
B = B * (1 - INSIDE_SET_MASK)

' Pack the R, G, and B matrices into a single integer color matrix
' The formula is equivalent to (R << 16) | (G << 8) | B
COLOR_MATRIX = R * 65536 + G * 256 + B


' --- 6. Draw the Final Image ---
PRINT "Drawing final image with PLOTRAW..."

' Call the new high-performance procedure to draw the entire matrix at once
PLOTRAW 0, 0, COLOR_MATRIX, DX, DY

' Push the completed drawing to the screen
SCREENFLIP

PRINT "Calculation complete. Press any key to exit."
do
 a$ = inkey$()
 sleep 10
loop until a$ > ""


' --- End of File ---

Filename: map2_test.jdb
CLS
PRINT "--- jdBasic Complete Map Feature Demo ---"
PRINT

' =================================================================
PRINT "--- 1. Initialization and Basic Assignments ---"
' =================================================================
' Create a map to hold a player's inventory
DIM inventory AS MAP

inventory{"gold"} = 1250
inventory{"potions"} = 15
inventory{"sword"} = "Iron Longsword"
inventory{"level"} = 25

PRINT "Initial Inventory:"
PRINT inventory
PRINT

' =================================================================
PRINT "--- 2. Querying the Map ---"
' =================================================================

' --- MAP.EXISTS ---
PRINT "Does the 'sword' key exist? "; MAP.EXISTS(inventory, "sword") ' TRUE
PRINT "Does the 'armor' key exist? "; MAP.EXISTS(inventory, "armor") ' FALSE
PRINT

' --- MAP.SIZE ---
PRINT "Number of items in inventory: "; MAP.SIZE(inventory) ' 4
PRINT

' --- MAP.KEYS ---
key_list = MAP.KEYS(inventory)
PRINT "Inventory Keys: "; key_list
PRINT

' --- MAP.VALUES ---
value_list = MAP.VALUES(inventory)
PRINT "Inventory Values: "; value_list
PRINT

' =================================================================
PRINT "--- 3. Modifying the Map ---"
' =================================================================

' --- MAP.DELETE ---
PRINT "Player sold their sword..."
MAP.DELETE inventory, "sword"
PRINT "Inventory after deleting 'sword': "; inventory
PRINT "New size: "; MAP.SIZE(inventory)
PRINT

' --- MAP.MERGE ---
' Create another map with new items to add
DIM loot AS MAP
loot{"gold"} = 250 ' This will overwrite the existing gold value
loot{"armor"} = "Leather Tunic"
loot{"gems"} = 5

PRINT "Found loot: "; loot
PRINT "Merging loot into inventory..."
MAP.MERGE inventory, loot
PRINT "Inventory after merge: "; inventory
PRINT "New size: "; MAP.SIZE(inventory)
PRINT

' =================================================================
PRINT "--- 4. Iterating with MAP.ITEMS ---"
' =================================================================

' --- MAP.ITEMS ---
' This is the best way to loop through all key-value pairs
PRINT "--- Full Inventory Report ---"
inventory_items = MAP.ITEMS(inventory)
num_items = LEN(inventory_items)[0] ' Get number of rows

FOR i = 0 TO num_items - 1
    item_key$ = inventory_items[i, 0]
    item_value = inventory_items[i, 1]
    PRINT FORMAT$("{:<10} | {}", item_key$, item_value)
NEXT i
PRINT

' =================================================================
PRINT "--- 5. Clearing the Map ---"
' =================================================================

' --- MAP.CLEAR ---
PRINT "Dropping all items..."
MAP.CLEAR inventory
PRINT "Is the inventory empty now? "; MAP.SIZE(inventory) = 0 ' TRUE
PRINT "Final inventory state: "; inventory
PRINT

PRINT "--- Demo Complete ---"

' --- End of File ---

Filename: master.jdb
' ==============================================================================
'  JD-BASIC SEQUENCER STUDIO
' ==============================================================================
'  A full GUI environment for live coding music.
' ==============================================================================

' --- FILE I/O ---
EXPORT SUB LOADCONFIG(filename$)
    TRY
        CONFIG = JSON.PARSE$(TXTREADER$(filename$))
        PRINT "Configuration loaded from " + filename$
    CATCH
        PRINT "Error loading file: " + ERRMSG$
    ENDTRY
ENDSUB

FUNC FIND_ITEM(clist, key$)
    key$ = UCASE$(key$)
    ' Is it a numeric ID?
    IF REGEX.MATCH("^\d+$", key$) THEN
        id = VAL(key$)
        FOR EACH item IN clist
            IF item{"id"} = id THEN RETURN item
        NEXT
    ELSE
        FOR EACH item IN clist
            IF UCASE$(item{"name"}) = key$ THEN RETURN item
        NEXT        
    ENDIF
    RETURN {}
ENDFUNC

DIM CONFIG AS MAP

SCREEN 1920, 1080, "Mixme", 1.2
SOUND.INIT()
SOUND.BPM(120)
SOUND.RESET 
LOADCONFIG "sq_data.json"

' ==============================================================================
'  DATA DEFINITIONS & PRESETS
' ==============================================================================

' --- 1. Instrument Presets ---
DIM InstPresets AS MAP 
InstPresets = CONFIG{"instruments"}
InstNames = []
FOR EACH inst in InstPresets
    InstNames = APPEND(InstNames, inst{"name"})
NEXT

' --- 2. Pattern Presets ---
PatPresets = CONFIG{"sequences"}
PatNames = []
FOR EACH inst in PatPresets
    PatNames = APPEND(PatNames, inst{"name"})
NEXT

' --- 3. Track State Initialization ---
TYPE TrackState
    Name AS STRING
    Mute AS INTEGER
    Gain AS DOUBLE
    Pan AS DOUBLE
    Pattern AS STRING
    
    ' Voice Settings
    Wave AS STRING
    Att AS DOUBLE
    Dec AS DOUBLE
    Sus AS DOUBLE
    Rel AS DOUBLE
    Cutoff AS DOUBLE
    
    ' FX Settings
    LFOSpeed As DOUBLE
    LFODepth As DOUBLE
    FMAmt AS DOUBLE
    FMRat AS DOUBLE
    BitDepth AS DOUBLE
    BitRate AS DOUBLE
    RingFreq AS DOUBLE
    RingMix AS DOUBLE
    EQLow AS DOUBLE
    EQMid AS DOUBLE
    EQHigh AS DOUBLE
    UnisonVoices AS DOUBLE
    UnisonDetune AS DOUBLE
    UnisonSpread AS DOUBLE

    ' UI State
    SelectedInst AS INTEGER
    SelectedPat AS INTEGER
ENDTYPE

DIM Tracks[8] AS TrackState

FOR i = 0 TO 7
    Tracks[i].Name = "Track " + STR$(i)
    Tracks[i].Mute = FALSE
    Tracks[i].Gain = 1.0
    Tracks[i].Pan = 0.5
    Tracks[i].Pattern = "~"
    
    ' Default Synth Settings
    Tracks[i].Wave = "SINE"
    Tracks[i].Att = 0.01
    Tracks[i].Dec = 0.1
    Tracks[i].Sus = 0.8
    Tracks[i].Rel = 0.1
    Tracks[i].Cutoff = 20000
    
    ' FX Defaults
    Tracks[i].FMRat = 1.0
    Tracks[i].BitRate = 1.0
NEXT

' --- 4. Global State ---
DIM MasterBPM = 120
DIM DelayActive = FALSE
DIM DelayTime = 300
DIM DelayFeed = 0.4
DIM DelayMix = 0.3
DIM GlobalDist = 0.0

' ==============================================================================
'  HELPER SUBROUTINES
' ==============================================================================

SUB ApplyTrack(id)
    ' 1. Voice
    SOUND.VOICE id, Tracks[id].Wave, Tracks[id].Att, Tracks[id].Dec, Tracks[id].Sus, Tracks[id].Rel
    
    ' 2. Basic FX
    SOUND.FILTER id, Tracks[id].Cutoff
    SOUND.PAN id, Tracks[id].Pan
    
    ' 3. Mute Logic (Gain 0 if muted)
    finalGain = Tracks[id].Gain
    IF Tracks[id].Mute THEN finalGain = 0.0
    SOUND.GAIN id, finalGain
    
    ' 4. Advanced FX
    SOUND.FM id, Tracks[id].FMAmt, Tracks[id].FMRat
    SOUND.BITCRUSH id, Tracks[id].BitDepth, Tracks[id].BitRate
    SOUND.RINGMOD id, Tracks[id].RingFreq, Tracks[id].RingMix
    
    ' 5. Sequencer
    ' Use "VOICE" so it respects the settings above
    SOUND.SEQ id, Tracks[id].Pattern, "VOICE"
ENDSUB

SUB LoadInstrument(trackId, instName$)
    data = FIND_ITEM(InstPresets, instName$)
    Tracks[trackId].Wave = data{"waveform"}
    Tracks[trackId].Att  = data{"adsr"}[0]
    Tracks[trackId].Dec  = data{"adsr"}[1]
    Tracks[trackId].Sus  = data{"adsr"}[2]
    Tracks[trackId].Rel  = data{"adsr"}[3]
    Tracks[trackId].Cutoff = data{"filter"}
    
    ' Reset FX
    Tracks[trackId].FMAmt = 0
    Tracks[trackId].BitDepth = 0
    
    ' Load optional FX if present
    IF MAP.EXISTS(data, "FM_Amt") THEN 
        Tracks[trackId].FMAmt = data{"fm"}[0]
        Tracks[trackId].FMRat = data{"fm"}[1]
    ENDIF
    IF MAP.EXISTS(data, "Bit") THEN 
        Tracks[trackId].BitDepth = data{"bitcrush"}[0]
        Tracks[trackId].BitRate = data{"bitcrush"}[1]
    ENDIF
    
    ApplyTrack(trackId)
ENDSUB

' ==============================================================================
'  MAIN LOOP
' ==============================================================================

DO
    CLS
   
    ' --- Start Main Window ---
    GUI.BEGIN "Sequencer Studio", 0, 0, 1920, 1080
    
    ' --- Top Bar: Transport & Master FX ---
    GUI.TEXT "MASTER CONTROL"
    GUI.SAME_LINE
    IF GUI.BUTTON("STOP / RESET") THEN
        SOUND.RESET
        SOUND.BPM MasterBPM ' Re-apply BPM after reset
    ENDIF
    
    GUI.SEPARATOR
    
    ' Global Controls
    GUI.BEGIN_TABLE "MasterTable", 5
        GUI.TABLE_NEXT_ROW
        
        GUI.TABLE_NEXT_COLUMN
        OldBPM = MasterBPM
        MasterBPM = GUI.SLIDER("BPM", MasterBPM, 60, 240)
        IF OldBPM <> MasterBPM THEN SOUND.BPM MasterBPM
        
        GUI.TABLE_NEXT_COLUMN
        OldDist = GlobalDist
        GlobalDist = GUI.SLIDER("Distortion", GlobalDist, 0.0, 5.0)
        IF OldDist <> GlobalDist THEN SOUND.DISTORTION GlobalDist
        
        GUI.TABLE_NEXT_COLUMN
        OldDActive = DelayActive
        DelayActive = GUI.CHECKBOX("Delay On", DelayActive)
        
        GUI.TABLE_NEXT_COLUMN
        DelayTime = GUI.SLIDER("D.Time", DelayTime, 10, 1000)
        
        GUI.TABLE_NEXT_COLUMN
        DelayMix = GUI.SLIDER("D.Mix", DelayMix, 0.0, 1.0)
        
        ' Update Delay if any param changed
        IF OldDActive <> DelayActive OR TRUE THEN ' Optimization skipped for brevity
            SOUND.DELAY DelayActive, DelayTime, 0.5, DelayMix
        ENDIF
    GUI.END_TABLE
    
    GUI.SEPARATOR
    'GUI.SPACING
    
    ' --- Waveform Visualizer ---
    WaveData = SOUND.GET_WAVE()
    GUI.PLOT_LINES("", WaveData, "Master Output", -1.0, 1.0)
    
    GUI.SEPARATOR
    'GUI.SPACING
    
    ' --- The Tracks Table ---
    ' Columns: Controls, Instrument, Pattern, Voice Config, Effects
    IF GUI.BEGIN_TABLE("TracksTable", 5, GUI.FLAG("BORDERS"), 0, 0) THEN
        
        ' Setup Columns
        GUI.TABLE_SETUP_COLUMN "Track / Mix", 0, 100
        GUI.TABLE_SETUP_COLUMN "Presets", 0, 150
        GUI.TABLE_SETUP_COLUMN "Pattern Editor", 0, 200
        GUI.TABLE_SETUP_COLUMN "Voice Design (ADSR/Filter)", 0, 250
        GUI.TABLE_SETUP_COLUMN "FX Rack", 0, 200
        GUI.TABLE_HEADERS_ROW
        
        FOR i = 0 TO 7
            GUI.PUSH_ID i ' Unique ID for each row
            GUI.TABLE_NEXT_ROW
            
            ' --- COL 1: Mix ---
            GUI.TABLE_NEXT_COLUMN
            GUI.TEXT "Track " + i
            
            ' Mute Button (Toggle)
            oldMute = Tracks[i].Mute
            Tracks[i].Mute = GUI.CHECKBOX("Mute", Tracks[i].Mute)
            
            ' Gain Slider
            GUI.SET_NEXT_ITEM_WIDTH 80
            oldGain = Tracks[i].Gain
            Tracks[i].Gain = GUI.SLIDER("Vol", Tracks[i].Gain, 0.0, 2.0)
            
            ' Pan Slider
            GUI.SET_NEXT_ITEM_WIDTH 80
            oldPan = Tracks[i].Pan
            Tracks[i].Pan = GUI.SLIDER("Pan", Tracks[i].Pan, 0.0, 1.0)
            
            ' Apply updates if mix changed
            IF oldMute <> Tracks[i].Mute OR oldGain <> Tracks[i].Gain OR oldPan <> Tracks[i].Pan THEN
                ApplyTrack(i)
            ENDIF
            
            ' --- COL 2: Presets ---
            GUI.TABLE_NEXT_COLUMN
            GUI.SET_NEXT_ITEM_WIDTH 130
            
            ' Instrument Dropdown
            prevInst = Tracks[i].SelectedInst
            Tracks[i].SelectedInst = GUI.COMBO("Sound", Tracks[i].SelectedInst, InstNames)
            IF prevInst <> Tracks[i].SelectedInst THEN
                LoadInstrument(i, InstNames[Tracks[i].SelectedInst])
            ENDIF
            
            'GUI.SPACING
            
            ' Pattern Dropdown
            prevPat = Tracks[i].SelectedPat
            Tracks[i].SelectedPat = GUI.COMBO("Rhythm", Tracks[i].SelectedPat, PatNames)
            IF prevPat <> Tracks[i].SelectedPat THEN
                Tracks[i].Pattern = PatPresets{PatNames[Tracks[i].SelectedPat]}
                ApplyTrack(i)
            ENDIF
            
            ' --- COL 3: Pattern Editor ---
            GUI.TABLE_NEXT_COLUMN
            GUI.SET_NEXT_ITEM_WIDTH 180
            oldPat$ = Tracks[i].Pattern
            Tracks[i].Pattern = GUI.INPUT("##Pat", Tracks[i].Pattern)
            ' Apply if user pressed Enter or clicked away
            IF GUI.ITEM_DEACTIVATED_AFTER_EDIT() THEN
                ApplyTrack(i)
            ENDIF
            GUI.TEXT "Current: " + Tracks[i].Pattern
            
            ' --- COL 4: Voice Design ---
            GUI.TABLE_NEXT_COLUMN
            needUpdate = FALSE
            
            ' Waveform Selector (Manual)
            waves = ["SINE", "SQUARE", "SAW", "TRIANGLE", "NOISE"]
            ' Simple cycle button for wave
            IF GUI.BUTTON(Tracks[i].Wave) THEN
                ' Cycle logic here would be complex without index lookup, keeping simple
            ENDIF
            GUI.SAME_LINE
            GUI.TEXT "Wave"
            
            GUI.SET_NEXT_ITEM_WIDTH 60
            oldCut = Tracks[i].Cutoff
            Tracks[i].Cutoff = GUI.SLIDER("Cutoff", Tracks[i].Cutoff, 100, 20000)
            IF oldCut <> Tracks[i].Cutoff THEN needUpdate = TRUE
            
            ' ADSR sliders (compact)
            GUI.SET_NEXT_ITEM_WIDTH 50
            oldA = Tracks[i].Att
            Tracks[i].Att = GUI.SLIDER("A", Tracks[i].Att, 0.001, 1.0)
            GUI.SAME_LINE
            oldD = Tracks[i].Dec
            Tracks[i].Dec = GUI.SLIDER("D", Tracks[i].Dec, 0.001, 1.0)
            
            GUI.SET_NEXT_ITEM_WIDTH 50
            oldS = Tracks[i].Sus
            Tracks[i].Sus = GUI.SLIDER("S", Tracks[i].Sus, 0.0, 1.0)
            GUI.SAME_LINE
            oldR = Tracks[i].Rel
            Tracks[i].Rel = GUI.SLIDER("R", Tracks[i].Rel, 0.001, 2.0)
            
            IF oldA <> Tracks[i].Att OR oldD <> Tracks[i].Dec OR oldS <> Tracks[i].Sus OR oldR <> Tracks[i].Rel THEN
                needUpdate = TRUE
            ENDIF
            
            ' --- COL 5: FX Rack ---
            GUI.TABLE_NEXT_COLUMN
            
            ' FM
            GUI.TEXT "FM:"
            GUI.SAME_LINE
            GUI.SET_NEXT_ITEM_WIDTH 60
            oldFM = Tracks[i].FMAmt
            Tracks[i].FMAmt = GUI.SLIDER("##fm", Tracks[i].FMAmt, 0.0, 5.0)
            
            ' Bitcrush
            GUI.TEXT "Bit:"
            GUI.SAME_LINE
            GUI.SET_NEXT_ITEM_WIDTH 60
            oldBit = Tracks[i].BitDepth
            Tracks[i].BitDepth = GUI.SLIDER("##bit", Tracks[i].BitDepth, 0.0, 16.0)
            
            ' Ring Mod
            GUI.TEXT "Ring:"
            GUI.SAME_LINE
            GUI.SET_NEXT_ITEM_WIDTH 60
            oldRing = Tracks[i].RingMix
            Tracks[i].RingMix = GUI.SLIDER("##rng", Tracks[i].RingMix, 0.0, 1.0)
            
            IF oldFM <> Tracks[i].FMAmt OR oldBit <> Tracks[i].BitDepth OR oldRing <> Tracks[i].RingMix THEN
                needUpdate = TRUE
            ENDIF
            
            ' Apply updates if any slider moved
            IF needUpdate THEN ApplyTrack(i)
            
            GUI.POP_ID
        NEXT
        
        GUI.END_TABLE
    ENDIF
    
    GUI.END ' End Main Window
    SCREENFLIP
    
    SLEEP 10 ' CPU Saver
LOOP

Filename: MATH.jdb
export MODULE MATH

' This function will be public because of the 'export' keyword.
' Other programs that import this module can use it.
export func add(a, b)
    return a + b
endfunc

' This function is private to this module because it does NOT have 'export'.
' It can be called by other functions inside this file, but not from outside.
func secret_multiplier()
    return 2
endfunc

' This exported function demonstrates calling a private function.
export func add_and_double(a, b)
    ' First, add the two numbers using the public 'add' function
    result = add(a, b)
    
    ' Then, multiply by a secret internal value
    result = result * secret_multiplier()
    
    return result
endfunc

' This is an exported procedure.
export sub print_sum(a, b)
    print a; " + "; b; " = "; add(a,b)
endsub

' --- End of File ---

Filename: matlab.jdb
' ==========================================================
' ==  The jdBasic Math & Statistics Lab
' ==  Demonstrates: IMPORT, array math, user functions
' ==========================================================
'
' Import our library of statistical functions
IMPORT MLAB

' Initialize a sample dataset
DATASET = INT(RND(IOTA(100)) * 100 + 1)
DATASET = MLAB.SORT(DATASET) ' Use the imported SORT function

DO
  ' --- Main Menu ---
  CLS
  PRINT "--- jdBasic Math & Statistics Lab ---"
  PRINT
  PRINT "Current Dataset: "; DATASET
  PRINT
  PRINT "--- Analysis ---"
  PRINT "1. Show Descriptive Statistics"
  PRINT "2. Plot a Histogram"
  PRINT "9. Exit"
  PRINT
  INPUT "Choose an option: "; choice

  ' --- Handle User Choice ---
  IF choice = 1 THEN
    ' Calculate and display all statistics
    PRINT
    PRINT "--- Descriptive Statistics ---"
    PRINT "Count:          "; LEN(DATASET)[0]
    PRINT "Mean (Average): "; MLAB.MEAN(DATASET)      ' From funclib
    PRINT "Median:         "; MLAB.MEDIAN(DATASET)     ' From funclib
    PRINT "Std. Deviation: "; MLAB.STDDEV(DATASET)     ' From funclib
    PRINT "Minimum:        "; MIN(DATASET)        ' Built-in
    PRINT "Maximum:        "; MAX(DATASET)        ' Built-in
    PRINT "Sum:            "; SUM(DATASET)        ' Built-in
    PRINT
    INPUT "Press Enter to continue..."; pause$

  ELSEIF choice = 2 THEN
    ' Generate and plot a simple histogram
    PRINT
    PRINT "--- Histogram ---"
    num_bins = 5
    min_val = MIN(DATASET)
    max_val = MAX(DATASET)
    bin_width = (max_val - min_val) / num_bins

    bins = iota(num_bins)*0

    FOR i = 0 to LEN(DATASET)[0] - 1
      val = DATASET[i]
      bin_index = INT((val - min_val) / bin_width)
      IF bin_index >= num_bins THEN bin_index = num_bins - 1
      bins[bin_index] = bins[bin_index] + 1
    NEXT i

    FOR i = 0 to num_bins - 1
        bin_start = min_val + (i * bin_width)
        bin_end = bin_start + bin_width
        label$ = STR$(INT(bin_start)) + "-" + STR$(INT(bin_end))
        bar$ = "█" * bins[i]
        PRINT MLAB.PAD_LEFT$(label$, 10, " "); " | "; bar$
    NEXT i
    PRINT
    INPUT "Press Enter to continue..."; pause$

  ELSEIF choice = 9 THEN
    EXITDO ' Exit the main loop
  ENDIF
LOOP

PRINT "Exiting Math Lab. Goodbye!"
' --- End of File ---

Filename: matrix_v2.jdb
' ==========================================================
' == MATRIX.JDB - Digital Rain
' ==========================================================

' --- Config ---
COLS = 80
ROWS = 25

' --- Arrays ---
' Y-positions of the "drops"
DIM Drops[COLS] 
' Length of each trail
DIM Lengths[COLS]
' Speed of each drop (counter)
DIM Speeds[COLS]

' --- Init ---
CLS
CURSOR FALSE

' Initialize drops randomly off-screen
FOR i = 0 TO COLS - 1
    Drops[i] = -1 * INT(RND(1) * 20)
    Lengths[i] = INT(RND(1) * 15) + 5
    Speeds[i] = 0
NEXT i

' --- Main Loop ---
DO
    IF INKEY$() <> "" THEN EXITDO
    
    FOR c = 0 TO COLS - 1
        ' Only update some columns per frame for varied speed effect
        ' Or use a counter. Here we update every frame but use logic.
        
        y = Drops[c]
        
        ' 1. Draw the "Head" (White/Bright)
        IF y >= 0 AND y < ROWS THEN
            LOCATE y + 1, c + 1
            COLOR 15, 0 ' Bright White
            ' Print random char (Kata-kana style if font supports, otherwise obscure ASCII)
            PRINT CHR$(INT(RND(1) * 50) + 33);
        ENDIF
        
        ' 2. Draw the "Tail" (Green)
        PrevY = y - 1
        IF PrevY >= 0 AND PrevY < ROWS THEN
            LOCATE PrevY + 1, c + 1
            COLOR 10, 0 ' Bright Green
            PRINT CHR$(INT(RND(1) * 50) + 33);
        ENDIF

        ' 3. Erase the end of the trail
        EraseY = y - Lengths[c]
        IF EraseY >= 0 AND EraseY < ROWS THEN
            LOCATE EraseY + 1, c + 1
            PRINT " ";
        ENDIF
        
        ' 4. Move Drop
        Drops[c] = Drops[c] + 1
        
        ' 5. Reset if off screen
        IF (y - Lengths[c]) > ROWS THEN
            Drops[c] = 0
            Lengths[c] = INT(RND(1) * 15) + 5
            ' Randomize start time slightly to break patterns
            IF RND(1) > 0.8 THEN Drops[c] = -1 * INT(RND(1) * 10)
        ENDIF
    NEXT c
    
    SLEEP 30 ' Control global speed
LOOP

CURSOR TRUE
COLOR 7, 0
CLS
' --- End of File ---

Filename: md.jdb
' ==========================================================
' == jdBasic Console Markdown Renderer
' == Renders a subset of Markdown to the console using color.
' ==========================================================

' --- COLOR CONSTANTS ---
COLOR_DEFAULT = 7  ' Light Gray
COLOR_HEADING = 14 ' Yellow
COLOR_BOLD = 15    ' Bright White
COLOR_ITALIC = 11  ' Light Cyan
COLOR_CODE = 13    ' Light Magenta
COLOR_BLOCKQUOTE = 10 ' Light Green
COLOR_LIST = 7     ' Light Gray
COLOR_HR = 8       ' Dark Gray

' ==========================================================
' == HELPER SUB: PROCESSINLINES(line$)
' == This sub handles inline formatting like bold and italic.
' == It prints the line piece-by-piece, changing colors
' == as it encounters formatting markers.
' ==========================================================
SUB ProcessInlines(line$)
    ' This is a simplified parser. A full implementation would be a state machine.
    ' This version handles basic cases well.

    ' For simplicity, we'll use string replacement and then print.
    ' A more advanced version would parse and print segments.
    ' NOTE: jdBasic's REGEX.REPLACE is powerful for this.
    ' We will replace markdown with placeholder characters and then color them.
    
    ' The order is important: process bold/italic first.
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("\*\*\*([^*]+)\*\*\*", line$, CHR$(1) + "$1" + CHR$(1)) ' Bold+Italic
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("\*\*([^*]+)\*\*", line$, CHR$(2) + "$1" + CHR$(2)) ' Bold
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("\*([^*]+)\*", line$, CHR$(3) + "$1" + CHR$(3))     ' Italic
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("`([^`]+)`", line$, CHR$(4) + "$1" + CHR$(4))       ' Inline Code

    ' Now, print character by character, changing color based on our placeholders.
    current_color = COLOR_DEFAULT
    COLOR current_color, 0
    
    FOR i = 0 TO LEN(line$) -1
        char$ = MID$(line$, i, 1)
        
        SWITCH ASC(char$)
            CASE 1: ' Bold+Italic
                current_color = IIF(current_color = COLOR_BOLD, COLOR_DEFAULT, COLOR_BOLD)
                COLOR current_color, 0
            CASE 2: ' Bold
                current_color = IIF(current_color = COLOR_BOLD, COLOR_DEFAULT, COLOR_BOLD)
                COLOR current_color, 0
            CASE 3: ' Italic
                current_color = IIF(current_color = COLOR_ITALIC, COLOR_DEFAULT, COLOR_ITALIC)
                COLOR current_color, 0
            CASE 4: ' Code
                current_color = IIF(current_color = COLOR_CODE, COLOR_DEFAULT, COLOR_CODE)
                COLOR current_color, 0
            DEFAULT
                PRINT char$;
        ENDSWITCH
    NEXT i
    
    COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
    PRINT ' Print a newline at the end of the line.
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == MAIN SUB: RENDERMARKDOWN(markdown_text$)
' == Takes a block of Markdown text and prints it formatted.
' ==========================================================
SUB RenderMarkdown(markdown_text$)
    lines = SPLIT(markdown_text$, CHR$(10))
    in_code_block = FALSE

    FOR EACH line IN lines
        trimmed_line$ = TRIM$(line)
        ' --- 1. Handle Code Blocks (Stateful) ---
        IF 3 / trimmed_line$ = "```" THEN
            in_code_block = NOT in_code_block
            GOTO outi
        ENDIF

        IF in_code_block THEN
            COLOR COLOR_CODE, 0
            PRINT "    "; line
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            GOTO outi
        ENDIF

        ' --- 2. Handle Block-Level Elements ---

        ' Headings (#, ##, ...)
        IF 1 / trimmed_line$ = "#" THEN
            level = 0
            FOR i = 0 TO LEN(trimmed_line$) - 1
                IF MID$(trimmed_line$, i, 1) = "#" THEN
                    level = level + 1
                ELSE
                    EXITFOR
                ENDIF
            NEXT i
            
            heading_text$ = TRIM$(MID$(trimmed_line$, level))
            COLOR COLOR_HEADING, 0
            PRINT
            ProcessInlines heading_text$ 
            COLOR COLOR_HR, 0
            PRINT "─" * LEN(heading_text$)
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            PRINT
        
        ' Horizontal Rule (---, ***)
        ELSEIF REGEX.MATCH("^-{3,}$", trimmed_line$) OR REGEX.MATCH("^\*{3,}$", trimmed_line$) THEN
            COLOR COLOR_HR, 0
            PRINT "─" * 80
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
        
        ' Blockquote (>)
        ELSEIF REGEX.MATCH("^>", trimmed_line$) THEN
            quote_text$ = TRIM$(MID$(trimmed_line$, 2))
            COLOR COLOR_BLOCKQUOTE, 0
            PRINT " │  ";
            ProcessInlines(quote_text$)
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            
        ' Unordered List (*, -, +)
        ELSEIF REGEX.MATCH("^\s*[*+-]\s+", trimmed_line$) THEN
            list_text$ = REGEX.REPLACE("^\s*[*+-]\s+", trimmed_line$, "")
            COLOR COLOR_LIST, 0
            PRINT "  • ";
            ProcessInlines(list_text$)
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
        
        ' Empty Line
        ELSEIF trimmed_line$ = "" THEN
            PRINT

        ' Paragraph
        ELSE
            ProcessInlines(line)
        ENDIF
outi:        
    
    NEXT
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' ==                 EXAMPLE USAGE
' ==========================================================

' --- Call the main subroutine to render the document ---
CLS

RenderMarkdown(txtreader$("test.md"))

COLOR 5,0

' --- End of File ---

Filename: md2.jdb
' ==========================================================
' == jdBasic Console Markdown Renderer
' == Renders a subset of Markdown to the console using ANSI escape codes.
' ==========================================================

' --- ANSI STYLE ENUM ---
' Defines constants for ANSI escape codes for text styling.
ENUM Style
    Default = 0
    Bold = 1
    Italic = 3
    NoItalic = 23
    NoBold = 22
    Underline = 4
    NoUnderline = 24
    Negative = 7
    Positive  = 27
ENDENUM

' --- ANSI HELPER FUNCTION ---
' Wraps a style code in the required ANSI escape sequence.
FUNC cesc_m(EscapeCode)
    IF TYPEOF(EscapeCode) <> "STRING" THEN
        EscapeCode = STR$(EscapeCode)
    ENDIF
    RETURN CHR$(27) + "[" + EscapeCode + "m"
ENDFUNC


' --- COLOR CONSTANTS (for block elements) ---
COLOR_DEFAULT = 7      ' Light Gray
COLOR_HEADING = 14     ' Yellow
COLOR_CODE = 13        ' Light Magenta (Used for non-highlighted code blocks)
COLOR_BLOCKQUOTE = 10  ' Light Green
COLOR_LIST = 7         ' Light Gray
COLOR_HR = 8           ' Dark Gray

' --- SYNTAX HIGHLIGHTING COLORS ---
COLOR_JDB_DEFAULT = 13 ' Light Magenta
COLOR_JDB_KEYWORD = 11 ' Light Cyan
COLOR_JDB_STRING = 10  ' Light Green
COLOR_JDB_COMMENT = 8  ' Dark Gray
COLOR_JDB_NUMBER = 9   ' Light Blue

' --- GLOBAL FOR KEYWORDS ---
DIM jdBasicKeywords AS MAP

' ==========================================================
' == HELPER SUB: INITKEYWORDMAP()
' == Populates a map with all jdBasic keywords for fast lookup.
' ==========================================================
SUB InitKeywordMap()
    ' NOTE: This is a subset of keywords from your KeywordRepository.hpp for brevity.
    ' For a complete implementation, all keywords should be added here.
    keywordList = SPLIT("IF,THEN,ELSE,ENDIF,FOR,TO,STEP,NEXT,EACH,IN,DO,LOOP,WHILE,SUB,ENDSUB,FUNC,ENDFUNC,RETURN,PRINT,INPUT,DIM,AS,STRING,INTEGER,DOUBLE,MAP,ARRAY,TRUE,FALSE,SWITCH,CASE,DEFAULT,ENDSWITCH", ",")
    FOR EACH kw IN keywordList
        jdBasicKeywords{TRIM$(kw)} = TRUE
    NEXT
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == HELPER SUB: HIGHLIGHTJDBASIC(line$)
' == A simple tokenizer to apply syntax highlighting to a line of jdBasic code.
' ==========================================================
SUB HighlightJdBasic(line$)
    PRINT "    "; ' Indent the code block line
    
    buffer$ = ""
    in_string = FALSE
    
    FOR i = 0 TO LEN(line$) - 1
        char$ = MID$(line$, i, 1)

        ' 1. Check for start of a comment (if not in a string)
        IF char$ = "'" AND NOT in_string THEN
            PrintToken(buffer$) ' Print any preceding token
            buffer$ = ""
            COLOR COLOR_JDB_COMMENT, 0
            PRINT MID$(line$, i); ' Print the rest of the line as a comment
            GOTO end_of_line
        ENDIF

        ' 2. Handle strings
        IF char$ = CHR$(34) THEN
            PrintToken(buffer$) ' Print any preceding token
            buffer$ = ""
            in_string = NOT in_string
            COLOR COLOR_JDB_STRING, 0
            PRINT CHR$(34);
            CONTINUE
        ENDIF

        IF in_string THEN
            PRINT char$;
            CONTINUE
        ENDIF

        ' 3. Tokenize based on common delimiters
        IF INSTR$("=+-*/\^()[]{},<> ", char$) >= 0 THEN
            PrintToken(buffer$) ' Delimiter found, process the buffer
            buffer$ = ""
            COLOR COLOR_JDB_DEFAULT, 0 ' Color for operators/delimiters
            PRINT char$;
        ELSE
            buffer$ = buffer$ + char$ ' Build up the token
        ENDIF
    NEXT i

    PrintToken(buffer$) ' Print any trailing token at the end of the line

end_of_line:
    COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0 ' Reset to default terminal color
    PRINT ' Newline
ENDSUB

' Helper sub for the highlighter
SUB PrintToken(token$)
    IF token$ = "" THEN RETURN
    
    ucase_token$ = UCASE$(token$)
    
    IF MAP.EXISTS(jdBasicKeywords, ucase_token$) THEN
        COLOR COLOR_JDB_KEYWORD, 0
    ELSEIF VAL(token$) <> 0 OR token$ = "0" THEN ' Simple number check
        COLOR COLOR_JDB_NUMBER, 0
    ELSE
        COLOR COLOR_JDB_DEFAULT, 0
    ENDIF
    PRINT token$;
ENDSUB


' ==========================================================
' == HELPER SUB: PROCESSINLINES(line$)
' == This sub handles inline formatting like bold and italic.
' == It replaces markdown with ANSI escape codes and prints the result.
' ==========================================================
SUB ProcessInlines(line$)
    bold_start$ = cesc_m(Style.Bold)
    bold_end$ = cesc_m(Style.NoBold)
    italic_start$ = cesc_m(Style.Italic)
    italic_end$ = cesc_m(Style.NoItalic)
    bold_italic_start$ = bold_start$ + italic_start$
    bold_italic_end$ = bold_end$ + italic_end$
    code_start$ = cesc_m(Style.Negative)
    code_end$ = cesc_m(Style.Positive)

    ' The order is important to correctly handle nested styles.
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("\*\*\*([^*]+)\*\*\*", line$, bold_italic_start$ + "$1" + bold_italic_end$) ' 1. Bold + Italic (***...***)
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("\*\*([^*]+)\*\*", line$, bold_start$ + "$1" + bold_end$) ' 2. Bold (**...**)
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("\*([^*]+)\*", line$, italic_start$ + "$1" + italic_end$) ' 3. Italic (*...*)
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("`([^`]+)`", line$, code_start$ + "$1" + code_end$)       ' 4. Inline Code (`...`)

    PRINT line$ ' Print the processed line with embedded ANSI codes.
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == MAIN SUB: RENDERMARKDOWN(markdown_text$)
' == Takes a block of Markdown text and prints it formatted.
' ==========================================================
SUB RenderMarkdown(markdown_text$)
    lines = SPLIT(markdown_text$, CHR$(10))
    in_code_block = FALSE
    highlight_jdbasic = FALSE

    FOR EACH line IN lines
        trimmed_line$ = TRIM$(line)
        
        ' --- 1. Handle Code Blocks (Stateful) ---
        IF 3 / trimmed_line$ = "```" THEN
            IF in_code_block THEN ' Closing a block
                in_code_block = FALSE
                highlight_jdbasic = FALSE
            ELSE ' Opening a new block
                in_code_block = TRUE
                lang$ = LCASE$(TRIM$(MID$(trimmed_line$, 3)))
                IF lang$ = "jdbasic" THEN
                    highlight_jdbasic = TRUE
                ENDIF
            ENDIF
            GOTO outi
        ENDIF

        IF in_code_block THEN
            IF highlight_jdbasic THEN
                HighlightJdBasic(line)
            ELSE
                COLOR COLOR_CODE, 0
                PRINT "    "; line
                COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            ENDIF
            GOTO outi
        ENDIF

        ' --- 2. Handle Block-Level Elements ---
        ' Headings (#, ##, ...)
        IF 1 / trimmed_line$ = "#" THEN
            level = 0
            FOR i = 0 TO LEN(trimmed_line$) - 1
                IF MID$(trimmed_line$, i, 1) = "#" THEN
                    level = level + 1
                ELSE
                    EXITFOR
                ENDIF
            NEXT i
            
            heading_text$ = TRIM$(MID$(trimmed_line$, level))
            COLOR COLOR_HEADING, 0
            PRINT
            ProcessInlines(heading_text$) 
            COLOR COLOR_HR, 0
            PRINT "─" * LEN(heading_text$)
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            PRINT
        
        ' Horizontal Rule (---, ***)
        ELSEIF REGEX.MATCH("^-{3,}$", trimmed_line$) OR REGEX.MATCH("^\*{3,}$", trimmed_line$) THEN
            COLOR COLOR_HR, 0
            PRINT "─" * 80
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
        
        ' Blockquote (>)
        ELSEIF REGEX.MATCH("^>", trimmed_line$) THEN
            quote_text$ = TRIM$(MID$(trimmed_line$, 2))
            COLOR COLOR_BLOCKQUOTE, 0
            PRINT " │  ";
            ProcessInlines(quote_text$)
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            
        ' Unordered List (*, -, +)
        ELSEIF REGEX.MATCH("^\s*[*+-]\s+", trimmed_line$) THEN
            list_text$ = REGEX.REPLACE("^\s*[*+-]\s+", trimmed_line$, "")
            COLOR COLOR_LIST, 0
            PRINT "  • ";
            ProcessInlines(list_text$)
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
        
        ' Empty Line
        ELSEIF trimmed_line$ = "" THEN
            PRINT

        ' Paragraph
        ELSE
            ProcessInlines(line)
        ENDIF
outi:       
    
    NEXT
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' ==                 EXAMPLE USAGE
' ==========================================================

' --- Initialize keyword map ---
InitKeywordMap()

' --- Call the main subroutine to render the document ---
CLS

RenderMarkdown(txtreader$("test.md"))

COLOR 7,0 ' Reset to default color at the very end
' --- End of File ---

    IF TYPEOF(EscapeCode) <> "STRING" THEN
        EscapeCode = STR$(EscapeCode)
    ENDIF
    RETURN CHR$(27) + "[" + EscapeCode + "m"

' --- COLOR CONSTANTS ---
COLOR_DEFAULT = 7
COLOR_HEADING = 14
COLOR_CODE_BLOCK = 13
COLOR_BLOCKQUOTE = 10
COLOR_LIST = 7
COLOR_HR = 8
COLOR_LINK = 11        ' Changed for better visibility
COLOR_LINK_SELECTED = 15 ' Bright White
COLOR_LINKBG = 4       ' Dark Red for selected background

COLOR_JDB_DEFAULT = 13 ' Light Magenta

COLOR_JDB_STRING = 10  ' Light Green

Links = []
DIM current_link_index = -1
DIM jdBasicKeywords AS MAP

' ==========================================================
' == HELPER SUB: HIGHLIGHTJDBASIC(line$)
' == A simple tokenizer to apply syntax highlighting to a line of jdBasic code.
' ==========================================================
SUB HighlightJdBasic(line$)
    PRINT "    "; ' Indent the code block line
    
    buffer$ = ""
    in_string = FALSE
    
    FOR i = 0 TO LEN(line$) - 1
        char$ = MID$(line$, i, 1)

        IF char$ = "'" AND NOT in_string THEN
            PrintToken(buffer$)
            buffer$ = ""
            COLOR COLOR_JDB_COMMENT, 0
            PRINT MID$(line$, i);
            GOTO end_of_line
        ENDIF

        IF char$ = CHR$(34) THEN
            PrintToken(buffer$)
            buffer$ = ""
            in_string = NOT in_string
            COLOR COLOR_JDB_STRING, 0
            PRINT CHR$(34);
            CONTINUE
        ENDIF

        IF in_string THEN
            PRINT char$;
            CONTINUE
        ENDIF

        IF INSTR$("=+-*/\^()[]{},<> ", char$) >= 0 THEN
            PrintToken(buffer$)
            buffer$ = ""
            COLOR COLOR_JDB_DEFAULT, 0
            PRINT char$;
        ELSE
            buffer$ = buffer$ + char$
        ENDIF
    NEXT i

    PrintToken(buffer$)

end_of_line:
    COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
    PRINT
ENDSUB

' Helper sub for the highlighter
SUB PrintToken(token$)
    IF token$ = "" THEN RETURN
    ucase_token$ = UCASE$(token$)
    
    IF MAP.EXISTS(jdBasicKeywords, ucase_token$) THEN
        COLOR COLOR_JDB_KEYWORD, 0
    ELSEIF VAL(token$) <> 0 OR token$ = "0" THEN
        COLOR COLOR_JDB_NUMBER, 0
    ELSE
        COLOR COLOR_JDB_DEFAULT, 0
    ENDIF
    PRINT token$;
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == HELPER SUB: PROCESSINLINES(line$)
' ==========================================================
FUNC ProcessInlines(line$)
    local_links = []
    temp_line$ = line$
    
    DO WHILE INSTR$(temp_line$, "[") >= 0 AND INSTR$(temp_line$, "](") > INSTR$(temp_line$, "[")
        match = REGEX.MATCH("\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)]+)\)", temp_line$)
        IF TYPEOF(match) = "ARRAY" THEN
            link_text$ = match[0]
            link_url$ = match[1]
            local_links = APPEND(local_links, link_url$)
            temp_line$ = REPLACE$(temp_line$, "[" + link_text$ + "](" + link_url$ + ")", CHR$(1) + link_text$ + CHR$(1))
        ELSE
            EXITLOOP
        ENDIF
    LOOP
    line$ = temp_line$

    bold_start$ = cesc_m(Style.Bold)
    bold_end$ = cesc_m(Style.NoBold)
    italic_start$ = cesc_m(Style.Italic)
    italic_end$ = cesc_m(Style.NoItalic)
    bold_italic_start$ = bold_start$ + italic_start$
    bold_italic_end$ = bold_end$ + italic_end$
    code_start$ = cesc_m(Style.Negative)
    code_end$ = cesc_m(Style.Positive)

    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("\*\*\*([^*]+)\*\*\*", line$, bold_italic_start$ + "$1" + bold_italic_end$)
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("\*\*([^*]+)\*\*", line$, bold_start$ + "$1" + bold_end$)
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("\*([^*]+)\*", line$, italic_start$ + "$1" + italic_end$)
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("`([^`]+)`", line$, code_start$ + "$1" + code_end$)

    RETURN {"text": line$, "links": local_links}
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == HELPER SUB: RENDERANDCOLLECT(line_info)
' ==========================================================
SUB RenderAndCollect(line_info)
    formatted_line$ = line_info{"text"}
    local_links = line_info{"links"}
    link_counter = 0
    in_link = FALSE
    link_text_buffer$ = ""

    FOR i = 0 TO LEN(formatted_line$) - 1
        char$ = MID$(formatted_line$, i, 1)

        IF ASC(char$) = 1 THEN ' Our link placeholder
            in_link = NOT in_link
            IF in_link THEN
                link_x = GETX()
                link_y = GETY()
                COLOR COLOR_LINK, 0
            ELSE
                link_map = { "url": local_links[link_counter], "text": link_text_buffer$, "x": link_x, "y": link_y }
                Links = APPEND(Links, link_map)
                link_text_buffer$ = ""
                link_counter = link_counter + 1
                COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            ENDIF
        ELSEIF in_link THEN
            link_text_buffer$ = link_text_buffer$ + char$
            PRINT char$;
        ELSE
            PRINT char$;
        ENDIF
    NEXT
    PRINT
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == MAIN SUB: RENDERMARKDOWN(markdown_text$)
' ==========================================================
SUB RenderMarkdown(markdown_text$)
    lines = SPLIT(markdown_text$, CHR$(10))
    in_code_block = FALSE
    highlight_jdbasic = FALSE
    Links = [] ' Clear links for the new page
    current_link_index = -1

    FOR EACH line IN lines
        trimmed_line$ = TRIM$(line)
        
        IF 3 / trimmed_line$ = "```" THEN
            IF in_code_block THEN ' Closing a block
                in_code_block = FALSE
                highlight_jdbasic = FALSE
            ELSE ' Opening a new block
                in_code_block = TRUE
                lang$ = LCASE$(TRIM$(MID$(trimmed_line$, 3)))
                IF lang$ = "jdbasic" OR lang$ = "basic" THEN
                    highlight_jdbasic = TRUE
                ENDIF
            ENDIF
            GOTO outi
        ENDIF

        IF in_code_block THEN
            IF highlight_jdbasic THEN
                HighlightJdBasic(line)
            ELSE
                COLOR COLOR_CODE_BLOCK, 0
                PRINT "    "; line
                COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            ENDIF
            GOTO outi
        ENDIF
        
        IF 1 / trimmed_line$ = "#" THEN
            level = INSTR$(trimmed_line$, " ")
            heading_text$ = TRIM$(MID$(trimmed_line$, level))
            COLOR COLOR_HEADING, 0
            PRINT
            RenderAndCollect(ProcessInlines(heading_text$))
            COLOR COLOR_HR, 0
            PRINT "─" * LEN(heading_text$)
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            PRINT
        
        ELSEIF REGEX.MATCH("^-{3,}$", trimmed_line$) OR REGEX.MATCH("^\*{3,}$", trimmed_line$) THEN
            COLOR COLOR_HR, 0
            PRINT "─" * 80
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
        
        ELSEIF REGEX.MATCH("^>", trimmed_line$) THEN
            quote_text$ = TRIM$(MID$(trimmed_line$, 2))
            COLOR COLOR_BLOCKQUOTE, 0
            PRINT " │  ";
            RenderAndCollect(ProcessInlines(quote_text$))
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            
        ELSEIF REGEX.MATCH("^\s*[*+-]\s+", trimmed_line$) THEN
            list_text$ = REGEX.REPLACE("^\s*[*+-]\s+", trimmed_line$, "")
            COLOR COLOR_LIST, 0
            PRINT "  • ";
            RenderAndCollect(ProcessInlines(list_text$))
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
        
        ELSEIF trimmed_line$ = "" THEN
            PRINT

        ELSE ' Paragraph
            RenderAndCollect(ProcessInlines(line))
        ENDIF
outi:       
    NEXT
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == INTERACTION FUNCTIONS
' ==========================================================
SUB HighlightLink(index, selected)
    IF index < 0 OR index >= LEN(Links) THEN RETURN
    link = Links[index]
    LOCATE link[0]{"y"}, link[0]{"x"}
    IF selected THEN
        COLOR COLOR_LINK, COLOR_LINKBG
    ELSE
        COLOR COLOR_LINK_SELECTED, 0
    ENDIF
    PRINT cesc_m(Style.Underline); link[0]{"text"}; cesc_m(Style.NoUnderline);
    COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
ENDSUB

SUB SelectNextLink()
    HighlightLink current_link_index, FALSE
    current_link_index = (current_link_index + 1) MOD LEN(Links)
    HighlightLink current_link_index, TRUE 
ENDSUB

FUNC OpenSelectedLink()
    IF current_link_index < 0 OR LEN(Links) = 0 THEN RETURN ""

    url$ = Links[current_link_index][0]{"url"}
    
    IF LCASE$(RIGHT$(url$, 3)) = ".md" THEN
        RETURN url$
    ENDIF

    os$ = OS.GETOS()
    LOCATE GETY() + 2, 1
    PRINT "Opening external link: " + url$
    
    TRY
        IF os$ = "WINDOWS" THEN 
            OS.EXEC("start", [url$])
        ELSEIF os$ = "MACOS" THEN 
            OS.EXEC("open", [url$])
        ELSE 
            OS.EXEC("xdg-open", [url$])
        ENDIF
    CATCH
        PRINT "Failed to open link. Error: "; ERRMSG$
        SLEEP 2000
    ENDTRY
    
    RETURN ""
ENDFUNC

FUNC InteractionLoop()
    IF LEN(Links) = 0 THEN
        PRINT "No links on this page. Press any key to quit."
        WAITKEY$()
        RETURN "quit"
    ENDIF
    
    orig_y = GETY()
    LOCATE orig_y + 1, 1
    PRINT "TAB: cycle links, ENTER: open, ESC/Q: quit."
    SelectNextLink()
    
    DO
        key$ = WAITKEY$()
        SWITCH ASC(key$)
            CASE 9 ' TAB
                SelectNextLink()
            CASE 13 ' Enter
                next_action$ = OpenSelectedLink()
                IF next_action$ <> "" THEN
                    LOCATE orig_y + 1, 1: PRINT " " * 80
                    RETURN next_action$
                ENDIF
            CASE 27 ' ESC
                RETURN "quit"
        ENDSWITCH
        IF LCASE$(key$) = "q" THEN RETURN "quit"
    LOOP
ENDFUNC

' ==========================================================
' ==                 MAIN BROWSER LOOP
' ==========================================================
current_file$ = "test.md" ' Set your starting file here
InitKeywordMap() ' Initialize the keyword map once
CLS

DO
    content$ = ""
    IF LEFT$(LCASE$(current_file$), 4) = "http" THEN
        TRY
            PRINT "Fetching "; current_file$; "..."
            content$ = HTTP.GET$(current_file$)
        CATCH
            CLS
            PRINT "Error fetching URL: "; current_file$
            PRINT ERRMSG$
            SLEEP 3000
            current_file$ = "quit"
        ENDTRY
    ELSE
        TRY
            content$ = TXTREADER$(current_file$)
        CATCH
            CLS
            PRINT "Error reading local file: "; current_file$
            PRINT ERRMSG$
            SLEEP 3000
            current_file$ = "quit"
        ENDTRY
    ENDIF

    IF current_file$ <> "quit" THEN
        CLS
        RenderMarkdown(content$)
        current_file$ = InteractionLoop()
    ENDIF
LOOP UNTIL current_file$ = "quit"

' --- Cleanup ---
CLS
COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
PRINT "Markdown browser closed."
' --- End of File ---

Filename: mdfox.jdb
