' --- GLOBAL VARIABLES FOR INTERACTIVITY ---
Links = []
DIM current_link_index = -1

' ==========================================================
' == HELPER SUB: PROCESSINLINES(line$)
' == This sub handles inline formatting. It returns a map containing
' == the formatted string and an array of extracted link URLs.
' ==========================================================
FUNC ProcessInlines(line$)
    local_links = []
    temp_line$ = line$
    
    DO WHILE INSTR$(temp_line$, "[") >= 0 AND INSTR$(temp_line$, "](") > INSTR$(temp_line$, "[")
        match = REGEX.MATCH("\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)]+)\)", temp_line$)
        IF TYPEOF(match) = "ARRAY" THEN
            link_text$ = match[0]
            link_url$ = match[1]
            local_links = APPEND(local_links, link_url$)
            temp_line$ = REPLACE$(temp_line$, "[" + link_text$ + "](" + link_url$ + ")", CHR$(1) + link_text$ + CHR$(1))
        ELSE
            EXITLOOP
        ENDIF
    LOOP
    line$ = temp_line$

    bold_start$ = cesc_m(Style.Bold)
    bold_end$ = cesc_m(Style.NoBold)
    italic_start$ = cesc_m(Style.Italic)
    italic_end$ = cesc_m(Style.NoItalic)
    bold_italic_start$ = bold_start$ + italic_start$
    bold_italic_end$ = bold_end$ + italic_end$
    code_start$ = cesc_m(Style.Negative)
    code_end$ = cesc_m(Style.Positive)

    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("\*\*\*([^*]+)\*\*\*", line$, bold_italic_start$ + "$1" + bold_italic_end$)
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("\*\*([^*]+)\*\*", line$, bold_start$ + "$1" + bold_end$)
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("\*([^*]+)\*", line$, italic_start$ + "$1" + italic_end$)
    line$ = REGEX.REPLACE("`([^`]+)`", line$, code_start$ + "$1" + code_end$)

    RETURN {"text": line$, "links": local_links}
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == HELPER SUB: RENDERANDCOLLECT(line_info)
' == Prints a formatted line character by character, collecting
' == link positions as it goes.
' ==========================================================
SUB RenderAndCollect(line_info)
    formatted_line$ = line_info{"text"}
    local_links = line_info{"links"}
    link_counter = 0
    in_link = FALSE
    link_text_buffer$ = ""

    FOR i = 0 TO LEN(formatted_line$) - 1
        char$ = MID$(formatted_line$, i, 1)

        IF ASC(char$) = 1 THEN ' Our link placeholder
            in_link = NOT in_link
            IF in_link THEN
                link_x = GETX()
                link_y = GETY()
                COLOR COLOR_LINK, 0
            ELSE
                link_map = { "url": local_links[link_counter], "text": link_text_buffer$, "x": link_x, "y": link_y }
                Links = APPEND(Links, link_map)
                link_text_buffer$ = ""
                link_counter = link_counter + 1
                COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            ENDIF
        ELSEIF in_link THEN
            link_text_buffer$ = link_text_buffer$ + char$
            PRINT char$;
        ELSE
            PRINT char$;
        ENDIF
    NEXT
    PRINT
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == MAIN SUB: RENDERMARKDOWN(markdown_text$)
' == Takes a block of Markdown text and prints it formatted.
' ==========================================================
SUB RenderMarkdown(markdown_text$)
    lines = SPLIT(markdown_text$, CHR$(10))
    in_code_block = FALSE
    Links = [] ' Clear links for the new page
    current_link_index = -1

    FOR EACH line IN lines
        trimmed_line$ = TRIM$(line)
        
        IF 3 / trimmed_line$ = "```" THEN
            in_code_block = NOT in_code_block
            GOTO outi
        ENDIF

        IF in_code_block THEN
            COLOR COLOR_CODE_BLOCK, 0
            PRINT "    "; line
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            GOTO outi
        ENDIF
        
        IF 1 / trimmed_line$ = "#" THEN
            level = INSTR$(trimmed_line$, " ")
            heading_text$ = TRIM$(MID$(trimmed_line$, level))
            COLOR COLOR_HEADING, 0
            PRINT
            RenderAndCollect(ProcessInlines(heading_text$))
            COLOR COLOR_HR, 0
            PRINT "─" * LEN(heading_text$)
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            PRINT
        
        ELSEIF REGEX.MATCH("^-{3,}$", trimmed_line$) OR REGEX.MATCH("^\*{3,}$", trimmed_line$) THEN
            COLOR COLOR_HR, 0
            PRINT "─" * 80
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
        
        ELSEIF REGEX.MATCH("^>", trimmed_line$) THEN
            quote_text$ = TRIM$(MID$(trimmed_line$, 2))
            COLOR COLOR_BLOCKQUOTE, 0
            PRINT " │  ";
            RenderAndCollect(ProcessInlines(quote_text$))
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
            
        ELSEIF REGEX.MATCH("^\s*[*+-]\s+", trimmed_line$) THEN
            list_text$ = REGEX.REPLACE("^\s*[*+-]\s+", trimmed_line$, "")
            COLOR COLOR_LIST, 0
            PRINT "  • ";
            RenderAndCollect(ProcessInlines(list_text$))
            COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
        
        ELSEIF trimmed_line$ = "" THEN
            PRINT

        ELSE ' Paragraph
            RenderAndCollect(ProcessInlines(line))
        ENDIF
outi:       
    NEXT
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == INTERACTION FUNCTIONS
' ==========================================================
SUB HighlightLink(index, selected)
    IF index < 0 OR index >= LEN(Links) THEN RETURN
    link = Links[index]
    LOCATE link[0]{"y"}, link[0]{"x"}
    IF selected THEN
        COLOR COLOR_LINK, COLOR_LINKBG
    ELSE
        COLOR COLOR_LINK_SELECTED, 0
    ENDIF
    PRINT cesc_m(Style.Underline); link[0]{"text"}; cesc_m(Style.NoUnderline);
    COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
ENDSUB

SUB SelectNextLink()
    HighlightLink current_link_index, FALSE
    current_link_index = (current_link_index + 1) MOD LEN(Links)
    HighlightLink current_link_index, TRUE 
ENDSUB

' Decides whether to browse to a new .md file or open an external link.
' Returns the new .md file path, or "" if no navigation occurs.
FUNC OpenSelectedLink()
    IF current_link_index < 0 OR LEN(Links) = 0 THEN RETURN ""

    url$ = Links[current_link_index][0]{"url"}
    
    ' Check if it's a markdown file to browse internally
    IF LCASE$(RIGHT$(url$, 3)) = ".md" THEN
        RETURN url$
    ENDIF

    ' It's an external link, open it with the system
    os$ = OS.GETOS()
    LOCATE GETY() + 2, 1
    PRINT "Opening external link: " + url$
    
    ' TRY
    '     IF os$ = "WINDOWS" THEN 
    '         OS.EXEC("start", [url$])
    '     ELSEIF os$ = "MACOS" THEN 
    '         OS.EXEC("open", [url$])
    '     ELSE 
    '         OS.EXEC("xdg-open", [url$])
    '     ENDIF
    ' CATCH
    '     PRINT "Failed to open link. Error: "; ERRMSG$
    '     SLEEP 2000 ' Show error for a moment
    ' ENDTRY
    
    RETURN "" ' Indicate that no internal navigation should happen
ENDFUNC

' Handles the user input loop after a page is rendered.
' Returns the next action: a new URL to browse, or "quit".
FUNC InteractionLoop()
    IF LEN(Links) = 0 THEN
        PRINT "No links on this page. Press any key to quit."
        WAITKEY$()
        RETURN "quit"
    ENDIF
    
    orig_y = GETY()
    LOCATE orig_y + 1, 1
    PRINT "TAB: cycle links, ENTER: open, ESC/Q: quit."
    
    SelectNextLink() ' Highlight the first link
    
    DO
        key$ = WAITKEY$()
        SWITCH ASC(key$)
            CASE 9 ' TAB
                SelectNextLink()
            CASE 13 ' Enter
                next_action$ = OpenSelectedLink()
                IF next_action$ <> "" THEN
                    LOCATE orig_y + 1, 1: PRINT " " * 80
                    RETURN next_action$ ' Return new .md file to browse
                ENDIF
            CASE 27 ' ESC
                RETURN "quit"
        ENDSWITCH
        IF LCASE$(key$) = "q" THEN RETURN "quit"
    LOOP
ENDFUNC

' ==========================================================
' ==                 MAIN BROWSER LOOP
' ==========================================================
current_file$ = "test.md" ' Set your starting file here
CLS
DO
    content$ = ""
    ' --- 1. Load the content of the markdown file ---
    IF LEFT$(LCASE$(current_file$), 4) = "http" THEN
        TRY
            PRINT "Fetching "; current_file$; "..."
            content$ = HTTP.GET$(current_file$)
        CATCH
            CLS
            PRINT "Error fetching URL: "; current_file$
            PRINT ERRMSG$
            SLEEP 3000
            current_file$ = "quit" ' Exit on error
        ENDTRY
    ELSE
        TRY
            content$ = TXTREADER$(current_file$)
        CATCH
            CLS
            PRINT "Error reading local file: "; current_file$
            PRINT ERRMSG$
            SLEEP 3000
            current_file$ = "quit" ' Exit on error
        ENDTRY
    ENDIF

    ' --- 2. If content loaded, render it and start interaction ---
    IF current_file$ <> "quit" THEN
        CLS
        RenderMarkdown(content$)
        current_file$ = InteractionLoop()
    ENDIF

LOOP UNTIL current_file$ = "quit"

' --- 3. Cleanup ---
CLS
COLOR COLOR_DEFAULT, 0
PRINT "Markdown browser closed."
' --- End of File ---

Filename: mine.jdb
' ==========================================================
' == Minesweeper for jdBasic
' ==
' == Controls:
' ==   W, A, S, D   : Move Cursor
' ==   SPACE / E    : Reveal Cell
' ==   F            : Flag Cell
' ==   Q            : Quit
' ==========================================================

' --- Configuration ---
ROWS = 10
COLS = 15
NUM_MINES = 20

' --- State Constants ---
STATE_HIDDEN = 0
STATE_REVEALED = 1
STATE_FLAGGED = 2

' --- Grid Values ---
CELL_EMPTY = 0
CELL_MINE = 9

' --- Initialize Globals ---
DIM Grid[ROWS, COLS] AS INTEGER      ' Holds the numbers and mines
DIM State[ROWS, COLS] AS INTEGER     ' Holds hidden/revealed/flagged status
CursorX = 0
CursorY = 0
GameOver = FALSE
GameWon = FALSE
FirstMove = TRUE

DIM K$ AS STRING

SUB HandleKeys(data)
    K$ = chr$(data[0]{"scancode"})
ENDSUB

ON "KEYDOWN" CALL HandleKeys

' ==========================================================
' == SUB: Initialize Game
' ==========================================================
SUB InitGame()
    Grid = reshape([CELL_EMPTY],[rows,cols])
    State = reshape([STATE_HIDDEN],[rows,cols])

    CursorX = INT(COLS / 2)
    CursorY = INT(ROWS / 2)
    GameOver = FALSE
    GameWon = FALSE
    FirstMove = TRUE
    COLOR 8, 0
    CLS
    PRINT "Generating Minefield..."
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == SUB: Place Mines
' == We do this after the first move to ensure the first
' == click is never a mine.
' ==========================================================
SUB PlaceMines(safe_r, safe_c)
    mines_placed = 0
    
    DO WHILE mines_placed < NUM_MINES
        r = INT(RND(1) * ROWS)
        c = INT(RND(1) * COLS)
        
        ' Place mine if empty and not the safe starting spot
        IF Grid[r, c] <> CELL_MINE THEN
            ' Ensure we don't place it on the start or immediate neighbors (optional, simple check here)
            IF r <> safe_r OR c <> safe_c THEN
                Grid[r, c] = CELL_MINE
                mines_placed = mines_placed + 1
            ENDIF
        ENDIF
    
    LOOP 

    ' Calculate numbers for non-mine cells
    FOR r = 0 TO ROWS - 1
        FOR c = 0 TO COLS - 1
            IF Grid[r, c] <> CELL_MINE THEN
                count = 0
                ' Check all 8 neighbors
                FOR dr = -1 TO 1
                    FOR dc = -1 TO 1
                        nr = r + dr
                        nc = c + dc
                        IF nr >= 0 AND nr < ROWS AND nc >= 0 AND nc < COLS THEN
                            IF Grid[nr, nc] = CELL_MINE THEN count = count + 1
                        ENDIF
                    NEXT dc
                NEXT dr
                Grid[r, c] = count
            ENDIF
        NEXT c
    NEXT r
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == SUB: Reveal Cell (Recursive Flood Fill)
' ==========================================================
SUB Reveal(r, c)
    ' Bounds check
    IF r < 0 OR r >= ROWS OR c < 0 OR c >= COLS THEN RETURN
    
    ' Only reveal hidden cells
    IF State[r, c] <> STATE_HIDDEN THEN RETURN

    ' Reveal it
    State[r, c] = STATE_REVEALED

    ' If we hit a mine, game over
    IF Grid[r, c] = CELL_MINE THEN
        GameOver = TRUE
        RETURN
    ENDIF

    ' If it's empty (0), recursively reveal neighbors
    IF Grid[r, c] = CELL_EMPTY THEN
        FOR dr = -1 TO 1
            FOR dc = -1 TO 1
                IF dr <> 0 OR dc <> 0 THEN
                    Reveal(r + dr, c + dc)
                ENDIF
            NEXT dc
        NEXT dr
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == SUB: Check Win Condition
' ==========================================================
SUB CheckWin()
    revealed_cells = SUM(ABS(State = STATE_REVEALED))
    ' If all non-mine cells are revealed, you win
    total_safe_cells = (ROWS * COLS) - NUM_MINES
    IF revealed_cells = total_safe_cells THEN
        GameWon = TRUE
        GameOver = TRUE
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == SUB: Draw Board
' ==========================================================
SUB DrawBoard()
    LOCATE 1, 1
    PRINT "-" * 5;  " jdBasic Minesweeper "; "-" * 5
    PRINT "Arrow/WASD: Move | Space: Reveal"
    PRINT "F: Flag | R: Restart | Q: Quit"
    PRINT "Mines: "; NUM_MINES
    PRINT "-" * 31

    ' Board offset
    off_y = 6
    off_x = 3

    FOR r = 0 TO ROWS - 1
        LOCATE off_y + r, off_x
        
        FOR c = 0 TO COLS - 1
            ' Determine what to draw
            cell_char$ = "?"
            fg_col = 7  ' Default Gray
            bg_col = 0  ' Black

            curr_state = State[r, c]
            curr_val = Grid[r, c]

            IF curr_state = STATE_HIDDEN THEN
                cell_char$ = "."
                fg_col = 8 ' Dark Gray
                IF GameOver AND curr_val = CELL_MINE THEN
                    cell_char$ = "*" ' Show mines on loss
                    fg_col = 9 ' Light Red
                ENDIF
            ELSEIF curr_state = STATE_FLAGGED THEN
                cell_char$ = "F"
                fg_col = 9 ' Light Red
                IF GameOver AND curr_val <> CELL_MINE THEN
                    cell_char$ = "X" ' Wrong flag
                    fg_col = 5 ' Magenta
                ENDIF
            ELSEIF curr_state = STATE_REVEALED THEN
                IF curr_val = CELL_MINE THEN
                    cell_char$ = "*"
                    fg_col = 1 ' Red
                    bg_col = 4  ' Dark Red background
                ELSEIF curr_val = 0 THEN
                    cell_char$ = " "
                ELSE
                    cell_char$ = STR$(curr_val)
                    ' Color numbers
                    IF curr_val = 1 THEN fg_col = 4  ' Blue
                    IF curr_val = 2 THEN fg_col = 10 ' Green
                    IF curr_val = 3 THEN fg_col = 1 ' Red
                    IF curr_val >= 4 THEN fg_col = 5 ' Magenta
                ENDIF
            ENDIF

            ' Highlight Cursor
            IF r = CursorY AND c = CursorX THEN
                bg_col = 8 ' Dark Gray background for cursor
            ENDIF

            COLOR fg_col, bg_col
            PRINT cell_char$; " ";
        NEXT c
    NEXT r
    
    ' Reset colors
    COLOR 15, 0
    PRINT "" 
    PRINT ""
    IF GameWon THEN
        COLOR 10, 0
        PRINT "CONGRATULATIONS! YOU CLEARED THE MINEFIELD!"
    ELSEIF GameOver THEN
        COLOR 12, 0
        PRINT "BOOM! GAME OVER."
    ELSE
        PRINT "                     " ' Clear status line
    ENDIF
    COLOR 7, 0
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == Main Loop
' ==========================================================

InitGame()

DO
    DrawBoard()
    
    ' Simple input loop
    k$ = ""
    DO 
        SLEEP 10
    LOOP UNTIL k$ > ""

    k$ = LCASE$(k$)

    ' Only process input if game is not over
    IF NOT GameOver THEN
        ' Movement
        IF k$ = "w" AND CursorY > 0 THEN CursorY = CursorY - 1
        IF k$ = "s" AND CursorY < ROWS - 1 THEN CursorY = CursorY + 1
        IF k$ = "a" AND CursorX > 0 THEN CursorX = CursorX - 1
        IF k$ = "d" AND CursorX < COLS - 1 THEN CursorX = CursorX + 1
        
        ' Reveal (Space or E)
        IF k$ = " " OR k$ = "e" THEN
            IF State[CursorY, CursorX] = STATE_HIDDEN THEN
                IF FirstMove THEN
                    PlaceMines(CursorY, CursorX)
                    FirstMove = FALSE
                ENDIF
                Reveal(CursorY, CursorX)
                CheckWin()
            ENDIF
        ENDIF
        
        ' Flag (F)
        IF k$ = "f" THEN
            IF State[CursorY, CursorX] = STATE_HIDDEN THEN
                State[CursorY, CursorX] = STATE_FLAGGED
            ELSEIF State[CursorY, CursorX] = STATE_FLAGGED THEN
                State[CursorY, CursorX] = STATE_HIDDEN
            ENDIF
        ENDIF
    ENDIF

    ' Quit or Reset
    IF k$ = "q" THEN EXITDO
    IF k$ = "r" THEN InitGame()

LOOP

CLS
PRINT "Thanks for playing!"
' --- End of File ---

Filename: mine_joy.jdb
' ==========================================================
' == Minesweeper for jdBasic (Color, Joystick, Restart)
' ==========================================================

' --- Configuration ---
ROWS = 10
COLS = 15
NUM_MINES = 20

' --- State Constants ---
STATE_HIDDEN = 0
STATE_REVEALED = 1
STATE_FLAGGED = 2

' --- Grid Values ---
CELL_EMPTY = 0
CELL_MINE = 9

' --- Initialize Globals ---
DIM Grid[ROWS, COLS] AS INTEGER      
DIM State[ROWS, COLS] AS INTEGER     
DIM RenderedState[ROWS, COLS] AS INTEGER 

CursorX = 0
CursorY = 0
GameOver = FALSE
GameWon = FALSE
FirstMove = TRUE
BoardDirty = TRUE 
GameOverDrawn = FALSE

DIM K$ AS STRING
K$ = ""

' Callback for Keyboard Input
SUB HandleKeys(data)
    K$ = chr$(data[0]{"scancode"})
ENDSUB

ON "KEYDOWN" CALL HandleKeys

' ==========================================================
' == SUB: Initialize Game
' ==========================================================
SUB InitGame()
    Grid = reshape([CELL_EMPTY],[rows,cols])
    State = reshape([STATE_HIDDEN],[rows,cols])
    
    ' Force redraw of all cells
    RenderedState = reshape([-1],[rows,cols])

    CursorX = INT(COLS / 2)
    CursorY = INT(ROWS / 2)
    GameOver = FALSE
    GameWon = FALSE
    FirstMove = TRUE
    GameOverDrawn = FALSE
    
    K$ = "" 
    
    DrawStaticUI()
    BoardDirty = TRUE
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == SUB: Draw Static UI (Borders, Text)
' ==========================================================
SUB DrawStaticUI()
    CLS
    COLOR 15, 0 
    PRINT "   MINESWEEPER (" + NUM_MINES + " Mines)"
    COLOR 7, 0  
    PRINT "   [WASD] Move [Space] Reveal [F] Flag [R] Restart"
    PRINT ""
    
    ' Top Border using string multiplication
    line$ = "   +" + ("---" * COLS) + "+"
    PRINT line$
    
    ' Rows placeholders 
    row_space$ = " " * (COLS * 3)
    FOR r = 0 TO ROWS - 1
        PRINT "   |" + row_space$ + "|"
    NEXT r
    
    ' Bottom Border
    PRINT line$
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == SUB: Update Screen (Smart Redraw)
' ==========================================================
SUB UpdateScreen()
    StartRow = 5
    StartCol = 5

    FOR r = 0 TO ROWS - 1
        FOR c = 0 TO COLS - 1
            
            visVal = Grid[r, c]
            visState = State[r, c]
            isCursor = 0
            IF r = CursorY AND c = CursorX THEN isCursor = 1
            
            ' Hash: 1000*Cursor + 100*State + Value
            currentHash = (isCursor * 1000) + (visState * 100) + visVal
            
            ' Only redraw if visual state has changed
            IF currentHash <> RenderedState[r, c] THEN
                
                cellContent$ = " "
                fg = 7  
                bg = 0  

                IF visState = STATE_FLAGGED THEN
                    cellContent$ = "F"
                    fg = 12 
                ELSEIF visState = STATE_REVEALED THEN
                    IF visVal = CELL_MINE THEN 
                        cellContent$ = "*" 
                        bg = 4  
                        fg = 15 
                    ELSEIF visVal = 0 THEN
                        cellContent$ = " "
                    ELSE
                        cellContent$ = str$(visVal)
                        IF visVal = 1 THEN fg = 9 
                        IF visVal = 2 THEN fg = 10 
                        IF visVal = 3 THEN fg = 12 
                        IF visVal = 4 THEN fg = 1 
                        IF visVal = 5 THEN fg = 4 
                        IF visVal = 6 THEN fg = 3 
                        IF visVal = 7 THEN fg = 8 
                        IF visVal = 8 THEN fg = 7 
                    ENDIF
                ELSE
                    cellContent$ = "."
                    fg = 8 
                ENDIF
                
                finalStr$ = IIF(isCursor=1, "[" + cellContent$ + "]", " " + cellContent$ + " ")
                
                LOCATE StartRow + r, StartCol + (c * 3)
                COLOR fg, bg
                PRINT finalStr$;
                
                RenderedState[r, c] = currentHash
            ENDIF
        NEXT c
    NEXT r
    
    COLOR 7, 0

    ' Draw Game Over / Win Message ONCE
    IF GameOver AND NOT GameOverDrawn THEN
        LOCATE StartRow + ROWS + 1, 4
        IF GameWon THEN 
            COLOR 10, 0
            PRINT "YOU WIN!                    " 
        ELSE 
            COLOR 12, 0
            PRINT "GAME OVER!                  " 
        ENDIF
        
        LOCATE StartRow + ROWS + 2, 4
        COLOR 7, 0
        PRINT "Press R to Restart, Q to Quit."
        GameOverDrawn = TRUE
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == SUB: Place Mines
' ==========================================================
SUB PlaceMines(safeX, safeY)
    placed = 0
    DO WHILE placed < NUM_MINES
        r = INT(RND(1) * ROWS)
        c = INT(RND(1) * COLS)
        
        IF Grid[r, c] <> CELL_MINE AND (r <> safeY OR c <> safeX) THEN
            Grid[r, c] = CELL_MINE
            placed = placed + 1
            
            FOR dr = -1 TO 1
                FOR dc = -1 TO 1
                    nr = r + dr
                    nc = c + dc
                    IF nr >= 0 AND nr < ROWS AND nc >= 0 AND nc < COLS THEN
                        IF Grid[nr, nc] <> CELL_MINE THEN Grid[nr, nc] = Grid[nr, nc] + 1
                    ENDIF
                NEXT dc
            NEXT dr
        ENDIF
    LOOP
ENDSUB

SUB Reveal(r, c)
    IF r < 0 OR r >= ROWS OR c < 0 OR c >= COLS THEN EXITSUB
    IF State[r, c] <> STATE_HIDDEN THEN EXITSUB
    
    State[r, c] = STATE_REVEALED
    BoardDirty = TRUE
    
    IF Grid[r, c] = CELL_MINE THEN
        GameOver = TRUE
        FOR rr = 0 TO ROWS-1
            FOR cc = 0 TO COLS-1
                IF Grid[rr,cc] = CELL_MINE THEN 
                    State[rr,cc] = STATE_REVEALED
                    RenderedState[rr,cc] = -1 
                ENDIF
            NEXT cc
        NEXT rr
    ELSEIF Grid[r, c] = CELL_EMPTY THEN
        FOR dr = -1 TO 1
            FOR dc = -1 TO 1
                IF dr <> 0 OR dc <> 0 THEN Reveal(r + dr, c + dc)
            NEXT dc
        NEXT dr
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

SUB CheckWin()
    hidden_count = 0
    FOR r = 0 TO ROWS - 1
        FOR c = 0 TO COLS - 1
            IF State[r, c] <> STATE_REVEALED THEN hidden_count = hidden_count + 1
        NEXT c
    NEXT r
    
    IF hidden_count = NUM_MINES THEN
        GameWon = TRUE
        GameOver = TRUE
        BoardDirty = TRUE
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

' ==========================================================
' == Main Loop
' ==========================================================

InitGame()

DO
    UpdateScreen()
    
    MoveX_Dir = 0
    MoveY_Dir = 0
    DoReveal = FALSE
    DoFlag = FALSE
    DoQuit = FALSE
    DoRestart = FALSE
    ActionTaken = FALSE

    ' --- 1. Keyboard Input ---
    IF K$ > "" THEN
        k_lower$ = LCASE$(K$)
        IF k_lower$ = "w" THEN MoveY_Dir = -1
        IF k_lower$ = "s" THEN MoveY_Dir = 1
        IF k_lower$ = "a" THEN MoveX_Dir = -1
        IF k_lower$ = "d" THEN MoveX_Dir = 1
        IF k_lower$ = " " OR k_lower$ = "e" THEN DoReveal = TRUE
        IF k_lower$ = "f" THEN DoFlag = TRUE
        IF k_lower$ = "q" THEN DoQuit = TRUE
        IF k_lower$ = "r" THEN DoRestart = TRUE
        K$ = ""
        ActionTaken = TRUE
    ENDIF

    ' --- 2. Joystick Input ---
    IF JOY.COUNT() > 0 THEN
        joyID = 0
        
        AX = JOY.AXIS(joyID, 0)
        AY = JOY.AXIS(joyID, 1)
        
        IF AY < -0.5 THEN MoveY_Dir = -1
        IF AY > 0.5 THEN MoveY_Dir = 1
        IF AX < -0.5 THEN MoveX_Dir = -1
        IF AX > 0.5 THEN MoveX_Dir = 1
        
        Hat = JOY.HAT(joyID, 0)
        IF (Hat BAND 1) THEN MoveY_Dir = -1
        IF (Hat BAND 4) THEN MoveY_Dir = 1
        IF (Hat BAND 8) THEN MoveX_Dir = -1
        IF (Hat BAND 2) THEN MoveX_Dir = 1
        
        IF JOY.BUTTON(joyID, 0) THEN DoReveal = TRUE
        IF JOY.BUTTON(joyID, 1) OR JOY.BUTTON(joyID, 2) THEN DoFlag = TRUE
        IF JOY.BUTTON(joyID, 6) THEN DoQuit = TRUE
        IF JOY.BUTTON(joyID, 4) THEN DoRestart = TRUE ' Select/Back button for Restart
        
        IF MoveX_Dir <> 0 OR MoveY_Dir <> 0 OR DoReveal OR DoFlag OR DoRestart THEN ActionTaken = TRUE
    ENDIF

    IF DoQuit THEN EXITDO

    IF DoRestart THEN
        InitGame()
        ActionTaken = FALSE ' Skip move logic
    ENDIF

    IF NOT GameOver AND ActionTaken THEN
        IF MoveY_Dir = -1 AND CursorY > 0 THEN CursorY = CursorY - 1
        IF MoveY_Dir = 1 AND CursorY < ROWS - 1 THEN CursorY = CursorY + 1
        IF MoveX_Dir = -1 AND CursorX > 0 THEN CursorX = CursorX - 1
        IF MoveX_Dir = 1 AND CursorX < COLS - 1 THEN CursorX = CursorX + 1
        
        IF DoReveal THEN
            IF State[CursorY, CursorX] = STATE_HIDDEN THEN
                IF FirstMove THEN
                    PlaceMines(CursorY, CursorX)
                    FirstMove = FALSE
                ENDIF
                Reveal(CursorY, CursorX)
                CheckWin()
            ENDIF
        ENDIF
        
        IF DoFlag THEN
            IF State[CursorY, CursorX] = STATE_HIDDEN THEN
                State[CursorY, CursorX] = STATE_FLAGGED
            ELSEIF State[CursorY, CursorX] = STATE_FLAGGED THEN
                State[CursorY, CursorX] = STATE_HIDDEN
            ENDIF
        ENDIF
    ENDIF

    SLEEP 100
LOOP

CLS
PRINT "Thanks for playing!"
' --- End of File ---

Filename: minicalc.jdb
' ============================================================
' jdBasic Mini Calc (3x10) - Values Grid + Formula Bar
' ============================================================

OPTION "NOPAUSE"

' Consts
ROWS = 10
COLS = 3
DECI = -1

' State
DIM Cell$[ROWS, COLS] AS STRING
DIM Sheet[ROWS, COLS] AS DOUBLE

' defaults
FOR r = 0 TO ROWS - 1
    FOR c = 0 TO COLS - 1
        Cell$[r, c] = ""
    NEXT c
NEXT r

' sample
Cell$[0,0] = "10"
Cell$[0,1] = "5"
Cell$[0,2] = "=A1+B1"
Cell$[1,0] = "3"
Cell$[1,1] = "4"
Cell$[1,2] = "=SQR(A2^2 + B2^2)"

SelR = 0
SelC = 0
AutoRecalc = TRUE
WindowOpen = TRUE

EditActive = FALSE
EditBuf$ = ""
EditX = 0 : EditY = 0 : EditW = 0 : EditH = 0
NeedFocus = FALSE
EndMe = FALSE
LastKey = 0

' --- File dialog state ---
FileDlgOpen = FALSE
FileDlgMode$ = ""          ' "LOAD" or "SAVE"
FileDlgName$ = ""          ' user input (without extension ok)
FileDlgMsg$  = ""          ' status/error line

SUB HandleKey(KeyData)
    KeyCode = KeyData[0]{"keycode"}
    LastKey = KeyCode
    if KeyCode = 1073741891 THEN
        TOGGLE_FULLSCREEN
        ScreenW = ScreenWidth()
        ScreenH = ScreenHeight()
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

ON "KEYDOWN" CALL HandleKey

FUNC JDC_FILE$(name$)
    n$ = TRIM$(name$)
    IF n$ = "" THEN RETURN ""
    IF LCASE$(RIGHT$(n$, 4)) <> ".jdc" THEN n$ = n$ + ".jdc"
    RETURN n$
ENDFUNC

SUB SAVE_JDC(name$, CELLTXT$)
    fname$ = JDC_FILE$(name$)
    IF fname$ = "" THEN RETURN
    payload = {"rows": ROWS, "cols": COLS, "cell": CELLTXT$}
    s = JSON.STRINGIFY$(payload)
    TXTWRITER fname$, s
ENDSUB

SUB LOAD_JDC(name$)
    fname$ = JDC_FILE$(name$)
    IF fname$ = "" THEN RETURN

    txt$ = TXTREADER$(fname$)
    jo = JSON.PARSE$(txt$)

    Cell$ = reshape(jo{"cell"},[jo{"rows"},jo{"cols"}])

    Sheet = CALC_SHEET(Cell$)
ENDSUB

FUNC CELLNAME$(r, c)
    RETURN MID$("ABC", c, 1) + STR$(r + 1)
ENDFUNC

' --- Core calculation (iterative so forward refs also work) ---
FUNC CALC_SHEET(CELLTXT$)
    DIM OUT[ROWS, COLS] AS DOUBLE

    ' Pass 1: fill numbers (and blanks -> 0)
    FOR r = 0 TO ROWS - 1
        FOR c = 0 TO COLS - 1
            raw$ = TRIM$(CELLTXT$[r, c])
            IF raw$ = "" THEN
                OUT[r, c] = 0
            ELSEIF LEFT$(raw$, 1) <> "=" THEN
                OUT[r, c] = VAL(raw$)
            ELSE
                OUT[r, c] = 0
            ENDIF
        NEXT c
    NEXT r

    ' Pass 2+: iterate formulas a few times (mini-excel style)
    FOR iter = 1 TO 12
        FOR r = 0 TO ROWS - 1
            FOR c = 0 TO COLS - 1
                raw$ = TRIM$(CELLTXT$[r, c])
                IF raw$ <> "" AND LEFT$(raw$, 1) = "=" THEN
                    expr$ = MID$(raw$, 1) ' strip "="

                    ' Replace refs A10.. first, then A1.. (avoid A1 inside A10)
                    FOR rr = ROWS - 1 TO 0 STEP DECI
                        FOR cc = 0 TO COLS - 1
                            ref$ = CELLNAME$(rr, cc)
                            pat$ = "\b" + ref$ + "\b"
                            expr$ = REGEX.REPLACE(pat$, expr$, STR$(OUT[rr, cc]))
                        NEXT cc
                    NEXT rr

                    val = 0
                    TRY
                        val = EVAL("(" + expr$ + ")")
                    CATCH
                        val = 0
                    ENDTRY

                    OUT[r, c] = val
                ENDIF
            NEXT c
        NEXT r
    NEXT iter

    RETURN OUT
ENDFUNC

' --- UI -------------------------------------------------------

SCREEN 1920, 1080, "jdBasic Mini Calc (3x10)", 2
GUI.THEME "DARK"

' initial calc
Sheet = CALC_SHEET(Cell$)

FileDlg = FALSE

DO
    CLS

    WindowOpen = GUI.BEGIN("Mini Calc", 0, 0, 560, 440, WindowOpen, GUI.FLAG("MENUBAR"))

                ' --- Menu Bar ---
        IF GUI.BEGIN_MENU_BAR() THEN
            IF GUI.BEGIN_MENU("File") THEN
                IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("Load...") THEN
                    FileDlgMode$ = "LOAD"
                    FileDlgName$ = ""
                    FileDlgMsg$ = ""
                    FileDlg = TRUE
                ENDIF
                IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("Save...") THEN
                    FileDlgMode$ = "SAVE"
                    FileDlgName$ = ""
                    FileDlgMsg$ = ""
                    FileDlg = TRUE
                ENDIF
                GUI.SEPARATOR
                IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("Recalc") THEN 
                     Sheet = CALC_SHEET(Cell$)
                ENDIF
                GUI.SEPARATOR
                IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("Exit") THEN EndMe = TRUE
                GUI.END_MENU()
            ENDIF
            IF GUI.BEGIN_MENU("HELP") THEN
                IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("Show Help") THEN
                    OpenHelpMe = TRUE
                ENDIF
                GUI.END_MENU()
            ENDIF
            
            GUI.END_MENU_BAR()
        ENDIF
        IF FileDlg = TRUE THEN
            GUI.OPEN_POPUP("FileDlg")
            FileDlg = FALSE
        ENDIF
        IF GUI.BEGIN_POPUP_MODAL("FileDlg", TRUE, 0) THEN
            GUI.TEXT(FileDlgMode$ + " sheet (.jdc)")
            GUI.SEPARATOR
            FileDlgName$ = GUI.INPUT("Filename", FileDlgName$)
            GUI.TEXT("Will use: " + JDC_FILE$(FileDlgName$))
            IF FileDlgMsg$ <> "" THEN
                GUI.SEPARATOR
                GUI.TEXT(FileDlgMsg$)
            ENDIF
            GUI.SEPARATOR
            IF GUI.BUTTON("OK", 120, 0) THEN
                TRY
                    IF FileDlgMode$ = "SAVE" THEN
                        SAVE_JDC FileDlgName$, Cell$
                        FileDlgMsg$ = "Saved: " + JDC_FILE$(FileDlgName$)
                    ELSEIF FileDlgMode$ = "LOAD" THEN
                        LOAD_JDC FileDlgName$
                        FileDlgMsg$ = "Loaded: " + JDC_FILE$(FileDlgName$)
                    ENDIF
                    GUI.CLOSE_CURRENT_POPUP
                CATCH
                    PRINT ERRMSG$
                ENDTRY
            ENDIF
            GUI.SAME_LINE
            IF GUI.BUTTON("Cancel", 120, 0) THEN
                GUI.CLOSE_CURRENT_POPUP
            ENDIF
            GUI.END_POPUP()
        ENDIF

        GUI.TEXT "Click a cell to select. Edit content in the formula bar"
        GUI.SEPARATOR

        AutoRecalc = GUI.CHECKBOX("Auto recalc", AutoRecalc)
        GUI.SAME_LINE
        IF GUI.BUTTON("RECALC", 120, 0) THEN
            Sheet = CALC_SHEET(Cell$)
        ENDIF
        GUI.SAME_LINE
        IF GUI.BUTTON("Clear All", 120, 0) THEN
            FOR r = 0 TO ROWS - 1
                FOR c = 0 TO COLS - 1
                    Cell$[r, c] = ""
                NEXT c
            NEXT r
            Sheet = CALC_SHEET(Cell$)
        ENDIF

        GUI.SEPARATOR

        ' --- Formula bar (selected cell editor) ---
        GUI.TEXT "Selected: " + CELLNAME$(SelR, SelC)
        GUI.SAME_LINE
        GUI.TEXT "Value: " + FORMAT$("{:.6g}", CDBL(Sheet[SelR, SelC]))

        old$ = Cell$[SelR, SelC]
        new$ = GUI.INPUT("Formula / Value", old$)
        IF new$ <> old$ THEN
            Cell$[SelR, SelC] = new$
            IF AutoRecalc THEN
                Sheet = CALC_SHEET(Cell$)
            ENDIF
        ENDIF

        GUI.SEPARATOR

        IF GUI.BEGIN_TABLE("sheet", 4, 0) THEN
            GUI.TABLE_SETUP_COLUMN "", 0, 20 
            GUI.TABLE_SETUP_COLUMN "A", 0, 100
            GUI.TABLE_SETUP_COLUMN "B", 0, 100
            GUI.TABLE_SETUP_COLUMN "C", 0, 100
            GUI.TABLE_HEADERS_ROW

            FOR r = 0 TO ROWS - 1
                GUI.TABLE_NEXT_ROW

                GUI.TABLE_SET_COLUMN_INDEX(0)
                GUI.TEXT STR$(r+1)

                FOR c = 0 TO COLS - 1
                    GUI.TABLE_SET_COLUMN_INDEX c+1
                    disp$ = FORMAT$("{:.6g}", CDBL(Sheet[r, c]))
                    id$   = "##" + CELLNAME$(r, c)
                    label$ = disp$ + id$
                    IF GUI.SELECTABLE(label$, (r=SelR AND c=SelC), 0, 0, 0) THEN
                        SelR = r : SelC = c                    
                        IF r = SelR AND c = SelC THEN
                            rc = GUI.ITEM_RECT()
                            EditX = rc[0]
                            EditY = rc[1]
                            EditW = rc[2] - rc[0]
                            EditH = rc[3] - rc[1]
                            EditActive = TRUE
                            EditBuf$ = Cell$[SelR, SelC]     ' raw formula/value
                            NeedFocus = TRUE
                        ENDIF                    
                    ENDIF
                NEXT c
            NEXT r
            GUI.TABLE_NEXT_ROW
            GUI.TABLE_SET_COLUMN_INDEX 0
            GUI.TEXT "SUM"
            FOR c = 0 TO COLS - 1
                GUI.TABLE_SET_COLUMN_INDEX c+1
                sumA = SUM(SLICE(Sheet, 1, c))
                GUI.TEXT FORMAT$("{:.6g}", sumA)
            NEXT c
            GUI.END_TABLE
        ENDIF

        IF EditActive THEN
            GUI.SET_CURSOR_SCREEN_POS EditX, EditY
            GUI.SET_NEXT_ITEM_WIDTH EditW

            IF NeedFocus THEN
                GUI.SET_KEYBOARD_FOCUS
                NeedFocus = FALSE
            ENDIF

            ' Hidden label so it doesn't show text, unique id so no collisions
            EditBuf$ = GUI.INPUT("##cell_edit", EditBuf$)

            ' Commit immediately (simple + works everywhere)
            Cell$[SelR, SelC] = EditBuf$

            IF GUI.ITEM_DEACTIVATED_AFTER_EDIT() THEN
                EditActive = FALSE
                Sheet = CALC_SHEET(Cell$)
            ENDIF
        ENDIF


    GUI.END

    SCREENFLIP
    SLEEP 10
LOOP UNTIL EndME

END

Filename: minig.jdb
' ==========================================================
' == jdBasic Advanced Sprite & Tilemap Demo
' ==========================================================
'
' This program demonstrates:
' - Loading a Tiled map and drawing its layers.
' - Loading an animated sprite from Aseprite.
' - Player movement and animation state changes.
' - Basic camera follow.
' - Collision detection with the tilemap.
'
' --- CONTROLS ---
' - A/D or Left/Right Arrows: Move the player.
' - ESC: Quit the program.
'
' ----------------------------------------------------------

' --- 0. Evetis
ON "KEYDOWN" CALL HandleKeys
ON "KEYUP" CALL HandleKeysUP

Dim Event As Map
Event{"type"} = "NA"

SUB HandleKeys(data)
    Event  = data[0]
    Event{"type"} = "KEYDOWN"
ENDSUB

SUB HandleKeysUP(data)
    Event  = data[0]
    Event{"type"} = "KEYUP"
ENDSUB

' --- 0. Delete me
cd "D:\usr\dev\c\jdBasic\jdb"

' --- 1. Initialization ---
SCREEN 1920, 1080, "jdBasic Advanced Demo", 2
OPTION "NOPAUSE" ' Disable pause on focus loss

' --- 2. Asset Loading ---
PRINT "Loading assets..."

' Load the character animations from Aseprite
' The character will be sprite type ID 1
SPRITE.LOAD_ASEPRITE 1, "atomi.json"
SPRITE.LOAD 2, "pictures\point.png"

' Load the level map from Tiled
MAP.LOAD "level1", "tiled\jdrpg.json"
MAP.LOAD "main_menu", "main_menu.json"

SUB SortMenuItems()
    ' This uses a simple Bubble Sort to order the menu items
    ' based on the "index" custom property from Tiled.
    N = LEN(MENU_ITEMS)
    IF N < 2 THEN RETURN

    FOR I = 0 TO N - 2
        FOR J = 0 TO N - I - 2
            ' Get the index property of two adjacent items
            IndexA = VAL(MENU_ITEMS[J]{"index"})
            IndexB = VAL(MENU_ITEMS[J+1]{"index"})
            
            ' If the first is greater than the second, swap them
            IF IndexA > IndexB THEN
                TempItem = MENU_ITEMS[J]
                MENU_ITEMS[J] = MENU_ITEMS[J+1]
                MENU_ITEMS[J+1] = TempItem
            ENDIF
        NEXT
    NEXT
ENDSUB

DIM GameState AS STRING
GameState = "MainMenu"
SETFONT ".\fonts\SuperVanilla.ttf", 24
SOUND.INIT
SFX.LOAD THEME_MUSIC, "music/Theme1.wav"
MUSIC.PLAY THEME_MUSIC

DO WHILE GameState = "MainMenu"
    ' --- Menu Setup (runs once) ---
    MENU_ITEMS = MAP.GET_OBJECTS("main_menu", "menu_item")
    SortMenuItems
    CURRENT_SELECTION = 0
    SELECTOR_ID = SPRITE.CREATE(2, 0, 0)
    MUSIC.PLAY -1 
    ' --- The Actual Menu Loop ---
    DO
        ' --- Handle Menu Input ---
        IF Event{"type"} = "KEYDOWN" THEN
            ' Move selection down
            IF Event{"scancode"} = 81 OR Event{"scancode"} = 22 THEN ' S or Down Arrow
                CURRENT_SELECTION = CURRENT_SELECTION + 1
                IF CURRENT_SELECTION >= LEN(MENU_ITEMS) THEN CURRENT_SELECTION = 0
            ENDIF
            ' Move selection up
            IF Event{"scancode"} = 82 OR Event{"scancode"} = 26 THEN ' W or Up Arrow
                CURRENT_SELECTION = CURRENT_SELECTION - 1
                IF CURRENT_SELECTION < 0 THEN CURRENT_SELECTION = LEN(MENU_ITEMS) - 1
            ENDIF
            
            ' Handle selection action
            IF Event{"scancode"} = 40 THEN ' Enter key
                IF CURRENT_SELECTION = 0 THEN GameState = "Playing" : EXITDO ' Start Game
                IF CURRENT_SELECTION = 1 THEN PRINT "Load Game not implemented!"
                IF CURRENT_SELECTION = 2 THEN PRINT "Settings not implemented!"
                IF CURRENT_SELECTION = 3 THEN PRINT "Credits not implemented!"
                IF CURRENT_SELECTION = 4 THEN GameState = "Exit" : EXITDO ' Exit
            ENDIF
            
            Event{"type"} = "NA" ' Consume event
        ENDIF

        ' --- Update Menu Logic ---
        ' Position the selector sprite next to the selected text
        SELECTED_ITEM = MENU_ITEMS[CURRENT_SELECTION]
        SELECTOR_X = VAL(SELECTED_ITEM{"x"}) - 40 ' Position selector to the left
        SELECTOR_Y = VAL(SELECTED_ITEM{"y"})
        
        SPRITE.MOVE SELECTOR_ID, SELECTOR_X, SELECTOR_Y

        ' --- Drawing Phase ---
        CLS 20, 20, 40 ' Dark blue clear color
        MAP.DRAW_LAYER "main_menu", "Background"

        ' Draw all the menu text items
        FOR I = 0 TO LEN(MENU_ITEMS) - 1
            ITEM = MENU_ITEMS[I]
            TX = VAL(ITEM{"x"})
            TY = VAL(ITEM{"y"})
            ' Highlight the selected item in yellow
            IF I = CURRENT_SELECTION THEN
                TEXT TX, TY, ITEM{"text"}, 255, 255, 0
            ELSE
                TEXT TX, TY, ITEM{"text"}, 255, 255, 255
            ENDIF
        NEXT
        SPRITE.UPDATE 0
        SPRITE.DRAW_ALL 0,0' Draw the selector
        SCREENFLIP
    LOOP UNTIL 1=2

    ' --- Cleanup after menu exits ---
    SPRITE.DELETE SELECTOR_ID
LOOP ' This outer loop handles changing GameState

MUSIC.STOP

' --- 3. Game Object Creation ---
' Find the player's spawn point from the Tiled map data
IF GameState = "Playing" THEN
    SPAWN_POINTS = MAP.GET_OBJECTS("level1", "spawn_point")
    IF LEN(SPAWN_POINTS) > 0 THEN
        SPAWN = SPAWN_POINTS[0] ' Use the first spawn point found
        PLAYER_ID = SPRITE.CREATE(1, VAL(SPAWN{"x"}), VAL(SPAWN{"y"}))
    ELSE
        ' Fallback if no spawn point is found
        PLAYER_ID = SPRITE.CREATE(1, 400, 250)
    ENDIF

    ' --- 4. Game Variables ---
    PLAYER_SPEED = 75.0

    PLAYER_VX = 0.0 ' Player X velocity
    PLAYER_VY = 0.0 ' Player Y velocity

    ' Camera position
    CAM_X = 0
    CAM_Y = 0

LAST_TICK = TICK()

' --- 5. Main Game Loop ---

    DO
        ' --- Delta Time Calculation ---
        CURRENT_TICK = TICK()
        DELTA_TIME = (CURRENT_TICK - LAST_TICK) / 1000.0
        LAST_TICK = CURRENT_TICK
        
        ' --- Handle Input ---
        'EVENT = GET_EVENT() ' Get the latest event
        
        PLAYER_VX = 0
        PLAYER_VY = 0
        IF EVENT{"type"} = "KEYDOWN" THEN
            ' Move left
            IF EVENT{"scancode"} = 4 OR EVENT{"scancode"} = 80 THEN ' A or Left Arrow
                PLAYER_VX = -PLAYER_SPEED
                PLAYER_VY = 0
            ENDIF
            ' Move right
            IF EVENT{"scancode"} = 7 OR EVENT{"scancode"} = 79 THEN ' D or Right Arrow
                PLAYER_VX = PLAYER_SPEED
                PLAYER_VY = 0
            ENDIF
            ' Move up
            IF EVENT{"scancode"} = 26 OR EVENT{"scancode"} = 82 THEN ' W or Up Arrow
                    PLAYER_VY = -PLAYER_SPEED
                    PLAYER_VX = 0
            ENDIF
            ' Move down
            IF EVENT{"scancode"} = 22 OR EVENT{"scancode"} = 81 THEN ' W or Up Arrow
                    PLAYER_VY = PLAYER_SPEED
                    PLAYER_VX = 0
            ENDIF
            ' Exit
            IF EVENT{"scancode"} = 41 THEN EXITDO ' ESC key
            LAST_VX = PLAYER_VX
            LAST_VY = PLAYER_VY
        ENDIF

        ' --- Update Game Logic ---
        LAST_X = SPRITE.GET_X(PLAYER_ID)
        LAST_Y = SPRITE.GET_Y(PLAYER_ID)   

            ' Set player velocity
        SPRITE.SET_VELOCITY PLAYER_ID, PLAYER_VX, PLAYER_VY
        
        ' Update all sprite positions based on velocity
        SPRITE.UPDATE DELTA_TIME
        
        ' --- Collision Detection & Response ---
        IS_GROUNDED = FALSE
        IF MAP.COLLIDES(PLAYER_ID, "level1", "Collision") THEN
            PLAYER_VX = 0
            PLAYER_VY = 0
            SPRITE.SET_VELOCITY PLAYER_ID, 0, 0
            SPRITE.MOVE PLAYER_ID, LAST_X, LAST_Y
            SPRITE.SET_VELOCITY PLAYER_ID, PLAYER_VX, PLAYER_VY
        ENDIF

        ' --- Update Animation State ---
        IF PLAYERV_X <> 0 OR PLAYER_VY <> 0 THEN
              IF PLAYER_VY > 0 THEN
                SPRITE.SET_ANIMATION PLAYER_ID, "walk"
            ENDIF
            IF PLAYER_VY < 0 THEN 
                SPRITE.SET_ANIMATION PLAYER_ID, "backwalk"
            ENDIF
            IF PLAYERV_X < 0 THEN 
                SPRITE.SET_ANIMATION PLAYER_ID, "leftwalk"
            ENDIF
            IF PLAYERV_X > 0 THEN 
                SPRITE.SET_ANIMATION PLAYER_ID, "rightwalk"
            ENDIF
        ELSE
            IF LAST_VY > 0 THEN
                SPRITE.SET_ANIMATION PLAYER_ID, "idle"
            ENDIF
            IF LAST_VY < 0 THEN 
                SPRITE.SET_ANIMATION PLAYER_ID, "backidle"
            ENDIF
            IF LAST_VX < 0 THEN 
                SPRITE.SET_ANIMATION PLAYER_ID, "leftidle"
            ENDIF
            IF LAST_VX > 0 THEN 
                SPRITE.SET_ANIMATION PLAYER_ID, "rightidle"
            ENDIF
        ENDIF

        ' --- Update Camera ---
        ' Make the camera follow the player smoothly
        SX = SPRITE.GET_X(PLAYER_ID)
        SY = SPRITE.GET_Y(PLAYER_ID)
        CAM_X = CAM_X + (SX - CAM_X - 400) * 0.1
        CAM_Y = CAM_Y + (SY - CAM_Y - 300) * 0.1

        ' --- Drawing Phase ---
        CLS 50, 150, 255 ' Clear to a sky blue color

        ' Draw the map layers with the camera offset
        MAP.DRAW_LAYER "level1", "background", CAM_X, CAM_Y
        MAP.DRAW_LAYER "level1", "collision", CAM_X, CAM_Y
        
        ' Draw all sprites
        SPRITE.DRAW_ALL CAM_X, CAM_Y

        'MAP.DRAW_DEBUG_COLLISIONS PLAYER_ID, "level1", "collision"
        
        ' Draw UI
        TEXT 10, 10, "jdBasic RPG Demo X,y: " + str$(sx) + ", " + str$(sy), 255, 255, 0
        
        SCREENFLIP
    LOOP
ENDIF

' --- 6. Shutdown ---
PRINT "Program finished."

' --- End of File ---

Filename: mini_tensor.jdb
' ==========================================================
' == Save/Load Test Program - Complex Nested Structure
' == Goal: Isolate and debug the TENSOR.SAVEMODEL and
' ==       TENSOR.LOADMODEL functionality with a structure
' ==       that mimics the full model.
' ==========================================================

CLS
PRINT "--- Starting Save/Load Test (Complex Model) ---"
PRINT

' 1. Create a data structure that mimics the full model:
'    A main map containing a list (array) of layers, where each
'    layer is a map containing a tensor.
TEST_MODEL = {}
TEST_MODEL{"layers"} = []

' Create Layer 1
layer1 = {}
layer1_array = [[1, 2, 3], [4, 5, 6]]
layer1{"weights"} = TENSOR.FROM(layer1_array)
TEST_MODEL{"layers"} = APPEND(TEST_MODEL{"layers"}, layer1)

' Create Layer 2
layer2 = {}
layer2_array = [[10, 20, 30], [40, 50, 60]]
layer2{"weights"} = TENSOR.FROM(layer2_array)
TEST_MODEL{"layers"} = APPEND(TEST_MODEL{"layers"}, layer2)


' 2. Print the original object to see its structure
PRINT "--- ORIGINAL MODEL ---"
PRINT TEST_MODEL
PRINT


' 3. Save the model to a file
PRINT "--- SAVING MODEL ---"
TENSOR.SAVEMODEL TEST_MODEL, "test_model.json"
PRINT "Model saved to test_model.json"
PRINT


' 4. Load the model from the file into a new variable
PRINT "--- LOADING MODEL ---"
LOADED_MODEL = TENSOR.LOADMODEL("test_model.json")
IF LEN(LOADED_MODEL)=0 THEN
    PRINT "Error: Failed to load model from file."
    END
ENDIF
PRINT "Model loaded successfully."
PRINT


' 5. Print the loaded object to compare it with the original
PRINT "--- LOADED MODEL ---"
PRINT LOADED_MODEL
PRINT

' 6. Check if the loaded tensor's data can be accessed from deep
'    within the nested structure.
PRINT "--- VERIFYING LOADED DATA ---"
loaded_layer2_tensor = LOADED_MODEL{"layers"}[1]{"weights"}
loaded_array = TENSOR.TOARRAY(loaded_layer2_tensor)

' This line will cause an error if the shape information was lost during loading
PRINT "Value at (1, 2) of loaded layer 2 array: "; loaded_array[1, 2] 

PRINT
PRINT "--- Test Complete ---"

Filename: MLAB.jdb
' ==========================================================
' ==  A General-Purpose Function Library for jdBasic (MLAB)
' ==========================================================
' This module provides a collection of high-level functions
' for statistics, array manipulation, string utilities, and more,
' building upon the core features of the jdBasic language.
'
' To use these functions in another program, add the line:
' IMPORT MLAB
'
' Then call functions with: MLAB.SORT(my_array)
' ==========================================================

EXPORT MODULE MLAB

' ==========================================================
' == STATISTICS & DATA ANALYSIS
' ==========================================================

' Sorts a 1D array in ascending order.
' It uses the APL-style GRADE function, which returns the indices
' that *would* sort the array. We then use those indices to
' reorder the original array.

EXPORT FUNC SORT(A)
  RETURN A[GRADE(A)]
ENDFUNC

' Sorts a 1D array in descending order.
EXPORT FUNC SORT_DESC(A)
  ' We reverse the grade indices to get descending order.
  RETURN A[REVERSE(GRADE(A))]
ENDFUNC

' Calculates the Mean (average) of a 1D array.
EXPORT FUNC MEAN(A)
  ' The [0] is used to get the scalar length from the shape vector
  RETURN SUM(A) / LEN(A)[0]
ENDFUNC

' Calculates the Median of a 1D array.
EXPORT FUNC MEDIAN(A)
  SORTED_A = SORT(A)
  N = LEN(SORTED_A)[0]
  MID = INT(N / 2)
  IF N MOD 2 = 0 THEN
    ' Even number of elements, average the middle two
    RETURN (SORTED_A[MID - 1] + SORTED_A[MID]) / 2
  ELSE
    ' Odd number of elements, return the middle one
    RETURN SORTED_A[MID]
  ENDIF
ENDFUNC

' Creates a Map counting the occurrences of each unique item in an array.
EXPORT FUNC FREQUENCY_MAP(A)
    DIM FreqMap AS MAP
    FOR i = 0 TO LEN(A)[0] - 1
        key$ = STR$(A[i]) ' Map keys must be strings
        IF MAP.EXISTS(FreqMap, key$) THEN
            FreqMap{key$} = FreqMap{key$} + 1
        ELSE
            FreqMap{key$} = 1
        ENDIF
    NEXT i
    RETURN FreqMap
ENDFUNC

' Calculates the Mode (most common value) of a 1D array.
EXPORT FUNC MODE(A)
    FreqMap = FREQUENCY_MAP(A)
    IF LEN(MAP.KEYS(FreqMap)) = 0 THEN RETURN NULL ' Handle empty array
    
    MaxCount = 0
    ModeValue = NULL
    
    Keys = MAP.KEYS(FreqMap)
    FOR i = 0 TO LEN(Keys)[0] - 1
        key$ = Keys[i]
        Count = FreqMap{key$}
        IF Count > MaxCount THEN
            MaxCount = Count
            ModeValue = VAL(key$) ' Convert string key back to number
        ENDIF
    NEXT i
    RETURN ModeValue
ENDFUNC

' Calculates the Population Standard Deviation of a 1D array.
EXPORT FUNC STDDEV(A)
  AVG = MEAN(A)
  VAR = (A - AVG) ^ 2
  RETURN SQR(SUM(VAR) / LEN(A)[0])
ENDFUNC

' Calculates the Population Variance of a 1D array.
EXPORT FUNC VARIANCE(A)
    AVG = MEAN(A)
    VAR_SUM = SUM((A - AVG) ^ 2)
    RETURN VAR_SUM / LEN(A)[0]
ENDFUNC

' Finds the common elements between two 1D arrays.
EXPORT FUNC INTERSECT(A1, A2)
    ' Convert arrays to maps for efficient lookup
    DIM Map1 AS MAP
    FOR i = 0 TO LEN(A1)[0] - 1 : Map1{STR$(A1[i])} = TRUE : NEXT i
    
    Intersection = []
    FOR i = 0 TO LEN(A2)[0] - 1
        val = A2[i]
        IF MAP.EXISTS(Map1, STR$(val)) THEN
            ' To avoid duplicates, check if it's already in the result
            is_in_result = FALSE
            FOR j = 0 TO LEN(Intersection)[0] - 1
                IF Intersection[j] = val THEN is_in_result = TRUE : EXITFOR
            NEXT j
            IF NOT is_in_result THEN Intersection = APPEND(Intersection, val)
        ENDIF
    NEXT i
    RETURN Intersection
ENDFUNC

' ==========================================================
' == ARRAY & MATRIX CREATION
' ==========================================================

' NEW: Creates an array of a given shape filled with zeros.
EXPORT FUNC ZEROS(ShapeVector)
    RETURN RESHAPE([0], ShapeVector)
ENDFUNC

' NEW: Creates an array of a given shape filled with ones.
EXPORT FUNC ONES(ShapeVector)
    RETURN RESHAPE([1], ShapeVector)
ENDFUNC

' NEW: Creates an N x N identity matrix.
EXPORT FUNC EYE(N)
    ' Creates a boolean matrix where M[i,j] is TRUE if i=j,
    ' then multiplies by 1 to convert TRUE/FALSE to 1/0.
    RETURN OUTER(IOTA(N), IOTA(N), "=") * 1
ENDFUNC

' ==========================================================
' == COMBINATORICS & FINANCIAL
' ==========================================================

' Calculates Combinations ("n choose k").
EXPORT FUNC COMBINATIONS(n, k)
    IF k < 0 OR n < k THEN RETURN 0
    RETURN FAC(n) / (FAC(k) * FAC(n - k))
ENDFUNC

' Calculates Permutations.
EXPORT FUNC PERMUTATIONS(n, k)
    IF k < 0 OR n < k THEN RETURN 0
    RETURN FAC(n) / FAC(n - k)
ENDFUNC

' Calculates the periodic payment for a loan.
' rate: The interest rate per period.
' nper: The total number of payment periods.
' pv: The present value, or the total amount of the loan.
EXPORT FUNC PMT(rate, nper, pv)
    IF rate = 0 THEN RETURN pv / nper
    RETURN (rate * pv) / (1 - (1 + rate) ^ -nper)
ENDFUNC

' Calculates the future value of an investment.
' pmt: The payment made each period.
' pv: The present value (optional, use 0 if none).
EXPORT FUNC FV(rate, nper, pmt, pv)
    IF rate = 0 THEN RETURN pv + (pmt * nper)
    fv_pv = pv * (1 + rate) ^ nper
    fv_pmt = pmt * (((1 + rate) ^ nper - 1) / rate)
    RETURN fv_pv + fv_pmt
ENDFUNC

' ==========================================================
' == STRING UTILITIES
' ==========================================================

' Joins elements of an array into a single string with a delimiter.
EXPORT FUNC JOIN$(StringArray, Delimiter$)
    Result$ = ""
    NumElements = LEN(StringArray)[0]
    FOR i = 0 TO NumElements - 1
        Result$ = Result$ + StringArray[i]
        IF i < NumElements - 1 THEN
            Result$ = Result$ + Delimiter$
        ENDIF
    NEXT i
    RETURN Result$
ENDFUNC

' Pads a string on the left.
EXPORT FUNC PAD_LEFT$(original$, total_length, pad_char$)
  padding_needed = total_length - LEN(original$)
  IF padding_needed <= 0 THEN RETURN original$
  RETURN (pad_char$ * padding_needed) + original$
ENDFUNC

' Pads a string on the right.
EXPORT FUNC PAD_RIGHT$(original$, total_length, pad_char$)
  padding_needed = total_length - LEN(original$)
  IF padding_needed <= 0 THEN RETURN original$
  RETURN original$ + (pad_char$ * padding_needed)
ENDFUNC

' Pads a string on both sides to center it.
EXPORT FUNC PAD_CENTER$(original$, total_length, pad_char$)
    padding_needed = total_length - LEN(original$)
    IF padding_needed <= 0 THEN RETURN original$
    left_pad = INT(padding_needed / 2)
    right_pad = total_length - LEN(original$) - left_pad
    RETURN (pad_char$ * left_pad) + original$ + (pad_char$ * right_pad)
ENDFUNC

' Calculates the Levenshtein (edit) distance between two strings.
EXPORT FUNC LEVENSHTEIN(s1$, s2$)
    m = LEN(s1$)
    n = LEN(s2$)

    ' Create and initialize the distance matrix
    D = ZEROS([m + 1, n + 1])
    FOR i = 0 TO m : D[i, 0] = i : NEXT i
    FOR j = 0 TO n : D[0, j] = j : NEXT j

    ' Fill the rest of the matrix
    FOR j = 1 TO n
        FOR i = 1 TO m
            IF MID$(s1$, i, 1) = MID$(s2$, j, 1) THEN
                cost = 0
            ELSE
                cost = 1
            ENDIF
            
            ' Core calculation using the MIN reduction on a temporary array
            values = [D[i-1, j] + 1, D[i, j-1] + 1, D[i-1, j-1] + cost]
            D[i, j] = MIN(values)
        NEXT i
    NEXT j
    
    ' The final distance is in the bottom-right cell
    RETURN D[m, n]
ENDFUNC

' A simple word-wrap function.
EXPORT FUNC WORD_WRAP$(text$, max_width)
    words = SPLIT(text$, " ")
    result$ = ""
    current_line$ = ""
    FOR i = 0 TO LEN(words)[0] - 1
        word$ = words[i]
        IF LEN(current_line$ + " " + word$) > max_width THEN
            result$ = result$ + TRIM$(current_line$) + CHR$(10) ' CHR$(10) is newline
            current_line$ = word$ + " "
        ELSE
            current_line$ = current_line$ + word$ + " "
        ENDIF
    NEXT i
    result$ = result$ + TRIM$(current_line$)
    RETURN result$
ENDFUNC

' ==========================================================
' == PROCEDURAL GENERATION
' ==========================================================

' Generates a 2D array of value noise (a simple form of Perlin noise).
' width, height: Dimensions of the output noise map.
' scale: The "zoom" level of the noise. Larger scale = larger features.
' --- Helper function for smooth interpolation (Smoothstep) ---
FUNC FADE(t)
    RETURN t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10)
ENDFUNC

' ' --- Helper function for linear interpolation ---
' FUNC LERP(t, a, b)
'     RETURN a + t * (b - a)
' ENDFUNC

EXPORT FUNC PERLIN_NOISE(width, height, scale)

    ' --- Main Noise Generation ---
    ' Determine the size of the smaller random grid based on the scale
    grid_w = CEIL(width / scale) + 1
    grid_h = CEIL(height / scale) + 1

    ' Create a grid of random values between 0 and 1
    random_grid = RND(ONES([grid_h, grid_w]))

    noise_map = ZEROS([height, width])

    FOR y = 0 TO height - 1
        FOR x = 0 TO width - 1
            ' Calculate the corresponding coordinates in the random grid
            gx = x / scale
            gy = y / scale

            ' Get the integer coordinates of the grid cell's top-left corner
            g_x0 = INT(gx)
            g_y0 = INT(gy)

            ' Get the fractional coordinates within that cell (0.0 to 1.0)
            fx = gx - g_x0
            fy = gy - g_y0

            ' Apply the fade function for smoother transitions
            ufx = FADE(fx)
            ufy = FADE(fy)

            ' Get the random values from the four corners of the cell
            v00 = random_grid[g_y0, g_x0]
            v10 = random_grid[g_y0, g_x0 + 1]
            v01 = random_grid[g_y0 + 1, g_x0]
            v11 = random_grid[g_y0 + 1, g_x0 + 1]

            ' Interpolate between the corner values
            x_interp1 = LERP(ufx, v00, v10)
            x_interp2 = LERP(ufx, v01, v11)
            final_val = LERP(ufy, x_interp1, x_interp2)

            noise_map[y, x] = final_val
        NEXT x
    NEXT y

    RETURN noise_map
ENDFUNC

' Generates a perfect maze using the Recursive Backtracking algorithm.
' Returns a 2D array where 0 represents a path and 1 represents a wall.
EXPORT FUNC CREATE_MAZE(width, height)
    ' Ensure width and height are odd numbers for a proper maze structure
    w = INT(width / 2) * 2 + 1
    h = INT(height / 2) * 2 + 1

    maze = ONES([h, w])
    ' The stack will store coordinate pairs sequentially: [x1, y1, x2, y2, ...]
    stack_flat = []

    ' Start at a random odd-numbered cell
    start_x = INT(RND(1) * (w / 2)) * 2 + 1
    start_y = INT(RND(1) * (h / 2)) * 2 + 1
    
    maze[start_y, start_x] = 0 ' Mark start as path
    stack_flat = APPEND(stack_flat, [start_x, start_y])

    DO WHILE LEN(stack_flat)[0] > 0
        len_flat = LEN(stack_flat)[0]
        cx = stack_flat[len_flat - 2]
        cy = stack_flat[len_flat - 1]

        ' Find all unvisited neighbors
        neighbors_flat = []
        ' --- FIX: Use short-circuiting ANDALSO to prevent out-of-bounds access ---
        ' Check North (Y-2)
        IF cy - 2 >= 0 ANDALSO maze[cy - 2, cx] = 1 THEN neighbors_flat = APPEND(neighbors_flat, [cx, cy - 2, 0]) ' 0=N
        ' Check East (X+2)
        IF cx + 2 < w ANDALSO maze[cy, cx + 2] = 1 THEN neighbors_flat = APPEND(neighbors_flat, [cx + 2, cy, 1]) ' 1=E
        ' Check South (Y+2)
        IF cy + 2 < h ANDALSO maze[cy + 2, cx] = 1 THEN neighbors_flat = APPEND(neighbors_flat, [cx, cy + 2, 2]) ' 2=S
        ' Check West (X-2)
        IF cx - 2 >= 0 ANDALSO maze[cy, cx - 2] = 1 THEN neighbors_flat = APPEND(neighbors_flat, [cx - 2, cy, 3]) ' 3=W

        num_neighbors = LEN(neighbors_flat)[0] / 3
        IF num_neighbors > 0 THEN
            ' Manually extract the chosen neighbor from the flat array
            rand_idx = INT(RND(1) * num_neighbors)
            start_idx = rand_idx * 3
            nx = neighbors_flat[start_idx]
            ny = neighbors_flat[start_idx + 1]
            direction = neighbors_flat[start_idx + 2]
            
            ' Carve a path between the current cell and the chosen neighbor
            maze[ny, nx] = 0
            IF direction = 0 THEN maze[cy - 1, cx] = 0 ' Path North
            IF direction = 1 THEN maze[cy, cx + 1] = 0 ' Path East
            IF direction = 2 THEN maze[cy + 1, cx] = 0 ' Path South
            IF direction = 3 THEN maze[cy, cx - 1] = 0 ' Path West

            ' Push the new cell coordinates to the flat stack
            stack_flat = APPEND(stack_flat, [nx, ny])
        ELSE
            ' Backtrack by dropping the last coordinate pair
            stack_flat = TAKE(len_flat - 2, stack_flat)
        ENDIF
    LOOP

    RETURN maze
ENDFUNC

Filename: mouse_event.jdb
' ===============================================
' == jdBasic Rainbow Mouse Drawing Demo (Event-Driven v2)
' ===============================================
'
' This version is fully event-driven and stores all drawn
' lines in an array, redrawing them each frame.
'
' --- CONTROLS ---
' - Hold the LEFT mouse button to draw.
' - Press the ESC key to quit.
'
' -----------------------------------------------

' --- 1. Global State Variables ---
DIM is_drawing AS BOOLEAN
DIM prev_x AS INTEGER
DIM prev_y AS INTEGER
DIM hue_counter AS INTEGER
DIM G_QUIT AS BOOLEAN ' Flag to signal program exit

' --- 2. Event Handler Subroutines ---
SUB OnMouseDown(data)
    IF data[0]{"button"} = 1 THEN
        is_drawing = TRUE
        prev_x = data[0]{"x"}
        prev_y = data[0]{"y"}
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

SUB OnMouseUp(data)
    is_drawing = FALSE
    prev_x = -1
    prev_y = -1
ENDSUB

SUB OnMouseMove(data)
    IF is_drawing THEN
        mx = data[0]{"x"}
        my = data[0]{"y"}

        color_array = HSVtoRGB(hue_counter, 1.0, 1.0)
        
        ' Create a new line segment array: [x1, y1, x2, y2, r, g, b]
        new_line = [prev_x, prev_y, mx, my]
        new_color = [color_array[0], color_array[1], color_array[2]]
        
        ' Add the new line segment to our global list of lines
        G_LINES = APPEND(G_LINES, new_line)
        G_COLORS = APPEND(G_COLORS, new_color)

        prev_x = mx
        prev_y = my
        hue_counter = (hue_counter + 2) MOD 360
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

' --- NEW: Event handler for keyboard input ---
SUB OnKeyDown(data)
    ' Check if the pressed key is ESC (ASCII 27)
    PRINT data
    IF data[0]{"keycode"} = 27 THEN
        G_QUIT = TRUE
    ENDIF
ENDSUB


' --- 3. Helper Function: HSV to RGB Color Conversion ---
FUNC HSVtoRGB(h, s, v)
    c = v * s
    h_prime = h / 60.0
    x = c * (1 - ABS(h_prime MOD 2 - 1))
    m = v - c

    IF h_prime >= 0 AND h_prime < 1 THEN
        r_ = c : g_ = x : b_ = 0
    ELSEIF h_prime >= 1 AND h_prime < 2 THEN
        r_ = x : g_ = c : b_ = 0
    ELSEIF h_prime >= 2 AND h_prime < 3 THEN
        r_ = 0 : g_ = c : b_ = x
    ELSEIF h_prime >= 3 AND h_prime < 4 THEN
        r_ = 0 : g_ = x : b_ = c
    ELSEIF h_prime >= 4 AND h_prime < 5 THEN
        r_ = x : g_ = 0 : b_ = c
    ELSEIF h_prime >= 5 AND h_prime < 6 THEN
        r_ = c : g_ = 0 : b_ = x
    ELSE
        r_ = 0 : g_ = 0 : b_ = 0
    ENDIF

    r = (r_ + m) * 255
    g = (g_ + m) * 255
    b = (b_ + m) * 255

    RETURN [r, g, b]
ENDFUNC


' --- 4. Main Program Setup and Loop ---
SCREEN 800, 600, "Rainbow Paint (Event-Driven)"

' Initialize global state variables
is_drawing = FALSE
prev_x = -1
prev_y = -1
hue_counter = 0
G_LINES = []
G_COLORS = []
G_QUIT = FALSE

' Register ALL event handlers
ON "MOUSEDOWN" CALL OnMouseDown
ON "MOUSEUP" CALL OnMouseUp
ON "MOUSEMOVE" CALL OnMouseMove
ON "KEYDOWN" CALL OnKeyDown

PRINT "--- Starting Drawing App. Hold left-click to draw. Press ESC to quit. ---"

' The main loop now handles redrawing the entire scene from the G_LINES array.
DO

    if len(G_LINES) > 1 THEN
       LINE RESHAPE(G_LINES,[len(G_LINES)[0]/4,4]), RESHAPE(G_COLORS,[len(G_COLORS)[0]/3,3])
    ENDIF
    ' Update the screen
    SCREENFLIP
    
    ' Check the quit flag, which is set by the OnKeyDown handler
    IF G_QUIT THEN EXITDO
    if is_drawing = FALSE THEN 
        SLEEP 10 
    ENDIF
LOOP

' --- 5. Shutdown ---
CLS
PRINT "Drawing app closed."

' --- End of File ---

Filename: mouse_test.jdb
' ===============================================
' == jdBASIC Mouse Drawing Demo
' ===============================================
'
' This program demonstrates the MOUSEX, MOUSEY,
' and MOUSEB functions.
'
' --- CONTROLS ---
' - Move the mouse to position the cursor.
' - Hold the LEFT mouse button (1) to draw.
' - Hold the RIGHT mouse button (3) to erase.
' - Press the ESC key to quit.
'
' -----------------------------------------------

SCREEN 800, 600, "jdBasic Mouse Drawing"

' Main drawing loop
DO
  ' Get mouse coordinates
  CLS 20, 20, 30 ' Clear to a dark background
  TEXT 10, 10, "Left-click and drag to draw. Right-click to erase. ESC to quit.", 200, 200, 200
  MX = MOUSEX()
  MY = MOUSEY()

  ' Check if the left mouse button is held down
  IF MOUSEB(1) THEN
    ' Draw a small circle at the mouse position
    CIRCLE MX, MY, 3, 255, 200, 100
  ENDIF

  ' Check if the right mouse button is held down
  IF MOUSEB(3) THEN
    ' Erase by drawing a larger black circle
    CIRCLE MX, MY, 10, 20, 20, 30 ' Use the background color
  ENDIF

  ' Redraw the screen to show any new drawing
  SCREENFLIP
  
  ' Use INKEY$ to check for the ESC key (ASCII 27)
  KEY$ = INKEY$()
  IF KEY$ <> "" AND ASC(KEY$) = 27 THEN
    EXITDO
  ENDIF

  ' Small delay to prevent 100% CPU usage
  SLEEP 10
LOOP

Filename: msaccess.jdb
REM --- jdBasic MS Access SQL Executor ---
REM This program prompts for a database path and an SQL query,
REM executes the query, and displays the results.

DIM data = []
DIM fields = []


CLS
PRINT "--- jdBasic MS Access SQL Executor ---"
PRINT
PRINT "NOTE: Requires the 64-bit 'Microsoft Access Database Engine' driver."
PRINT

' --- 1. Get User Input ---
DIM db_path$
' INPUT "Enter path to Access DB (e.g., C:\Data\Orders.accdb): ", db_path$

db_path$ = "U:\v-m-s\Geldlogistik\Geldlogistik.accdb"

IF db_path$ = "" THEN
    PRINT "No database path provided. Exiting."
    END
ENDIF

PRINT
PRINT "Enter your SQL query (end with a blank line):"
DIM sql_query$
sql_query$ = ""

INPUT "> "; line$
sql_query$ = line$

IF TRIM$(sql_query$) = "" THEN
    PRINT "No SQL query provided. Exiting."
    END
ENDIF

PRINT
PRINT "Executing..."

' --- 2. Execute the Query ---
' Call our helper function and get the result
ExecuteSQL sql_query$

' --- 3. Display the Result ---
PRINT "----------------------------------------"

SWITCH TYPEOF(data)
    CASE "ARRAY"
        ' This was a SELECT query that returned data
        PRINT "--- Query Successful (SELECT) ---"
        IF LEN(data) = 0 THEN
            PRINT "(Query returned 0 rows)"
        ELSE
            PRINT "Found " + LEN(data) + " rows:"
            ' FRMV$ is the best way to print an array of maps
            PRINT FRMV$(data)
        ENDIF
        
    CASE "MAP"
        ' This was an action query (INSERT/UPDATE) or an error
        IF MAP.EXISTS(data, "status") AND data{"status"} = "OK" THEN
            PRINT "--- Query Successful (Action) ---"
            PRINT "Records Affected: " + result{"records_affected"}
        ELSEIF MAP.EXISTS(data, "status") AND data{"status"} = "Error"
            PRINT "--- Query Failed ---"
            PRINT "Line: " + data{"line"}
            PRINT "Error: " + data{"message"}
        ELSE
            PRINT "--- Unknown Result ---"
            PRINT data
        ENDIF
        
    DEFAULT
        PRINT "--- Unknown Error ---"
        PRINT "Received unexpected result type: " + TYPEOF(data)
ENDSWITCH
PRINT "----------------------------------------"

SUB ExecuteSQL(sql_query$)
    DIM conn 
    DIM rs 
    DIM conn_str$
    
    TRY
        ' 1. Create COM objects
        conn = CREATEOBJECT("ADODB.Connection")
        conn_str$ = "Provider=Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0;Data Source=" + db_path$
        
        ' 2. Open the connection
        conn.Open(conn_str$)

        ' 3. Check if it's a SELECT or an Action query
        IF UCASE$(LEFT$(TRIM$(sql_query$), 6)) = "SELECT" THEN
            ' --- This is a SELECT query ---
            rs = CREATEOBJECT("ADODB.Recordset")
            rs.Open(sql_query$, conn)

            IF rs.EOF THEN
                ' Valid query, but no results
                data = []
            ENDIF

            ' Get field names (headers) from the recordset
            fields = []
            FOR i = 0 TO rs.Fields.Count - 1
                fields = APPEND(fields, rs.Fields(i).Name)
            NEXT i

            ' Loop through records 
            data = []
            rows = 0
            DO WHILE NOT rs.EOF
                row_map = []
                FOR i = 0 TO LEN(fields) - 1
                    row_map = APPEND(row_map, rs.Fields(fields[i]).Value)
                NEXT i
                ' Add the completed row map to our result array
                data = APPEND(data, row_map)
                rows = rows + 1
                rs.MoveNext()
            LOOP
            data = reshape(data,[rows,rs.Fields.Count])

            
        ELSE
            ' --- This is an Action query (INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE) ---
            DIM records_affected
            ' conn.Execute returns the number of records affected
            conn.Execute(sql_query$, records_affected)
            
            ' For action queries, we'll return a status map
            data = {"status": "OK", "records_affected": records_affected}
        ENDIF

    CATCH
        ' If anything fails, return an error map
        DIM error_map AS MAP
        error_map{"status"} = "Error"
        error_map{"line"} = ERL
        error_map{"message"} = ERRMSG$
        data = error_map
        
    FINALLY
        ' This block *always* runs, ensuring we clean up COM objects
        ' to prevent file locking and memory leaks.
        
        ' Check if the object was created before trying to close it
        IF TYPEOF(rs) = "COMOBJECT" THEN
            IF rs.State = 1 THEN rs.Close() ' 1 = adStateOpen
        ENDIF
        IF TYPEOF(conn) = "COMOBJECT" THEN
            IF conn.State = 1 THEN conn.Close()
        ENDIF
        
        ' Release the objects
        rs = NULL
        conn = NULL
    ENDTRY
ENDSUB

Print 

'Print "'--- This will cause a shape mismatch error, as expected ---"
'PRINT A + V 

PRINT "--- Numeric Reductions ---"
V = [2, 3, 4, 5, 6]
PRINT "Vector is "; V
PRINT "SUM: "; SUM(V)         ' Should be 20
PRINT "PRODUCT: "; PRODUCT(V) ' Should be 720
PRINT "MIN: "; MIN(V)         ' Should be 2
PRINT "MAX: "; MAX(V)         ' Should be 6
PRINT ""

PRINT "--- Boolean Reductions ---"
B1 = [TRUE, TRUE, TRUE]
B2 = [TRUE, FALSE, TRUE]
PRINT "B1 is "; B1
PRINT "ANY(B1): "; ANY(B1)     ' Should be TRUE
PRINT "ALL(B1): "; ALL(B1)     ' Should be TRUE
PRINT ""
PRINT "B2 is "; B2
PRINT "ANY(B2): "; ANY(B2)     ' Should be TRUE
PRINT "ALL(B2): "; ALL(B2)     ' Should be FALSE
PRINT ""

PRINT "--- Reductions work on matrices too! ---"
M = RESHAPE(IOTA(9), [3,3])
PRINT "Matrix M:"; M
PRINT "SUM of M: "; SUM(M)     ' Should be 45
Print

PRINT "--- Testing MATMUL ---"
A = [[1, 2], [3, 4]]
B = [[5, 6], [7, 8]]
C = MATMUL(A, B)
PRINT "Matrix A:"; A
PRINT "Matrix B:"; B
PRINT "MATMUL(A, B) ="; C  ' Should be [[19, 22], [43, 50]]
PRINT ""

PRINT "--- Testing OUTER ---"
V1 = [10, 20]
V2 = [1, 2, 3]
PRINT "Vector V1: "; V1
PRINT "Vector V2: "; V2
M_MULT = OUTER(V1, V2, "*")
PRINT "OUTER(V1, V2, *) ="; M_MULT

M_ADD = OUTER(V1, V2, "+")
PRINT "OUTER(V1, V2, +) ="; M_ADD
Print

Print "--- Slicing and Sorting ---"
Print

V = IOTA(8)
PRINT "Original Vector V: "; V
PRINT ""
PRINT "TAKE(3, V) = "; TAKE(3, V)     ' Should be [1 2 3]
PRINT "TAKE(-3, V) = "; TAKE(-3, V)   ' Should be [6 7 8]
PRINT ""
PRINT "DROP(3, V) = "; DROP(3, V)     ' Should be [4 5 6 7 8]
PRINT "DROP(-3, V) = "; DROP(-3, V)   ' Should be [1 2 3 4 5]
Print

' Create an unsorted array of scores
SCORES = [98, 75, 100, 82, 91]
PRINT "Original Scores: "; SCORES
PRINT ""

' Get the indices that WOULD sort the scores
G = GRADE(SCORES)
PRINT "Grade of Scores: "; G  ' Should be [1 3 4 0 2] (indices of 75, 82, 91, 98, 100)
PRINT ""

' Now, use the grade vector to print the scores in sorted order
PRINT "Scores sorted by rank:"
FOR I = 0 TO LEN(G)-1
    PRINT SCORES[G[I]]
NEXT I
