Filename: serial.jdb
' =========================================================
' jdBasic Serial Client
' =========================================================

' Configuration
Port$ = "COM4"    ' Change this to your actual port
BaudRate = 9600   ' Must match the Arduino sketch

PRINT "Attempting to open " + Port$ + " at " + BaudRate + " baud..."

' 1. Open the Serial Port
hCom = SERIAL.OPEN(Port$, BaudRate)

' Check if connection was successful
IF TypeOf(hCom) <> "SERIAL_PORT" THEN
    PRINT "Error: Could not open serial port."
    STOP
ENDIF

PRINT "Connected successfully!"
PRINT "Type 'on', 'off', 'tempon', 'tempoff', 'status', or 'quit'."
PRINT "---------------------------------------"

' Main Loop
DO
    STARTDO:

    ' 2. Get User Input
    INPUT "Command> ", userInput$
    userInput$ = UCASE$(TRIM$(userInput$))

    IF userInput$ = "QUIT" THEN 
        EXITDO
    ENDIF

    ' 3. Prepare Protocol Command
    ' We map user words to the specific protocol the Arduino expects
    cmdToSend$ = ""
    
    SWITCH userInput$
        CASE "ON"
            cmdToSend$ = "LED_ON"
        CASE "OFF"
            cmdToSend$ = "LED_OFF"
        CASE "STATUS"
            cmdToSend$ = "STATUS"
        CASE "TEMPON"
            cmdToSend$ = "POLLON"
        CASE "TEMPOFF"
            cmdToSend$ = "POLLOFF"
        DEFAULT
            PRINT "Invalid command. Try 'on', 'off', or 'status'."
            GOTO STARTDO
    ENDSWITCH

    ' 4. Send Command
    ' We append CHR$(10) (Newline) because Arduino uses readStringUntil('\n')
    SERIAL.WRITE hCom, cmdToSend$ + CHR$(10)
    PRINT "-> Sent: " + cmdToSend$

    ' 5. Wait for Response
    ' Give the Arduino a moment to process (simple polling approach)
    SLEEP 100 

    ' 6. Read Response
    bytesWaiting = SERIAL.AVAILABLE(hCom)
    
    IF bytesWaiting > 0 THEN
        ' Read the data currently in the buffer
        response$ = SERIAL.READ$(hCom, bytesWaiting)
        PRINT "<- Received: " + TRIM$(response$)
    ELSE
        PRINT "<- (No response)"
    ENDIF
    
    PRINT "" 
LOOP UNTIL 1=2

' 7. Clean up
PRINT "Closing connection..."
SERIAL.CLOSE hCom
PRINT "Done."
' --- End of File ---

Filename: simpler.jdb
'================================================================
' NeReLaBasic (jdBasic) ASYNC/AWAIT Demonstration
' This program shows how to run tasks in the background
' and wait for their results without blocking the main program.
' ================================================================

PRINT "--- Starting ASYNC/AWAIT Demonstration ---"
PRINT

' ================================================================
'          A S Y N C   F U N C T I O N   D E F I N I T I O N S
' ================================================================

' This function simulates a "download" that takes some time.
ASYNC FUNC DOWNLOADFILE(url$, duration)
  PRINT "  [Task 1] Starting download from "; url$
  ' Simulate work by looping
  FOR i = 1 TO duration
    PRINT "  [Task 1] ... downloading chunk "; i; " of "; duration; " ..."
  NEXT i
  PRINT "  [Task 1] Download finished."
  RETURN "Download of " + url$ + " successful."
ENDFUNC

' This function simulates a "data processing" job.
ASYNC FUNC PROCESSDATA(dataset$, duration)
  PRINT "    [Task 2] Starting to process data from "; dataset$
  ' Simulate work by looping
  FOR i = 1 TO duration
    PRINT "    [Task 2] ... processing record block "; i; " of "; duration; " ..."
  NEXT i
  PRINT "    [Task 2] Data processing finished."
  RETURN "Processed " + dataset$ + " and found 42 insights."
ENDFUNC

' --- Step 1: Start the asynchronous tasks ---
' We call two ASYNC functions. This creates two new tasks that
' start running in the background. The calls return immediately
' with a "Task Reference" handle, which we store in variables.
PRINT "Main: Kicking off background tasks..."
task1 = DOWNLOADFILE("https://example.com/data.zip", 5)
task2 = PROCESSDATA("some_large_dataset.csv", 3)
PRINT "Main: Background tasks have been started."
PRINT

' --- Step 2: Main program continues its own work ---
' While the background tasks are running, the main program is not
' blocked. It can continue to execute other commands.
PRINT "Main: Now doing other work while tasks run in the background."
FOR i = 1 TO 4
  PRINT "Main: ... processing main task step "; i; " ..."
  ' In a real program, you could do other things here,
  ' like updating the UI or handling user input.
NEXT i
PRINT "Main: Finished with other work."
PRINT

' --- Step 3: Wait for the tasks to complete and get results ---
' Now we use AWAIT. This will pause the main program until the
' specified task is finished. It then returns the value from
' the task's RETURN statement.

PRINT "Main: Now waiting for Task 1 to complete..."
result1 = AWAIT task1
PRINT "Main: Task 1 finished with result: '"; result1; "'"
PRINT

PRINT "Main: Now waiting for Task 2 to complete..."
result2 = AWAIT task2
PRINT "Main: Task 2 finished with result: '"; result2; "'"
PRINT

PRINT "--- Demonstration Complete ---"



' --- End of File ---

Filename: sin3_vec.jdb
' jdBasic Sine Wave Plot
' This program demonstrates how to plot multiple sine waves with different
' properties using vectorized operations. It also includes simple axes.
' This version uses a single draw call with a line and color matrix.

' -------------------------------------------------
' 1. SETUP & INITIALIZATION
' -------------------------------------------------
SCREEN_W = 1920
SCREEN_H = 1080

' --- Graphing Parameters ---
NUM_POINTS = 1000 ' Number of points to calculate. More points = smoother curve.
NUM_SEGMENTS = NUM_POINTS - 1

' --- Wave 1 Parameters (Red) ---
AMPLITUDE1 = 400
FREQUENCY1 = 4
COLOR1 = [255, 50, 50]

' --- Wave 2 Parameters (Green) ---
AMPLITUDE2 = 250
FREQUENCY2 = 8
COLOR2 = [50, 255, 50]

' --- Wave 3 Parameters (Blue) ---
AMPLITUDE3 = 150
FREQUENCY3 = 2
COLOR3 = [100, 100, 255]

' --- Initialize Graphics ---
PRINT "Initializing graphics screen..."
SCREEN SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H, "Vectorized Sine Waves"
CLS 0, 0, 0 ' Clear screen to black

' -------------------------------------------------
' 2. GENERATE COORDINATE VECTORS
' -------------------------------------------------
' --- Create the base X-axis vector ---
' IOTA(NUM_POINTS) creates a vector [0, 1, 2, ..., NUM_POINTS-1]
x_axis = IOTA(NUM_POINTS)

' --- Scale the X-axis to fit the screen width ---
' This creates the pixel coordinates for the x-axis.
x_coords = x_axis * (SCREEN_W / (NUM_POINTS - 1))

' --- Animation Loop Variables ---
time_step = 0

' --- Create a dummy vector for building color matrices ---
dummy_vec = RESHAPE([1], [NUM_SEGMENTS])

' -------------------------------------------------
' 3. MAIN ANIMATION LOOP
' -------------------------------------------------
PRINT "Starting animation loop... Press any key to exit."
DO
    ' --- Clear the screen for the new frame ---
    CLS 0, 0, 0

    ' --- Draw Axes ---
    ' Draw the X-axis (horizontal line in the middle)
    LINE 0, SCREEN_H / 2, SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H / 2, 100, 100, 100
    ' Draw the Y-axis (vertical line near the left)
    LINE 20, 0, 20, SCREEN_H, 100, 100, 100

    ' --- Calculate Y Coordinates for all waves ---
    angle1 = (x_axis / NUM_POINTS) * FREQUENCY1 * 2 * PI + time_step
    y_coords1 = (SIN(angle1) * AMPLITUDE1) + (SCREEN_H / 2)

    angle2 = (x_axis / NUM_POINTS) * FREQUENCY2 * 2 * PI + time_step * 1.5
    y_coords2 = (SIN(angle2) * AMPLITUDE2) + (SCREEN_H / 2)

    angle3 = (x_axis / NUM_POINTS) * FREQUENCY3 * 2 * PI + time_step * 0.5
    y_coords3 = (SIN(angle3) * AMPLITUDE3) + (SCREEN_H / 2)

    ' --- Create line segment matrices for each wave ---
    x1_vec = SLICE(x_coords, 0, 0, NUM_SEGMENTS)
    x2_vec = SLICE(x_coords, 0, 1, NUM_SEGMENTS)

    y1_vec1 = SLICE(y_coords1, 0, 0, NUM_SEGMENTS)
    y2_vec1 = SLICE(y_coords1, 0, 1, NUM_SEGMENTS)
    lines1 = STACK(1, x1_vec, y1_vec1, x2_vec, y2_vec1)

    y1_vec2 = SLICE(y_coords2, 0, 0, NUM_SEGMENTS)
    y2_vec2 = SLICE(y_coords2, 0, 1, NUM_SEGMENTS)
    lines2 = STACK(1, x1_vec, y1_vec2, x2_vec, y2_vec2)

    y1_vec3 = SLICE(y_coords3, 0, 0, NUM_SEGMENTS)
    y2_vec3 = SLICE(y_coords3, 0, 1, NUM_SEGMENTS)
    lines3 = STACK(1, x1_vec, y1_vec3, x2_vec, y2_vec3)

    ' --- Create color matrices for each wave ---
    ' Each matrix will have NUM_SEGMENTS rows, with each row being the wave's color.
    colors1 = STACK(1, dummy_vec * COLOR1[0], dummy_vec * COLOR1[1], dummy_vec * COLOR1[2])
    colors2 = STACK(1, dummy_vec * COLOR2[0], dummy_vec * COLOR2[1], dummy_vec * COLOR2[2])
    colors3 = STACK(1, dummy_vec * COLOR3[0], dummy_vec * COLOR3[1], dummy_vec * COLOR3[2])

    ' --- Draw all lines ---

    LINE lines1, colors1
    LINE lines2, colors2
    LINE lines3, colors3

    ' --- Update the screen and advance the animation ---
    SCREENFLIP
    time_step = time_step + 0.05
    SLEEP 16 ' Aim for ~60 FPS

    ' --- Check for user input to exit the loop ---
    a$ = INKEY$()
LOOP UNTIL a$ > ""

' --- Clean up ---
PRINT "Animation finished."

' --- End of File ---

Filename: sin_graphic.jdb
' jdBasic Sine Wave Plot
' This program demonstrates how to plot multiple sine waves with different
' properties using vectorized operations. It also includes simple axes.

' -------------------------------------------------
' 1. SETUP & INITIALIZATION
' -------------------------------------------------
SCREEN_W = 1920
SCREEN_H = 1080

' --- Graphing Parameters ---
NUM_POINTS = 1000 ' Number of points to calculate. More points = smoother curve.

' --- Wave 1 Parameters (Red) ---
AMPLITUDE1 = 400
FREQUENCY1 = 4
COLOR1 = [255, 50, 50]

' --- Wave 2 Parameters (Green) ---
AMPLITUDE2 = 250
FREQUENCY2 = 8
COLOR2 = [50, 255, 50]

' --- Wave 3 Parameters (Blue) ---
AMPLITUDE3 = 150
FREQUENCY3 = 2
COLOR3 = [100, 100, 255]

' --- Axes Color ---
AXIS_COLOR = [100, 100, 100] ' Grey

' --- Initialize Graphics ---
PRINT "Initializing graphics screen..."
SCREEN SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H, "Vectorized Sine Waves"
CLS 0, 0, 0 ' Clear screen to black

' -------------------------------------------------
' 2. GENERATE COORDINATE VECTORS
' -------------------------------------------------
' --- Create the base X-axis vector ---
' IOTA(NUM_POINTS) creates a vector [0, 1, 2, ..., NUM_POINTS-1]
x_axis = IOTA(NUM_POINTS)

' --- Scale the X-axis to fit the screen width ---
' This creates the pixel coordinates for the x-axis.
x_coords = x_axis * (SCREEN_W / (NUM_POINTS - 1))

' --- Animation Loop Variables ---
time_step = 0

' -------------------------------------------------
' 3. MAIN ANIMATION LOOP
' -------------------------------------------------
PRINT "Starting animation loop... Press any key to exit."
DO
    ' --- Clear the screen for the new frame ---
    CLS 0, 0, 0

    ' --- Prepare and Draw Wave 1 (Red) ---
    angle1 = (x_axis / NUM_POINTS) * FREQUENCY1 * 2 * PI + time_step
    y_wave1 = SIN(angle1)
    y_coords1 = (y_wave1 * AMPLITUDE1) + (SCREEN_H / 2)
    x1_vec1 = SLICE(x_coords, 0, 0, NUM_POINTS - 1)
    y1_vec1 = SLICE(y_coords1, 0, 0, NUM_POINTS - 1)
    x2_vec1 = SLICE(x_coords, 0, 1, NUM_POINTS - 1)
    y2_vec1 = SLICE(y_coords1, 0, 1, NUM_POINTS - 1)
    lines_to_draw1 = STACK(1, x1_vec1, y1_vec1, x2_vec1, y2_vec1)
    DRAWCOLOR COLOR1
    LINE lines_to_draw1

    ' --- Prepare and Draw Wave 2 (Green) ---
    angle2 = (x_axis / NUM_POINTS) * FREQUENCY2 * 2 * PI + time_step * 1.5
    y_wave2 = SIN(angle2)
    y_coords2 = (y_wave2 * AMPLITUDE2) + (SCREEN_H / 2)
    x1_vec2 = SLICE(x_coords, 0, 0, NUM_POINTS - 1)
    y1_vec2 = SLICE(y_coords2, 0, 0, NUM_POINTS - 1)
    x2_vec2 = SLICE(x_coords, 0, 1, NUM_POINTS - 1)
    y2_vec2 = SLICE(y_coords2, 0, 1, NUM_POINTS - 1)
    lines_to_draw2 = STACK(1, x1_vec2, y1_vec2, x2_vec2, y2_vec2)
    DRAWCOLOR COLOR2
    LINE lines_to_draw2

    ' --- Prepare and Draw Wave 3 (Blue) ---
    angle3 = (x_axis / NUM_POINTS) * FREQUENCY3 * 2 * PI + time_step * 0.5
    y_wave3 = SIN(angle3)
    y_coords3 = (y_wave3 * AMPLITUDE3) + (SCREEN_H / 2)
    x1_vec3 = SLICE(x_coords, 0, 0, NUM_POINTS - 1)
    y1_vec3 = SLICE(y_coords3, 0, 0, NUM_POINTS - 1)
    x2_vec3 = SLICE(x_coords, 0, 1, NUM_POINTS - 1)
    y2_vec3 = SLICE(y_coords3, 0, 1, NUM_POINTS - 1)
    lines_to_draw3 = STACK(1, x1_vec3, y1_vec3, x2_vec3, y2_vec3)
    DRAWCOLOR COLOR3
    LINE lines_to_draw3

    ' --- Draw Axes ---
    ' Draw the X-axis (horizontal line in the middle)
    DRAWCOLOR AXIS_COLOR
    LINE 0, SCREEN_H / 2, SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H / 2
    ' Draw the Y-axis (vertical line near the left)
    LINE 20, 0, 20, SCREEN_H

    ' --- Update the screen and advance the animation ---
    SCREENFLIP
    time_step = time_step + 0.05
    SLEEP 16 ' Aim for ~60 FPS

    ' --- Check for user input to exit the loop ---
    a$ = INKEY$()
LOOP UNTIL a$ > ""

' --- Clean up ---
PRINT "Animation finished."

' --- End of File ---

Filename: snake_02.jdb
' ==========================================================
' == SNAKE.JDB - Classic Snake Clone
' ==========================================================

' --- Configuration ---
WIDTH = 40
HEIGHT = 20
SPEED = 100 ' Delay in ms

' --- Globals ---
DIM SnakeX, SnakeY ' Arrays to hold body coordinates
FoodX = 0
FoodY = 0
DirX = 1
DirY = 0
Score = 0
GameOver = FALSE
Paused = FALSE

DIM K$ AS STRING
K$ = ""

' Callback for Keyboard Input
SUB HandleKeys(data)
    K$ = chr$(data[0]{"scancode"})
ENDSUB

ON "KEYDOWN" CALL HandleKeys

' --- Initialization ---
SUB InitGame()
    ' Reverse order so [Tail, Body, Head]
    ' Index 0 is Tail, Last Index is Head
    SnakeX = [INT(WIDTH/2)-2, INT(WIDTH/2)-1, INT(WIDTH/2)]
    SnakeY = [INT(HEIGHT/2), INT(HEIGHT/2), INT(HEIGHT/2)]
    DirX = 1
    DirY = 0
    Score = 0
    GameOver = FALSE

    CURSOR FALSE
  
    DrawBorder()
    PlaceFood()
ENDSUB

SUB DrawBorder()
    CLS
    COLOR 7, 0
    PRINT "+" + ("-" * WIDTH) + "+"
    FOR y = 1 TO HEIGHT
        PRINT "|" + (" " * WIDTH) + "|"
    NEXT y
    PRINT "+" + ("-" * WIDTH) + "+"
    LOCATE HEIGHT + 3, 1
    PRINT "WASD / Arrows to Move. Q to Quit."
ENDSUB

SUB PlaceFood()
    ' Simple random placement
    FoodX = INT(RND(1) * WIDTH) + 1
    FoodY = INT(RND(1) * HEIGHT) + 1
    
    LOCATE FoodY + 1, FoodX + 1
    COLOR 12, 0 ' Red Food
    PRINT "@";
ENDSUB

' --- Main Loop ---
InitGame()

DO
    ' 1. Input Handling (Keyboard + Joystick)
    if K$ > "" then
        k_lower$ = LCASE$(k$)
    Endif

    ' Keyboard
    IF k_lower$ = "w" AND DirY = 0 THEN 
        DirX = 0 : DirY = -1
    ELSEIF k_lower$ = "s" AND DirY = 0 THEN 
        DirX = 0 : DirY = 1
    ELSEIF k_lower$ = "a" AND DirX = 0 THEN 
        DirX = -1 : DirY = 0
    ELSEIF k_lower$ = "d" AND DirX = 0 THEN 
        DirX = 1 : DirY = 0
    ELSEIF k_lower$ = "q" THEN 
        EXITDO
    ENDIF

    ' Joystick
    IF JOY.COUNT() > 0 THEN
        joyID = 0
        Hat = JOY.HAT(joyID, 0)
         
        ' D-Pad logic preventing 180 turns
        IF (Hat BAND 1) AND DirY = 0 THEN DirX = 0 : DirY = -1
        IF (Hat BAND 4) AND DirY = 0 THEN DirX = 0 : DirY = 1
        IF (Hat BAND 8) AND DirX = 0 THEN DirX = -1 : DirY = 0
        IF (Hat BAND 2) AND DirX = 0 THEN DirX = 1 : DirY = 0
    ENDIF

    ' 2. Logic
    IF NOT GameOver THEN
        Dims = LEN(SnakeX)
        Count = Dims[0]
        
        ' FIX: Head is now at the END of the array
        HeadIdx = Count - 1
        CurrHeadX = SnakeX[HeadIdx]
        CurrHeadY = SnakeY[HeadIdx]

        ' Calculate new Head
        NewHeadX = CurrHeadX + DirX
        NewHeadY = CurrHeadY + DirY

        ' Collision: Walls
        IF NewHeadX < 1 OR NewHeadX > WIDTH OR NewHeadY < 1 OR NewHeadY > HEIGHT THEN
            GameOver = TRUE
        ENDIF

        ' Collision: Self
        ' Check all body parts (0 to HeadIdx - 1)
        FOR i = 0 TO HeadIdx - 1
            IF SnakeX[i] = NewHeadX AND SnakeY[i] = NewHeadY THEN GameOver = TRUE
        NEXT i

        IF NOT GameOver THEN
            ' FIX: Append new head to the END
            SnakeX = APPEND(SnakeX, NewHeadX)
            SnakeY = APPEND(SnakeY, NewHeadY)

            ' Draw Head
            LOCATE NewHeadY + 1, NewHeadX + 1
            COLOR 10, 0 ' Green Snake
            PRINT "O";

            ' Check Food
            IF NewHeadX = FoodX AND NewHeadY = FoodY THEN
                Score = Score + 10
                PlaceFood()
                ' Don't drop tail -> Snake grows
            ELSE
                ' FIX: Erase Tail (Index 0 is Tail)
                TailX = SnakeX[0]
                TailY = SnakeY[0]
                
                LOCATE TailY + 1, TailX + 1
                PRINT " ";
                
                ' FIX: Remove Tail (Drop index 0)
                SnakeX = DROP(1, SnakeX)
                SnakeY = DROP(1, SnakeY)
            ENDIF
        ENDIF
    ENDIF

    ' 3. UI Update
    LOCATE HEIGHT + 2, 2
    COLOR 14, 0
    PRINT "Score: " + Score;
    
    IF GameOver THEN
        LOCATE HEIGHT / 2, (WIDTH / 2) - 4
        COLOR 15, 4
        PRINT " GAME OVER "
        LOCATE((HEIGHT / 2) + 1, (WIDTH / 2) - 8)
        COLOR 7, 0
        PRINT "Press Q to Quit"
    ENDIF

    SLEEP SPEED
LOOP

CURSOR TRUE
COLOR 2,0
CLS

' --- End of File ---

Filename: solve_test.jdb
' Test program for the new SOLVE function

' We want to solve the system Ax = b
'
'      [ 2  1 -1 ] [ x1 ]   [  8 ]
'  A = [ -3 -1  2 ] [ x2 ] = [ -11 ] = b
'      [ -2  1  2 ] [ x3 ]   [ -3 ]
'
' The known solution is x = [ 2, 3, -1 ]

PRINT "Setting up the matrix A and vector b..."

DIM A[3, 3]
A[0,0] = 2 : A[0,1] = 1 : A[0,2] = -1
A[1,0] = -3 : A[1,1] = -1 : A[1,2] = 2
A[2,0] = -2 : A[2,1] = 1 : A[2,2] = 2

DIM b[3]
b[0] = 8
b[1] = -11
b[2] = -3

PRINT "Matrix A:"
PRINT A
PRINT "Vector b:"
PRINT b
PRINT ""

PRINT "Calling SOLVE(A, b)..."
DIM x[0] ' Define x, will be resized by the function result
x = SOLVE(A, b)

PRINT "Solution vector x:"
PRINT x

PRINT "Expected solution is [2 3 -1]"

' --- End of File ---

Filename: sound_test.jdb
' ==========================================================
' == jdBASIC Interactive Synthesizer Demo
' ==========================================================
'
' This program demonstrates the SOUND.* commands by creating
' a playable on-screen piano keyboard.
'
' --- CONTROLS ---
' Keys A,S,D,F,G,H,J,K map to the white keys (C4 to C5).
' Keys W,E,T,Y,U map to the black keys.
' Press ESC to quit.
'
' ----------------------------------------------------------

' --- Initialization ---
OPTION "NOPAUSE"
SCREEN 800, 300, "jdBASIC Synthesizer"
SOUND.INIT

' Configure a "Rhodes" like electric piano sound on track 0
' waveform$, attack, decay, sustain, release
SOUND.VOICE 0, "SINE", 0.01, 0.3, 0.4, 0.4

' Configure a "Synth Bass" sound on track 1
SOUND.VOICE 1, "SAW", 0.02, 0.1, 0.9, 0.2


' --- Data Setup ---
' Create a MAP to store note frequencies for easy lookup.
DIM NOTES As MAP

NOTES{"C4"}  = 261.63 : NOTES{"C#4"} = 277.18
NOTES{"D4"}  = 293.66 : NOTES{"D#4"} = 311.13
NOTES{"E4"}  = 329.63
NOTES{"F4"}  = 349.23 : NOTES{"F#4"} = 369.99
NOTES{"G4"}  = 392.00 : NOTES{"G#4"} = 415.30
NOTES{"A4"}  = 440.00 : NOTES{"A#4"} = 466.16
NOTES{"B4"}  = 493.88
NOTES{"C5"}  = 523.25

' Map keyboard keys to note names.
DIM KEY_MAP As MAP
KEY_MAP{"A"}="C4"  : KEY_MAP{"S"}="D4" : KEY_MAP{"D"}="E4"
KEY_MAP{"F"}="F4"  : KEY_MAP{"G"}="G4" : KEY_MAP{"H"}="A4"
KEY_MAP{"J"}="B4"  : KEY_MAP{"K"}="C5"
' Black keys
KEY_MAP{"W"}="C#4" : KEY_MAP{"E"}="D#4" : KEY_MAP{"T"}="F#4"
KEY_MAP{"Y"}="G#4" : KEY_MAP{"U"}="A#4"


' --- Drawing Subroutine ---
SUB DRAW_KEYBOARD(LAST_KEY$)
  CLS 30, 30, 40
  
  ' Draw White Keys
  WHITE_KEYS$ = "ASDFGHJK"
  FOR i = 0 TO LEN(WHITE_KEYS$) - 1
    key_char$ = MID$(WHITE_KEYS$, i + 1, 1)
    x = 50 + (i * 80)
    
    ' Highlight the currently pressed key
    IF key_char$ = UCASE$(LAST_KEY$) THEN
      RECT x, 50, 78, 180, 200, 200, 255, TRUE
    ELSE
      RECT x, 50, 78, 180, 255, 255, 255, TRUE
    ENDIF
    RECT x, 50, 78, 180, 0, 0, 0, FALSE ' Border
    TEXT x + 30, 200, key_char$, 0, 0, 0
  NEXT i
  
  ' Draw Black Keys
  BLACK_KEYS$ = "WETYU"
  BLACK_KEY_POS = [1.5, 2.5, 4.5, 5.5, 6.5]
  FOR i = 0 TO LEN(BLACK_KEYS$) - 1
      key_char$ = MID$(BLACK_KEYS$, i + 1, 1)
      x = 50 + (BLACK_KEY_POS[i] * 80)
      
      IF key_char$ = UCASE$(LAST_KEY$) THEN
        RECT x, 50, 50, 120, 150, 150, 150, TRUE
      ELSE
        RECT x, 50, 50, 120, 0, 0, 0, TRUE
      ENDIF
      TEXT x + 18, 150, key_char$, 255, 255, 255
  NEXT i
  
  TEXT 10, 270, "Press keyboard keys to play notes. ESC to quit.", 200, 200, 200
  SCREENFLIP
ENDSUB


' --- Main Program Loop ---
LAST_KEY_PLAYED$ = ""

QUIT = false

DO
  ' Initial draw
  DRAW_KEYBOARD(LAST_KEY_PLAYED$)

  ' Loop while waiting for a key press
  DO
    KEY_PRESSED$ = UCASE$(INKEY$())
    IF KEY_PRESSED$ <> "" THEN EXITDO
    
    ' If a note was playing but no key is pressed now, release it.
    IF LAST_KEY_PLAYED$ <> "" THEN
      SOUND.RELEASE 0
      LAST_KEY_PLAYED$ = ""
      DRAW_KEYBOARD(LAST_KEY_PLAYED$) ' Redraw to un-highlight
    ENDIF
    
    SLEEP 10 ' Small delay to prevent high CPU usage
  LOOP UNTIL QUIT

  IF QUIT THEN EXITDO

  ' Check for ESC key to quit
  IF ASC(KEY_PRESSED$) = 27 THEN
    QUIT = TRUE
  ENDIF

  ' Check if the pressed key is a valid note and is a *new* note
  IF MAP.EXISTS(KEY_MAP, KEY_PRESSED$) AND KEY_PRESSED$ <> LAST_KEY_PLAYED$ THEN
      NOTE_NAME$ = KEY_MAP{KEY_PRESSED$}
      FREQ = NOTES{NOTE_NAME$}
      
      ' Play the new note
      SOUND.PLAY 0, FREQ
      
      ' Play a bass note an octave lower on track 1
      SOUND.PLAY 1, FREQ / 2
      
      LAST_KEY_PLAYED$ = KEY_PRESSED$
  ENDIF
  
LOOP UNTIL QUIT

' --- End of File ---

Filename: sprite_test.jdb
' ==========================================================
' == jdBasic Sprite System Test Program
' ==
' == This program demonstrates loading, creating, moving,
' == and detecting collisions between sprites.
' ==========================================================

' --- 1. Initialization ---
PRINT "Initializing Graphics and Sound..."
SCREEN 800, 600, "jdBasic Sprite Test"
SOUND.INIT

' --- 2. Load Assets ---
' NOTE: You must provide 'player.png' and 'enemy.png' files
' in the same directory as the interpreter to run this demo.
' They should be small images (e.g., 32x32 pixels).
PRINT "Loading sprite images..."
SPRITE.LOAD 1, "player.png" ' Assign image to type_id 1
SPRITE.LOAD 2, "enemy.png"  ' Assign image to type_id 2

' Configure a simple sound voice for collision feedback
SOUND.VOICE 0, "SQUARE", 0.01, 0.1, 0.5, 0.2

' --- 3. Create Game Objects ---
PRINT "Creating sprites..."
PLAYER_ID = SPRITE.CREATE(1, 400, 500) ' Create player sprite from type 1
ENEMY1_ID = SPRITE.CREATE(2, 100, 100) ' Create enemy sprite from type 2
ENEMY2_ID = SPRITE.CREATE(2, 700, 150) ' Create another enemy

' --- 4. Set Initial Velocities ---
' Make the enemies move automatically
SPRITE.SET_VELOCITY ENEMY1_ID, 2, 1.5
SPRITE.SET_VELOCITY ENEMY2_ID, -1.5, 2

' --- 5. Main Game Loop ---
PRINT "Starting main loop... Press ESC to quit."
DO
  ' --- Handle Input: Exit on ESC key ---
  k$ = INKEY$
  IF k$ = CHR$(27) THEN ' 27 is the ASCII code for ESC
    EXITDO
  ENDIF

  ' --- Player Control: Move player sprite to mouse position ---
  SPRITE.MOVE PLAYER_ID, MOUSEX(), MOUSEY()

  ' --- Update Game State ---
  SPRITE.UPDATE ' Move all sprites based on their set velocities

  ' --- Simple AI: Bounce enemies off the screen edges ---
  ' A real game would need GET_VELOCITY functions to do this properly.
  ' Here we just reset the position for simplicity.
  IF SPRITE.GET_X(ENEMY1_ID) < 0 OR SPRITE.GET_X(ENEMY1_ID) > 770 THEN
      SPRITE.MOVE ENEMY1_ID, 400, 100
  ENDIF
  IF SPRITE.GET_Y(ENEMY2_ID) < 0 OR SPRITE.GET_Y(ENEMY2_ID) > 570 THEN
      SPRITE.MOVE ENEMY2_ID, 400, 100
  ENDIF

  ' --- Check for Collisions ---
  COLLISION_STATUS$ = "No Collision"
  ' Check if the player sprite is touching either enemy sprite
  IF SPRITE.COLLISION(PLAYER_ID, ENEMY1_ID) OR SPRITE.COLLISION(PLAYER_ID, ENEMY2_ID) THEN
      COLLISION_STATUS$ = "COLLISION!"
      SOUND.PLAY 0, 220 ' Play a low 'A' note
  ENDIF

  ' --- Drawing Phase ---
  CLS 0, 0, 50 ' Clear screen to a dark blue

  SPRITE.DRAW_ALL ' Draw all active sprites at their new positions

  ' Draw UI / Status Text
  TEXT 10, 10, "jdBasic Sprite Demo", 255, 255, 0 ' Yellow
  TEXT 10, 40, "Move mouse to control player. Press ESC to quit.", 200, 200, 200 ' Grey
  TEXT 650, 10, COLLISION_STATUS$, 255, 0, 0 ' Red

  SCREENFLIP ' Push everything drawn to the window

  SLEEP 16 ' Wait for ~16ms to target roughly 60 FPS

LOOP UNTIL FALSE ' Loop "forever" until EXITDO is called

' --- 6. Shutdown ---
PRINT "Program finished."
' The graphics system will be shut down automatically by the interpreter.
' --- End of File ---

Filename: SQ.jdb
EXPORT MODULE SQ

'DIM CONFIG AS MAP must be defined in the calling module!

' --- INIT ---
EXPORT SUB START()
    SOUND.INIT
    SOUND.BPM 120
    
    ' Try to load existing data, otherwise create defaults
    TRY
        json$ = TXTREADER$("sq_data.json")
        CONFIG = JSON.PARSE$(json$)
        PRINT "Loaded sq_data.json"
    CATCH
        PRINT "No config found. Loading defaults..."
        INIT_DEFAULTS()
    ENDTRY
    
    PRINT "Sequencer Ready."
ENDSUB

EXPORT SUB STOP()
    SOUND.RESET
    PRINT "Sequencer stopped."
ENDSUB

' --- HELP SYSTEM (Dynamic) ---
EXPORT SUB H(more$)
    more$ = UCASE$(more$)
    
    IF more$ = "" THEN
        PRINT "--- SEQUENCER COMMANDS ---"
        PRINT "START : Init Sound & BPM"
        PRINT "V tr, id/code : Set Instrument (e.g. V 0, ""TP"")"
        PRINT "S tr, id/code : Set Sequence (e.g. S 0, ""44"")"
        PRINT "D rhy, kit    : Start Drums (Rhythm: 1-5, Kit: 1-5)"
        PRINT "P id          : Global Effects (1=Delay, 5=Reverb, etc.)"
        PRINT "SC root, mode : Set Scale (e.g. SC ""C3"", ""DORIAN"")"
        PRINT "EUCLID(...)   : Generate Euclidean Rhythm string"
        PRINT "STOP          : Stop Sound"
        PRINT ""
        PRINT "H ""I"" : List Instruments"
        PRINT "H ""S"" : List Sequences"
        PRINT "H ""D"" : List Drum Kits & Rhythms"
        PRINT "H ""P"" : List Global Effects"
    ENDIF

    IF more$ = "I" THEN
        PRINT "--- INSTRUMENTS ---"
        PRINT "ID   CODE   NAME"
        FOR EACH inst IN CONFIG{"instruments"}
            ' Build details string showing active effects
            details$ = ""
            IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "filter") THEN details$ = details$ + "FLT "
            IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "fm") THEN details$ = details$ + "FM "
            IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "bitcrush") THEN details$ = details$ + "BIT "
            
            ' Use FORMAT$ for alignment
            PRINT FORMAT$("{:<4} {:<6} {} ({})", inst{"id"}, inst{"code"}, inst{"name"}, TRIM$(details$))
        NEXT
    ENDIF

    IF more$ = "S" THEN
        PRINT "--- SEQUENCES ---"
        PRINT "ID   CODE   PATTERN"
        FOR EACH seq IN CONFIG{"sequences"}
            PRINT FORMAT$("{:<4} {:<6} {:<20} {}", seq{"id"}, seq{"code"}, left$(seq{"name"},20), seq{"pattern"})
        NEXT
    ENDIF

    IF more$ = "D" THEN
        PRINT "--- DRUM KITS ---"
        FOR EACH key IN MAP.KEYS(CONFIG{"kits"})
            kit = CONFIG{"kits"}{key}
            PRINT "Kit " + key + ": " + kit{"name"} + " (K:" + kit{"kick"} + " S:" + kit{"snare"} + " H:" + kit{"hat"} + ")"
        NEXT
        
        PRINT ""
        PRINT "--- RHYTHMS ---"
        FOR EACH key IN MAP.KEYS(CONFIG{"rhythms"})
            rhy = CONFIG{"rhythms"}{key}
            PRINT "Rhy " + key + ": " + rhy{"name"}
        NEXT
    ENDIF

    IF more$ = "P" THEN
        PRINT "--- GLOBAL EFFECTS ---"
        TRY
            FOR EACH fx IN CONFIG{"effects"}
                PRINT FORMAT$("{:<4} {:<10} {}", fx{"id"}, fx{"type"}, fx{"name"})
            NEXT
        CATCH
            PRINT "No effects loaded."
        ENDTRY
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

' --- GLOBAL EFFECTS (P) ---
EXPORT SUB P(effect_id)
    ' 1. Check for Reset/Off command (usually 0 or invalid ID)
    IF effect_id <= 0 THEN
        SOUND.DELAY FALSE, 0, 0, 0
        PRINT "Global effects off."
        RETURN
    ENDIF

    ' 2. Find the effect in the JSON list
    selected_fx = {}
    FOR EACH fx IN CONFIG{"effects"}
        IF fx{"id"} = effect_id THEN selected_fx = fx
    NEXT
    IF len(selected_fx) = 2 THEN
        SOUND.DELAY FALSE, 0, 0, 0
        PRINT "Effect ID " + effect_id + " not found. Effects off."
        RETURN
    ENDIF

    ' 3. Apply based on Type
    type$ = selected_fx{"type"}
    par = selected_fx{"params"}
    IF MAP.EXISTs(selected_fx,"active") THEN
        isactive = selected_fx{"active"}
    ENDIF
    
    SWITCH type$
        CASE "DELAY"
            ' Delay expects: TRUE, time, feedback, mix
            SOUND.DELAY isactive, par[0], par[1], par[2]
            
        CASE "REVERB"
            ' Reverb expects: mix, room_size, damp, width (based on SQ_old usage)
            SOUND.REVERB par[0], par[1], par[2], par[3]
            
        CASE "COMPRESSOR"
            ' Compressor params from SQ_old: threshold, ratio, attack, release, gain
            SOUND.COMPRESSOR par[0], par[1], par[2], par[3], par[4]
            
        DEFAULT
            PRINT "Unknown effect type: " + type$
    ENDSWITCH

    PRINT selected_fx{"name"}
ENDSUB

' --- FILE I/O ---
EXPORT SUB LOAD(filename$)
    TRY
        CONFIG = JSON.PARSE$(TXTREADER$(filename$))
        PRINT "Configuration loaded from " + filename$
    CATCH
        PRINT "Error loading file: " + ERRMSG$
    ENDTRY
ENDSUB

EXPORT SUB SAVE(filename$)
    TRY
        json$ = JSON.STRINGIFY$(CONFIG)
        TXTWRITER filename$, json$
        PRINT "Configuration saved to " + filename$
    CATCH
        PRINT "Error saving file: " + ERRMSG$
    ENDTRY
ENDSUB

' --- HELPER: Find Item in List by "id" or "code" ---
FUNC FIND_ITEM(clist, key$)
    key$ = UCASE$(key$)
    ' Is it a numeric ID?
    IF REGEX.MATCH("^\d+$", key$) THEN
        id = VAL(key$)
        FOR EACH item IN clist
            IF item{"id"} = id THEN RETURN item
        NEXT
    ELSE
        ' It is a string code (e.g. "TP")
        FOR EACH item IN clist
            IF UCASE$(item{"code"}) = key$ THEN RETURN item
        NEXT
    ENDIF
    RETURN {}
ENDFUNC

' --- INSTRUMENTS (V) ---
EXPORT SUB V(track, name$)
    inst = FIND_ITEM(CONFIG{"instruments"}, STR$(name$))
    
    IF typeof(inst) <> "MAP" THEN
        PRINT "Instrument not found: " + name$
        RETURN
    ENDIF
    
    PRINT "Track " + track + ": " + inst{"name"}

    ' --- TR-808 SAMPLE HANDLING ---
    IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "type") ANDALSO inst{"type"} = "TR808" THEN
        sfx_id = LOAD_TR808_SAMPLE(inst)
        IF sfx_id > 0 THEN
            SOUND.SAMPLE track, sfx_id, "C3", FALSE 
            SOUND.VOICE track, "SAMPLE", 0.001, 0.1, 2.0, 0.1
        ELSE
            PRINT "Error loading 808 sample."
        ENDIF
        RETURN
    ENDIF    
    
    ' Apply Base Voice Params
    ' JSON format: "adsr": [0.01, 0.1, 0.8, 0.2]
    adsr = inst{"adsr"}
    SOUND.VOICE track, inst{"waveform"}, adsr[0], adsr[1], adsr[2], adsr[3]
    
    ' Apply Optional Effects if they exist in the map
    IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "filter") THEN SOUND.FILTER track, inst{"filter"}
    IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "lfo") THEN 
        lfo = inst{"lfo"}
        SOUND.LFO track, lfo[0], lfo[1]
    ENDIF
    IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "fm") THEN 
        fm = inst{"fm"}
        SOUND.FM track, fm[0], fm[1]
    ENDIF
    IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "bitcrush") THEN 
        bc = inst{"bitcrush"}
        SOUND.BITCRUSH track, bc[0], bc[1]
    ENDIF
    IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "ringmod") THEN 
        rm = inst{"ringmod"}
        SOUND.RINGMOD track, rm[0], rm[1]
    ENDIF
    IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "eq") THEN 
        eq = inst{"eq"}
        SOUND.EQ track, eq[0], eq[1], eq[2]
    ENDIF
    IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "unison") THEN 
        un = inst{"unison"}
        SOUND.UNISON track, un[0], un[1], un[2]
    ENDIF
    IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "delay") THEN 
        dl = inst{"delay"}
        SOUND.DELAY TRUE, dl[0], dl[1], dl[2]
    ENDIF
    IF MAP.EXISTS(inst, "reverb") THEN 
        rv = inst{"reverb"}
        SOUND.REVERB rv[0], rv[1], rv[2], rv[3]
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

' --- SEQUENCES (S) ---
EXPORT SUB S(track, p$)
    ' Try to find it in the config list
    seq = FIND_ITEM(CONFIG{"sequences"}, STR$(p$))
    pattern$ = ""
    
    IF len(seq) > 2 THEN
        pattern$ = seq{"pattern"}
        PRINT "Seq " + seq{"id"} + " (" + seq{"code"} + "): " + pattern$
    ELSE
        ' Assume raw string input
        pattern$ = p$
        PRINT "Custom Seq: " + pattern$
    ENDIF
    
    SOUND.SEQ track, pattern$, "VOICE"
ENDSUB

' --- HELPER: Load 808 Sample ---
FUNC LOAD_TR808_SAMPLE(inst)
    code$ = inst{"sample"} ' e.g. "BD"
    knobs = inst{"knobs"}  ' e.g. [50, 50] or [25]
    
    ' Construct Filename based on knob count (See TR808.TXT)
    fname$ = code$
    
    ' 0 Knobs: CB, CH, CL, CP, MA, RS
    IF code$ = "CB" OR code$ = "CH" OR code$ = "CL" OR code$ = "CP" OR code$ = "MA" OR code$ = "RS" THEN
        fname$ = fname$ + ".WAV"
    ' 2 Knobs: BD, CY, SD (Tone+Decay/Snappy)
    ELSEIF code$ = "BD" OR code$ = "CY" OR code$ = "SD" THEN
        ' Pad to 2 digits (e.g. 0 -> "00", 50 -> "50")
        t$ = STR$(knobs[0]) : IF LEN(t$) < 2 THEN t$ = "00" + t$
        d$ = STR$(knobs[1]) : IF LEN(d$) < 2 THEN d$ = "00" + d$
        fname$ = fname$ + t$ + d$ + ".WAV"
    ' 1 Knob: All others (HC, HT, LC, etc)
    ELSE
        k$ = STR$(knobs[0]) : IF LEN(k$) < 2 THEN k$ = "00" + k$
        fname$ = fname$ + k$ + ".WAV"
    ENDIF
    
    path$ = "D:/usr/dev/c/jdBasic/jdb/intern/Roland TR-808/" + code$ + "/" + fname$
    
    ' Check Cache
    IF MAP.EXISTS(SFX_CACHE, path$) THEN
        RETURN SFX_CACHE{path$}
    ENDIF
    
    ' Load New
    PRINT "Loading 808: " + fname$
    id = NEXT_SFX_ID
    TRY
        SFX.LOAD id, path$
        SFX_CACHE{path$} = id
        NEXT_SFX_ID = NEXT_SFX_ID + 1
        RETURN id
    CATCH
        PRINT "Failed to load: " + path$
        RETURN -1
    ENDTRY
ENDFUNC

' --- DRUMS (D) ---
EXPORT SUB D(rhythm_id, kit_id)
    ' 1. Load Kit
    kit_key$ = STR$(kit_id)
    kits_map = CONFIG{"kits"}
    
    IF NOT MAP.EXISTS(kits_map, kit_key$) THEN
        PRINT "Kit not found: " + kit_id
        RETURN
    ENDIF
    
    kit = kits_map{kit_key$}
    PRINT "Loading Kit " + kit{"name"} + "..."
    
    V 0, STR$(kit{"kick"})
    V 1, STR$(kit{"snare"})
    V 2, STR$(kit{"hat"})
    IF kit_id = 8 THEN
        V 3, STR$(kit{"clap"})
    ENDIF
    
    ' 2. Load Rhythm
    rhy_key$ = STR$(rhythm_id)
    rhy_map = CONFIG{"rhythms"}
    
    IF NOT MAP.EXISTS(rhy_map, rhy_key$) THEN
        PRINT "Rhythm not found: " + rhythm_id
        RETURN
    ENDIF
    
    rhy = rhy_map{rhy_key$}
    PRINT "Playing " + rhy{"name"}
    
    SOUND.SEQ 0, rhy{"kick_pat"}, "VOICE"
    SOUND.SEQ 1, rhy{"snare_pat"}, "VOICE"
    SOUND.SEQ 2, rhy{"hat_pat"}, "VOICE"
ENDSUB

' --- UTILITIES ---
EXPORT SUB SC(track, root$, mode$)
    SOUND.SCALE track, root$, mode$
    PRINT "Scale set: " + root$ + " " + mode$
ENDSUB

EXPORT FUNC EUCLID(hits, steps, note$)
    ' Bjorklund algorithm wrapper
    pat$ = ""
    IF hits > steps THEN hits = steps
    bucket = 0
    FOR i = 0 TO steps - 1
        bucket = bucket + hits
        IF bucket >= steps THEN
            bucket = bucket - steps
            pat$ = pat$ + note$ + " "
        ELSE
            pat$ = pat$ + "~ "
        ENDIF
    NEXT
    RETURN "[" + TRIM$(pat$) + "]"
ENDFUNC

' --- DEFAULT DATA GENERATOR (Fallback) ---
SUB INIT_DEFAULTS()
    instruments = []
    instruments = APPEND(instruments, {"id":0, "code":"TP", "name":"Trance Pluck", "waveform":"SAW", "adsr":[0.001, 0.3, 0.0, 0.2], "filter":2000})
    
    sequences = []
    sequences = APPEND(sequences, {"id":1, "code":"44", "pattern":"c1(4,4)"})

    kits = {
        "1": {"name":"Standard", "kick":20, "snare":19, "hat":21}
    }

    rhythms = {
        "1": {"name":"Techno", "kick_pat":"c1 c1 c1 c1", "snare_pat":"~ c1 ~ c1", "hat_pat":"[c1 c1] [c1 c1] [c1 c1] [c1 c1]"}
    }
    
    effects = [
        { "id": 1, "name": "Power on", "type": "DELAY", "params": [120, 0.0, 0.5] }
    ]

    CONFIG = {"instruments": instruments, "sequences": sequences, "kits": kits, "rhythms": rhythms, "effects": effects}
ENDSUB

' sqlite_test.jdb

' Load the new SQLite module

PRINT "SQLite Test Program"
PRINT "-------------------"

PRINT "Current Directory: ";
PWD

' Try to open a database.
db = SQLITE.OPEN("test.db")

' Check if the handle is valid
IF TYPEOF(db) <> "SQLITEDB" THEN
    PRINT
    PRINT "--- DATABASE OPEN FAILED! ---"
    PRINT "Reason: " + SQLITE.ERROR$() ' <-- ADD THIS LINE
    PRINT "-----------------------------"
    GOTO Ende
ENDIF


PRINT "Database opened successfully."

' Create a table. Use EXEC for commands that don't return rows.
sql_create$ = "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT, score REAL);"
success = SQLITE.EXEC(db, sql_create$)
IF NOT success THEN
    PRINT "Error creating table: " + SQLITE.ERROR$()
    SQLITE.CLOSE db
    GOTO Ende
ENDIF

' Insert some data
SQLITE.EXEC(db, "INSERT INTO users (name, score) VALUES ('Alice', 100.5);")
SQLITE.EXEC(db, "INSERT INTO users (name, score) VALUES ('Bob', 95.2);")
SQLITE.EXEC(db, "INSERT INTO users (name, score) VALUES ('Charlie', 110.0);")

PRINT "Data inserted."

' Query the data back
sql_query$ = "SELECT id, name, score FROM users WHERE score > 100;"
results = SQLITE.QUERY(db, sql_query$)

IF TYPEOF(results) = "ARRAY" THEN
    PRINT
    PRINT "Query Results (score > 100):"
    FOR i = 0 TO LEN(results)[0] - 1
        user_row = results[i]
        PRINT "ID: " + user_row{"id"} + ", Name: " + user_row{"name"} + ", Score: " + user_row{"score"}
    NEXT i
ELSE
    PRINT "Query failed: " + SQLITE.ERROR$()
ENDIF

' Close the database connection
SQLITE.CLOSE db
PRINT
PRINT "Database closed."
goto out

ende:
PRINT "An error occured"
out:
PRINT "Program finished"

Filename: stock_chart.jdb
' =================================================================
' == jdBasic Stock Data to Excel Chart
' ==
' == This program fetches intraday stock data from Alpha Vantage,
' == parses the JSON response, opens Microsoft Excel via COM,
' == writes the data to a worksheet, and creates a line chart.
' =================================================================

' --- 1. Fetch Data from API ---
PRINT "Fetching stock data for IBM from Alpha Vantage..."

' Define the API URL. NOTE: The "demo" key is limited.
' Replace with your own free key from alphavantage.co for reliability.

current_stock$ = "IBM"
API_KEY$ = "demo"
'API_KEY$ = Getenv$("ALPHA_VANTAGE_API_KEY")
API_URL$ = "https://www.alphavantage.co/query?function=TIME_SERIES_INTRADAY&symbol=" + current_stock$ + "&interval=5min&apikey=" + API_KEY$

' Use the built-in HTTP.GET$ function to retrieve the data.
JSON_RESPONSE$ = HTTP.GET$(API_URL$)

' Basic error check for the HTTP request.
IF ERR <> 0 OR LEN(JSON_RESPONSE$) < 100 THEN
    PRINT "Error: Failed to fetch data from the API."
    PRINT "HTTP Status Code: "; HTTP.STATUSCODE()
    PRINT "Response: "; JSON_RESPONSE$
    END
ENDIF

PRINT "Data fetched successfully."
PRINT

' --- 2. Parse the JSON Data ---
PRINT "Parsing JSON response..."

' The JSON.PARSE$ function converts the string into a nested Map object.
STOCK_DATA = JSON.PARSE$(JSON_RESPONSE$)

' Extract the main time series object from the parsed data.
TIME_SERIES = STOCK_DATA{"Time Series (5min)"}
PRINT "JSON parsed. Found time series data."
PRINT

' --- 3. Automate Excel using COM ---
PRINT "Launching Microsoft Excel..."

' Use CREATEOBJECT to start Excel. It runs in the background by default.
objXL = CREATEOBJECT("Excel.Application")
IF ERR <> 0 THEN
    PRINT "Error: Could not start Excel. Please ensure it is installed."
    END
ENDIF

' Make the Excel window visible to the user.
objXL.Visible = TRUE

' Add a new, empty workbook.
objWB = objXL.Workbooks.Add()


PRINT "Excel is running and a new workbook has been created."
PRINT

' --- 4. Write Data to the Worksheet ---
PRINT "Writing data to the worksheet..."

' Set column headers.
objcell = objXL.ActiveSheet.Cells(1, 1)
objcell.Value = "Timestamp"
objcell = objXL.ActiveSheet.Cells(1, 2)
objcell.Value =  "Closing Price"

' Get an array of all the timestamps (which are the keys of the time series map).
TIMESTAMPS = MAP.KEYS(TIME_SERIES)

'Remember the date for Cchart title
current_date$ = TIMESTAMPS[0]

' Loop through the timestamps, get the corresponding data, and write to cells.
' We start at row 2 because row 1 has the headers.
FOR i = 0 TO LEN(TIMESTAMPS) - 1
    current_timestamp$ = TIMESTAMPS[i]
    
    ' Get the data object for the current timestamp.
    price_data = TIME_SERIES{current_timestamp$}
    ' Only get the time value
    current_timestamp$ = RIGHT$(current_timestamp$,8)
    
    ' Extract the closing price. Note the key is "4. close".
    closing_price = VAL(price_data{"4. close"})
    
    ' Write the timestamp to column A and the price to column B.
    objcell = objXL.ActiveSheet.Cells(i + 2, 1)
    objcell.Value = current_timestamp$
    objcell = objXL.ActiveSheet.Cells(i + 2, 2)
    objcell.Value = closing_price
NEXT i

PRINT "Data writing complete."
PRINT

' --- 5. Create the Chart ---
PRINT "Creating the chart..."

' Get the total number of data rows to define the chart's data range.
num_data_rows = LEN(TIMESTAMPS)[0]
data_range = objXL.ActiveSheet.Range("A1:B" + STR$(num_data_rows + 1))

' Create a new chart object on the worksheet.
chartObject = objXL.ActiveSheet.ChartObjects.Add(150, 20, 600, 350) ' Left, Top, Width, Height
chart = chartObject.Chart

' Set the data source for the chart.
' This tells Excel which cells contain the data to be plotted.
' It includes the header row for automatic legend/axis titles.
a = chart.SetSourceData(data_range)

' Set the chart type to a line graph. (xlLine = 4)
chart.ChartType = 4

' Add a title to the chart.
chart.HasTitle = TRUE
chart.ChartTitle.Text = current_stock$ + " Intraday Stock Price (5min) " + LEFT$(current_date$,10)

PRINT "Chart created successfully."
PRINT
PRINT "--- Program Finished ---"

' NOTE: The script will end here, but Excel will remain open
' with the data and the chart for you to view.


' --- End of File ---

PRINT "--- String Operator Showcase ---"

' 1. Concatenation (+)
PRINT "Hello " + "World!"

' 2. Replacement (-)
PRINT "The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog" - "brown "
PRINT "ababababc" - "ab"

' 4. Slicing (/)

' 5. Unary Minus (-) to split string
char_array = -"jdBasic"
PRINT "Character array from 'jdBasic':"
PRINT char_array

' Chaining them together
PRINT ("Atomi "+("A"*10)+" truncate me." - "AA") / 3

    SWITCH UCASE$(command$)
        CASE "START"
            PRINT "Starting process..."
            ' ... code to start ...

        CASE "STOP"
            PRINT "Stopping process..."
            ' ... code to stop ...

        CASE "PAUSE"

Filename: s_edit.jdb
' =================================================================
' == jdBasic Sprite Editor (ImGui + SDL3)
' =================================================================

' --- 1. CONFIGURATION & GLOBALS ---
OPTION "NOPAUSE"
SCREEN_W = 1920
SCREEN_H = 1080
TITLE$ = "jdBasic Sprite Editor v1.0"

' Canvas Settings
CANVAS_W = 16
CANVAS_H = 16
ZOOM_LEVEL = 20.0 ' Pixels per sprite-pixel
SCALE_LEVEL = 1.5 ' Scale of the whole viewport

' Tools Enum
ENUM TOOLS
    PENCIL
    ERASER
    LINE
    CIRCLE
    FILL
    PICKER
    SELECT_TOOL
ENDENUM

CURRENT_TOOL = TOOLS.PENCIL
SHOW_GRID = TRUE

' Colors (RGBA [0-255])
CURRENT_COLOR = [255, 255, 255, 255]
BG_COLOR = [20, 20, 20, 255]

' Selection State
SEL_START_X = -1
SEL_START_Y = -1
SEL_END_X = -1
SEL_END_Y = -1
HAS_SELECTION = FALSE
CLIPBOARD_DATA = []
CLIPBOARD_W = 0
CLIPBOARD_H = 0

' Mouse State
MOUSE_DOWN = FALSE
LAST_X = -1
LAST_Y = -1

' Initialize Graphics
SCREEN SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H, TITLE$, SCALE_LEVEL
GUI.THEME "DARK"

' --- 2. DATA STRUCTURES ---

' The Sprite Data is a 1D array of RGBA arrays.
' Index = y * Width + x
' Value = [r, g, b, a]
SPRITE_DATA = []

SUB INIT_SPRITE(w, h)
    CANVAS_W = w
    CANVAS_H = h
    TOTAL_PIXELS = w * h
    SPRITE_DATA = []
    ' Initialize with transparent black
    FOR i = 0 TO TOTAL_PIXELS - 1
        SPRITE_DATA = APPEND(SPRITE_DATA, [0, 0, 0, 0])
    NEXT i
    ' Reset selection
    HAS_SELECTION = FALSE
ENDSUB

' Initialize default sprite
INIT_SPRITE 16, 16

' --- 3. PALETTES ---
' Pico-8 Palette (16 colors)
PALETTE_PICO8 = [ [0,0,0,255], [29,43,83,255], [126,37,83,255], [0,135,81,255], 
    [171,82,54,255], [95,87,79,255], [194,195,199,255], [255,241,232,255], 
    [255,0,77,255], [255,163,0,255], [255,236,39,255], [0,228,54,255], 
    [41,173,255,255], [131,118,156,255], [255,119,168,255], [255,204,170,255] 
]

' Simple NES-ish (approximate)
PALETTE_NES = [ [124,124,124,255], [0,0,252,255], [0,0,188,255], [68,40,188,255], 
    [148,0,132,255], [168,0,32,255], [168,16,0,255], [136,20,0,255], 
    [80,48,0,255], [0,120,0,255], [0,104,0,255], [0,88,0,255], 
    [0,64,88,255], [0,0,0,255], [0,0,0,255], [0,0,0,255] 
]

' Grayscale
PALETTE_GRAY = []
FOR i = 0 TO 15
    v = i * 17
    PALETTE_GRAY = APPEND(PALETTE_GRAY, [v, v, v, 255])
NEXT i

ACTIVE_PALETTE = PALETTE_PICO8

' --- 4. DRAWING FUNCTIONS ---

SUB SET_PIXEL(x, y, color)
    IF x >= 0 AND x < CANVAS_W AND y >= 0 AND y < CANVAS_H THEN
        idx = y * CANVAS_W + x
        SPRITE_DATA[idx] = color
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

FUNC GET_PIXEL(x, y)
    IF x >= 0 AND x < CANVAS_W AND y >= 0 AND y < CANVAS_H THEN
        idx = y * CANVAS_W + x
        RETURN SPRITE_DATA[idx]
    ENDIF
    RETURN [0,0,0,0]
ENDFUNC

SUB DRAW_LINE(x0, y0, x1, y1, color)
    dx = ABS(x1 - x0)
    dy = ABS(y1 - y0) * -1
    sx = -1 : IF x0 < x1 THEN sx = 1
    sy = -1 : IF y0 < y1 THEN sy = 1
    err_val = dx + dy
    
    DO WHILE TRUE
        SET_PIXEL x0, y0, color
        IF x0 = x1 AND y0 = y1 THEN EXITDO
        e2 = 2 * err_val
        IF e2 >= dy THEN
            err_val = err_val + dy
            x0 = x0 + sx
        ENDIF
        IF e2 <= dx THEN
            err_val = err_val + dx
            y0 = y0 + sy
        ENDIF
    LOOP
ENDSUB

SUB DRAW_CIRCLE(cx, cy, r, color)
    x = 0
    y = r
    d = 3 - 2 * r
    DO WHILE y >= x
        SET_PIXEL cx+x, cy+y, color
        SET_PIXEL cx-x, cy+y, color
        SET_PIXEL cx+x, cy-y, color
        SET_PIXEL cx-x, cy-y, color
        SET_PIXEL cx+y, cy+x, color
        SET_PIXEL cx-y, cy+x, color
        SET_PIXEL cx+y, cy-x, color
        SET_PIXEL cx-y, cy-x, color
        x = x + 1
        IF d > 0 THEN
            y = y - 1
            d = d + 4 * (x - y) + 10
        ELSE
            d = d + 4 * x + 6
        ENDIF
    LOOP
ENDSUB

SUB FLOOD_FILL(x, y, target_color, fill_color)
    ' Basic recursive fill (stack-based to avoid recursion depth limits)
    ' Check if target matches fill
    IF target_color[0] = fill_color[0] AND target_color[1] = fill_color[1] AND target_color[2] = fill_color[2] AND target_color[3] = fill_color[3] THEN RETURN

    curr = GET_PIXEL(x, y)
    IF curr[0] <> target_color[0] OR curr[1] <> target_color[1] OR curr[2] <> target_color[2] OR curr[3] <> target_color[3] THEN RETURN

    stack = [[x, y]]
    
    DO WHILE LEN(stack)[0] > 0
        ' Pop
        idx = LEN(stack)[0] - 1
        pt = stack[idx]
        stack = TAKE(idx, stack) ' Remove last
        
        px = pt[0]
        py = pt[1]
        
        curr_p = GET_PIXEL(px, py)
        ' Check color match
        IF curr_p[0] = target_color[0] AND curr_p[1] = target_color[1] AND _
           curr_p[2] = target_color[2] AND curr_p[3] = target_color[3] THEN
            
            SET_PIXEL px, py, fill_color
            
            IF px > 0 THEN stack = APPEND(stack, [px - 1, py])
            IF px < CANVAS_W - 1 THEN stack = APPEND(stack, [px + 1, py])
            IF py > 0 THEN stack = APPEND(stack, [px, py - 1])
            IF py < CANVAS_H - 1 THEN stack = APPEND(stack, [px, py + 1])
        ENDIF
    LOOP
ENDSUB

' --- 5. CLIPBOARD FUNCTIONS ---

SUB COPY_SELECTION()
    IF HAS_SELECTION THEN
        CLIPBOARD_W = ABS(SEL_END_X - SEL_START_X) + 1
        CLIPBOARD_H = ABS(SEL_END_Y - SEL_START_Y) + 1
        CLIPBOARD_DATA = []
        
        sx = MIN([SEL_START_X, SEL_END_X])
        sy = MIN([SEL_START_Y, SEL_END_Y])
        
        FOR y = 0 TO CLIPBOARD_H - 1
            FOR x = 0 TO CLIPBOARD_W - 1
                p = GET_PIXEL(sx + x, sy + y)
                CLIPBOARD_DATA = APPEND(CLIPBOARD_DATA, p)
            NEXT x
        NEXT y
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

SUB PASTE_SELECTION(dx, dy)
    IF LEN(CLIPBOARD_DATA)[0] > 0 THEN
        FOR y = 0 TO CLIPBOARD_H - 1
            FOR x = 0 TO CLIPBOARD_W - 1
                idx = y * CLIPBOARD_W + x
                col = CLIPBOARD_DATA[idx]
                ' Don't paste invisible pixels if alpha is 0
                IF col[3] > 0 THEN
                    SET_PIXEL dx + x, dy + y, col
                ENDIF
            NEXT x
        NEXT y
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

' --- 6. FILE I/O ---

SUB SAVE_PROJECT(filename$)
    ' Create a map to save
    project = {}
    project{"width"} = CANVAS_W
    project{"height"} = CANVAS_H
    project{"data"} = SPRITE_DATA
    
    json$ = JSON.STRINGIFY$(project)
    TXTWRITER filename$, json$
    PRINT "Saved to " + filename$
ENDSUB

SUB LOAD_PROJECT(filename$)
    content$ = TXTREADER$(filename$)
    IF content$ <> "" THEN
        project = JSON.PARSE$(content$)
        CANVAS_W = project{"width"}
        CANVAS_H = project{"height"}
        SPRITE_DATA = project{"data"}
        PRINT "Loaded " + filename$
    ENDIF
ENDSUB

' --- 7. MAIN UI LOOP ---

DO
    CLS BG_COLOR[0], BG_COLOR[1], BG_COLOR[2]
    
' This allows the SDL Graphics (CLS and RECT) to be seen behind the UI.
    ' ID 2 = ImGuiCol_WindowBg
    GUI.PUSH_STYLE_COLOR 2, [0, 0, 0, 0]

    ' Start Main Window
    GUI.BEGIN "Sprite Editor", 0, 0, SCREEN_W, SCREEN_H, FALSE, GUI.FLAG("MENUBAR") + GUI.FLAG("NO_TITLEBAR") + GUI.FLAG("NO_RESIZE") + GUI.FLAG("NO_MOVE")
    
    ' Pop the WindowBg style immediately so it doesn't affect child windows by default
    GUI.POP_STYLE_COLOR 1
    
    ' --- MENU BAR ---
    IF GUI.BEGIN_MENU_BAR() THEN
        IF GUI.BEGIN_MENU("File") THEN
            IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("New 16x16") THEN INIT_SPRITE 16, 16
            IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("New 16x32") THEN INIT_SPRITE 16, 32
            IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("New 32x16") THEN INIT_SPRITE 32, 16
            IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("New 32x32") THEN INIT_SPRITE 32, 32
            GUI.SEPARATOR
            IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("Save Project") THEN SAVE_PROJECT "mysprite.json"
            IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("Load Project") THEN LOAD_PROJECT "mysprite.json"
            GUI.SEPARATOR
            IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("Exit") THEN EXITDO
            GUI.END_MENU
        ENDIF
        
        IF GUI.BEGIN_MENU("Edit") THEN
            IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("Copy", "Ctrl+C", FALSE, HAS_SELECTION) THEN COPY_SELECTION
            IF GUI.MENU_ITEM("Paste", "Ctrl+V", FALSE, LEN(CLIPBOARD_DATA)[0] > 0) THEN 
                ' Paste at top left for now, or center
                PASTE_SELECTION 0, 0
            ENDIF
            GUI.END_MENU
        ENDIF
        
        IF GUI.BEGIN_MENU("View") THEN
            SHOW_GRID = GUI.CHECKBOX("Show Grid", SHOW_GRID)
            GUI.END_MENU
        ENDIF
        
        GUI.END_MENU_BAR
    ENDIF

    ' --- LAYOUT ---
    ' Left Column: Tools & Palette
    GUI.BEGIN_CHILD "ToolsPanel", 200, 0, TRUE
        GUI.TEXT "TOOLS"
        GUI.SEPARATOR
        
        CURRENT_TOOL = GUI.RADIO("Pencil", CURRENT_TOOL, TOOLS.PENCIL) 
        CURRENT_TOOL =  GUI.RADIO("Eraser", CURRENT_TOOL, TOOLS.ERASER)
        CURRENT_TOOL =  GUI.RADIO("Line", CURRENT_TOOL, TOOLS.LINE) 
        CURRENT_TOOL =  GUI.RADIO("Circle", CURRENT_TOOL, TOOLS.CIRCLE)
        CURRENT_TOOL = GUI.RADIO("Fill", CURRENT_TOOL, TOOLS.FILL) 
        CURRENT_TOOL =  GUI.RADIO("Picker", CURRENT_TOOL, TOOLS.PICKER)
        CURRENT_TOOL =  GUI.RADIO("Select", CURRENT_TOOL, TOOLS.SELECT_TOOL)
        
        GUI.DUMMY 0, 20
        GUI.TEXT "COLOR"
        GUI.SEPARATOR
        GUI.COLOR "##CurrentColor", CURRENT_COLOR
        
        GUI.DUMMY 0, 20
        GUI.TEXT "PALETTES"
        GUI.SEPARATOR
        IF GUI.BUTTON("Pico-8", 180, 25) THEN ACTIVE_PALETTE = PALETTE_PICO8
        IF GUI.BUTTON("NES-ish", 180, 25) THEN ACTIVE_PALETTE = PALETTE_NES
        IF GUI.BUTTON("Gray", 180, 25) THEN ACTIVE_PALETTE = PALETTE_GRAY
        
        GUI.DUMMY 0, 10
        ' Draw Palette Swatches
        NUM_COLORS = LEN(ACTIVE_PALETTE)[0]

        ' Get ImGui Style Color IDs
        ID_BTN = GUI.COL("BUTTON")
        ID_HOV = GUI.COL("BUTTONHOVERED")
        ID_ACT = GUI.COL("BUTTONACTIVE")

        FOR i = 0 TO NUM_COLORS - 1
            GUI.PUSH_ID i
            
            col = SLICE(ACTIVE_PALETTE, 0, i)
            
            ' Create variations for hover (lighter) and active (darker) states
            ' We use CLAMP to ensure values stay within 0-255
            col_hov = CLAMP(col * 1.3, 0, 255)
            col_act = CLAMP(col * 0.7, 0, 255)
            
            ' Push the style colors for this button
            GUI.PUSH_STYLE_COLOR ID_BTN, col
            GUI.PUSH_STYLE_COLOR ID_HOV, col_hov
            GUI.PUSH_STYLE_COLOR ID_ACT, col_act
            
            ' Draw the button
            ' Using an empty label "" makes it look like a pure color swatch
            IF GUI.BUTTON("", 40, 20) THEN 
                CURRENT_COLOR = col
            ENDIF
            
            ' Important: Remove the style colors to avoid affecting other widgets
            GUI.POP_STYLE_COLOR 3
            
            ' Layout: 3 buttons per row
            IF (i + 1) MOD 4 <> 0 THEN GUI.SAME_LINE
            GUI.POP_ID
        NEXT i
        
    GUI.END_CHILD

    GUI.SAME_LINE

    ' Middle: Canvas
    COL_CHILDBG = 3 ' Hardcoded ImGuiCol_ChildBg (since "CHILDBG" isn't in GUI.COL yet)
    GUI.PUSH_STYLE_COLOR COL_CHILDBG, [0, 0, 0, 0] ' Transparent

    GUI.BEGIN_CHILD "CanvasPanel", 0, 0, TRUE
        
        ' 1. Handle Input Logic for Canvas
        ' We need global mouse coords, offset by window pos.
        ' ImGui cursor pos is relative to window content region.
        ' Since we don't have GetCursorScreenPos exposed, we assume standard layout.
        ' Let's use ImGui InvisibleButton to capture inputs over the area.
        
        CANVAS_PIXEL_W = CANVAS_W * ZOOM_LEVEL
        CANVAS_PIXEL_H = CANVAS_H * ZOOM_LEVEL
        
        ' Draw the canvas pixels manually using the DrawList (if exposed) or RECTs
        ' Since PLOTRAW is for the main screen, and ImGui overlays, we have to be clever.
        ' We will use ImGui's drawing commands if available, OR
        ' we assume the "Child" window background is transparent and we draw UNDER it using jdBasic commands?
        ' No, jdBasic commands draw to the SDL renderer. ImGui draws on top.
        ' We can use dlBasic graphics commands, but we need to know the offset.
        
        ' Let's calculate offset. 
        ' Sidebar width ~ 200 + padding. Top ~ menu bar height.
        OFFSET_X = 220
        OFFSET_Y = 40
        
        ' --- RENDER SPRITE TO SCREEN (Under ImGui) ---
        ' Draw checkerboard background
        FOR y = 0 TO CANVAS_H - 1
            FOR x = 0 TO CANVAS_W - 1
                sx = OFFSET_X + x * ZOOM_LEVEL
                sy = OFFSET_Y + y * ZOOM_LEVEL
                
                ' Checkerboard
                IF (x + y) MOD 2 = 0 THEN 
                    RECT sx, sy, ZOOM_LEVEL, ZOOM_LEVEL, 100, 100, 100, TRUE
                ELSE
                    RECT sx, sy, ZOOM_LEVEL, ZOOM_LEVEL, 150, 150, 150, TRUE
                ENDIF
                
                ' Sprite Pixel
                col = GET_PIXEL(x, y)
                IF typeof(col) = "ARRAY" THEN
                    RECT sx, sy, ZOOM_LEVEL, ZOOM_LEVEL, col[0], col[1], col[2], TRUE
                ENDIF
                
                ' Grid
                IF SHOW_GRID THEN
                    RECT sx, sy, ZOOM_LEVEL, ZOOM_LEVEL, 50, 50, 50, FALSE
                ENDIF
                
                ' Selection Highlight
                IF HAS_SELECTION THEN
                    sx_min = MIN([SEL_START_X, SEL_END_X])
                    sx_max = MAX([SEL_START_X, SEL_END_X])
                    sy_min = MIN([SEL_START_Y, SEL_END_Y])
                    sy_max = MAX([SEL_START_Y, SEL_END_Y])
                    
                    IF x >= sx_min AND x <= sx_max AND y >= sy_min AND y <= sy_max THEN
                        ' Draw a yellow border around selected pixels
                        RECT sx, sy, ZOOM_LEVEL, ZOOM_LEVEL, 255, 255, 0, FALSE
                    ENDIF
                ENDIF
                
            NEXT x
        NEXT y
        
        ' --- MOUSE INTERACTION ---
        MX = MOUSEX()
        MY = MOUSEY()
        
        ' Check if mouse is inside canvas
        IF MX >= OFFSET_X AND MX < OFFSET_X + CANVAS_PIXEL_W AND MY >= OFFSET_Y AND MY < OFFSET_Y + CANVAS_PIXEL_H THEN
           
            GX = INT((MX - OFFSET_X) / ZOOM_LEVEL)
            GY = INT((MY - OFFSET_Y) / ZOOM_LEVEL)
            
            ' Draw Hover Cursor
            CX = OFFSET_X + GX * ZOOM_LEVEL
            CY = OFFSET_Y + GY * ZOOM_LEVEL
            RECT CX, CY, ZOOM_LEVEL, ZOOM_LEVEL, 255, 0, 0, FALSE
            
            IF MOUSEB(1) THEN ' Left Click
                IF NOT MOUSE_DOWN THEN
                    ' On Click Start
                    MOUSE_DOWN = TRUE
                    START_X = GX
                    START_Y = GY
                    
                    IF CURRENT_TOOL = TOOLS.FILL THEN
                        target = GET_PIXEL(GX, GY)
                        IF typeof(col) <> "ARRAY" THEN 
                            target = [0,0,0,0]
                        ENDIF
                        FLOOD_FILL GX, GY, target, CURRENT_COLOR
                    ELSEIF CURRENT_TOOL = TOOLS.PICKER THEN
                        CURRENT_COLOR = GET_PIXEL(GX, GY)
                        CURRENT_TOOL = TOOLS.PENCIL ' Switch back
                    ELSEIF CURRENT_TOOL = TOOLS.SELECT_TOOL THEN
                        SEL_START_X = GX
                        SEL_START_Y = GY
                        SEL_END_X = GX
                        SEL_END_Y = GY
                        HAS_SELECTION = TRUE
                    ENDIF
                ELSE
                    ' On Drag
                    IF CURRENT_TOOL = TOOLS.PENCIL THEN
                        SET_PIXEL GX, GY, CURRENT_COLOR
                        ' Simple interpolation for fast mouse movement could go here
                    ELSEIF CURRENT_TOOL = TOOLS.ERASER THEN
                        SET_PIXEL GX, GY, [0,0,0,0]
                    ELSEIF CURRENT_TOOL = TOOLS.SELECT_TOOL THEN
                        SEL_END_X = GX
                        SEL_END_Y = GY
                    ENDIF
                ENDIF
                LAST_X = GX
                LAST_Y = GY
            ELSE
                IF MOUSE_DOWN THEN
                    ' On Release (For shapes)
                    MOUSE_DOWN = FALSE
                    IF CURRENT_TOOL = TOOLS.LINE THEN
                        DRAW_LINE START_X, START_Y, GX, GY, CURRENT_COLOR
                    ELSEIF CURRENT_TOOL = TOOLS.CIRCLE THEN
                        R = SQR((GX - START_X)^2 + (GY - START_Y)^2)
                        DRAW_CIRCLE START_X, START_Y, INT(R), CURRENT_COLOR
                    ENDIF
                ENDIF
            ENDIF
        ELSE
            IF MOUSEB(1) = FALSE THEN MOUSE_DOWN = FALSE
        ENDIF
        
        ' Dummy content to fill ImGui child so it doesn't collapse
        GUI.TEXT "Canvas Area: " + STR$(CANVAS_W) + "x" + STR$(CANVAS_H)
        GUI.TEXT "Mouse Grid: " + STR$(GX) + ", " + STR$(GY)
        
    GUI.END_CHILD
    GUI.POP_STYLE_COLOR 1

    GUI.END
    SCREENFLIP
    
LOOP

' --- End of File ---

    if a > 1 then

    else
        return a
    endif
endfunc

Dim o_map[20]

sub printresult(result)
   for i = 0 to len(result)-1
       if result[i]>0 then
           print result[i], " ";
       endif
   next i
print
endsub

func iseven(a)
   if a mod 2 = 0 then
      r=1
   else
      r=0
   endif
   return r
endfunc

func filter(fu,iin[],out[])
   j=0
   for i = 0 to len(iin) - 1
      m=fu(iin[i])
      if m = 1 then
         out[j]=iin[i]
         j=j+1
      endif
   next i
endfunc

filter(iseven@,s_map,o_map)

print "Result filter: "
printresult o_map

print
print "Done"
